  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoBO方法存在两大局限：要么只自适应一个组件（代理模型或采集函数），导致另一个组件成为瓶颈；要么在共享准则下联合选择代理-采集对，忽略了二者的不同角色——代理模型选择应取决于预测可靠性，而采集函数适应应响应优化进程的上下文环境。

Method: DASH包含三个核心部分：(1) 代理模型选择：基于预测可靠性、不确定性校准和排序一致性来独立选择最优代理模型；(2) 两阶段采集函数控制器：周期性在各采集函数间重新分配配额，据此构建BO候选集，并委托LLM进行最终选择；(3) 集成框架：包括知识引导的冷启动和结构化记忆，将优化过程锚定在领域知识和累积反馈中。

Result: 在四个化学优化任务中，DASH相比最佳AutoBO基线在轨迹级Acceleration Factor上提升12.51%，在端点Enhancement Factor上提升5.00%。结果在不同LLM骨干上表现稳定，消融实验验证了所有组件的互补贡献。全文表和行为污染检查未发现直接基准记忆化或源单元格泄漏的证据。

Conclusion: DASH验证了解耦自适应代理模型与采集函数设计的有效性——分别针对两者的不同角色进行独立优化，结合LLM的决策能力和知识引导框架，能够显著提升AutoBO在化学优化等科学任务中的综合性能，同时排除了记忆污染对结果的解释。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) relies on a surrogate model and an acquisition function, yet the most suitable choices vary across tasks and optimization stages. Automated Bayesian optimization (AutoBO) addresses this variability by adapting BO components online. However, existing AutoBO methods either adapt one component, leaving the other mismatched and creating a bottleneck, or jointly select surrogate--acquisition pairs under a shared criterion, overlooking their distinct roles: surrogate selection depends on predictive reliability, whereas acquisition adaptation should respond to campaign context.In this paper, we propose DASH, a Decoupled Adaptive Surrogate--Acquisition Harness for large-language- model (LLM)-enhanced AutoBO. DASH selects surrogates by predictive reliability, uncertainty calibration, and ranking consistency; its two-stage acquisition controller periodically reallocates quotas across acquisition functions, builds a BO shortlist accordingly, and delegates final selection to an LLM. DASH also incorporates an integrated harness, consisting of knowledge-guided warm start and structured memory, to ground optimization in domain knowledge and accumulated feedback. Across four chemical optimization tasks, DASH outperforms the best AutoBO baseline by 12.51% in trajectory-level Acceleration Factor and 5.00% in endpoint Enhancement Factor. Results remain strong across LLM backbones, and ablations verify the complementary contributions of all components. Full-table and behavioral contamination checks find no detectable evidence that direct benchmark memorization or source-cell leakage explains these gains.

</details>


### [48] [HetGPS: Scalable Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Physics-Anchored Adaptive Safety for EV Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00679)
*Xiangwei Wang,Nanduni Nimalsiri,Yu Xia,Peng Wang,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HetGPS，一种混合图控制框架，将学习到的图风险与物理锚定修正相结合，通过分离干预幅度与修正方向，在保障共享安全约束（如电压）的同时最小化对任务导向策略的干扰。在200-3218辆EV的配电网中，将母线-步电压违规率从3.93-7.74%降至0.52-3.44%，同时保持99%以上的离开成功率，且模型参数规模不随车队规模增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网络耦合智能体的安全干预必须在保护共享约束的同时，避免不必要地覆写任务导向的策略决策。已有的安全过滤器或物理投影方法缺乏对干预权重的自适应能力，难以在安全性和任务性能之间取得平衡。

Method: HetGPS采用混合图控制框架：一个动作条件下的图残差模型负责调度状态依赖的干预权值（干预幅度），而物理模型决定修正方向。与一个参数共享的异构图soft actor-critic（SAC）策略耦合，实现拓扑感知的协调，模型大小与车队规模无关。

Result: 在5个嵌套配电网（200-3218辆EV，100个评估日）上，Adaptive Authority将母线-步电压违规率从无过滤时的3.93-7.74%降至0.52-3.44%，同时维持99.06-100%的离开成功率。相比固定权值的物理投影，在5个网络上均提升平均奖励，4个网络上降低平均安全评分。模型仅含383,702个参数，在3218辆EV场景下比中心化SAC actor小约170倍。8变压器系统训练的策略可零样本迁移至16和32变压器系统，违规率0.57-0.75%，离开成功率≥99.99%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，学习到的图风险能够在规模化场景下分配干预权威，同时由馈线物理模型锚定修正动作，实现了安全性与任务性能的有效平衡。

Abstract: Safety interventions for large populations of network-coupled agents must protect shared constraints without unnecessarily overriding task-oriented policy decisions. We present HetGPS, a hybrid graph-control framework synergizing learned graph risk with physics-anchored correction by separating intervention magnitude from corrective direction. An action-conditioned graph residual model schedules state-dependent intervention authority, while a physics model determines its direction. For electric vehicle (EV) charging, we couple this filter with a parameter-shared heterogeneous graph soft actor-critic policy, enabling topology-aware coordination with a learned model size independent of fleet size. Across five nested distribution networks with 200--3,218 EVs and 100 evaluation days, Adaptive Authority reduces bus--step voltage violations from 3.93--7.74\% without filtering to 0.52--3.44\%, while maintaining 99.06--100\% departure success. Relative to the same physics-directed projection with fixed authority, it improves mean reward on all five networks and lowers the mean safety score on four. The deployed policy-and-risk model contains 383,702 learned parameters at every scale; at 3,218 EVs, a matched centralized SAC actor is about $170\times$ larger. A policy trained on the eight-transformer system transfers zero-shot to the 16- and 32-transformer systems, attaining 0.57--0.75\% violation rates and at least 99.99\% departure success. These results show that learned graph risk can allocate intervention authority at scale while feeder physics anchors corrective action.

</details>


### [49] [Multi-Dimensional Assessment for AI Cognition (MAAC): A Theoretical Framework for Process-Oriented Cognitive Evaluation of Text-Based AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00680)
*Abdalla Doleh,Ratna Babu Chinnam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多维度AI认知评估框架（MAAC），从"产出什么"转向"如何思考"，定义了九个认知维度，并基于认知科学理论（Marr三水平假设、Baddeley工作记忆模型、Sweller认知负荷理论等）进行了五项理论分析来验证框架的一致性、覆盖度和可检验性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI评估依赖基于结果的基准（outcome-based benchmarks），虽然能衡量任务准确性、鲁棒性或公平性，但缺乏对AI系统底层认知过程的诊断性洞察，无法回答AI如何推理、整合记忆、管理复杂度或避免幻觉等关键问题。研究者希望从"产生了什么输出"转向"如何思考"来评估文本AI系统。

Method: 提出了MAAC（Multi-Dimensional Assessment for AI Cognition）框架，定义了九个基于认知科学的维度：认知负荷、工具执行、内容质量、记忆整合、复杂度处理、幻觉控制、知识迁移、处理效率及过程-结果对齐。每个维度均以认知科学理论为基础（Marr三水平假设、Baddeley工作记忆模型、Sweller认知负荷理论及统一认知理论）。通过五项理论分析验证框架：维度-理论映射、覆盖矩阵（衡量广度与非冗余性）、与现有评估实践的正式差距分析、诊断示例以及预测未来实证检验的先验相互依赖预测。

Result: 五项理论分析初步支持了MAAC框架的一致性和经验可检验性，表明该框架能够对文本AI系统进行基于认知理论的过程级评估，并与现有的基于结果的基准形成互补。

Conclusion: MAAC为文本AI系统提供了理论上严谨、操作可行且多维度、认知导向的过程级评估框架，补充了现有基于结果的评估方法。

Abstract: Evaluating artificial intelligence systems has historically relied on outcome-based benchmarks that measure task accuracy, robustness, or fairness. While indispensable, these benchmarks provide limited diagnostic insight into the underlying cognitive processes that generate performance-leaving critical questions unanswered about how AI systems reason, integrate memory, manage complexity, or avoid generating false information. This paper introduces the Multi-Dimensional Assessment for AI Cognition (MAAC), a theoretically grounded framework for shifting evaluation from what text-based AI systems produce to how they think. MAAC defines nine cognitively motivated dimensions: Cognitive Load, Tool Execution, Content Quality, Memory Integration, Complexity Handling, Hallucination Control, Knowledge Transfer, Processing Efficiency, and Process-Outcome Alignment. Each dimension is grounded in established cognitive science theory-drawing on Marr's tri-level hypothesis, Baddeley's working memory model, Sweller's cognitive load theory, and unified theories of cognition. Five theoretical analyses provide initial support for the framework's coherence and empirical testability: dimension-to-theory mapping; a coverage matrix assessing breadth and non-redundancy; a formal gap analysis relative to current evaluation practice; a worked diagnostic illustration; and a set of a priori interdependency predictions for future empirical testing. MAAC provides a theoretical and operational framework for principled process-level cognitive assessment of text-based AI systems, complementing existing outcome-based benchmarks with cognitively grounded, multi-dimensional evaluation.

</details>


### [50] [When Does LLM Orchestration Pay Off? A Controlled Evaluation of Accuracy, Cost, and Task Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00685)
*Nicolas Leins,Nico Pelleriti,Jana Gonnermann-Müller,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对三种LLM编排方法（Self-Refine、Best-of-N、Debate）与单次调用基线进行了受控对比评估，覆盖五个LLM骨干模型和三个领域（竞赛编程、国际象棋谜题、数学）。研究发现编排仅带来中等且依赖基准的收益（相比优化后的CoT最大提升4.6个百分点），却需2-4倍的推理成本；编排效果与骨干模型存在强烈交互作用，但与任务难度无显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编排常被认为能通过增加推理时计算来提升推理能力，但其收益可能不值得额外成本。现有比较往往忽略优化努力的差异，难以隔离编排本身的价值。本文旨在通过控制优化努力进行公平评估，厘清编排方法的真实增益。

Method: 采用受控实验设计：在五个LLM骨干模型和三个领域（竞赛编程、国际象棋谜题、数学）上，对Self-Refine、Best-of-N、Debate与任务专用和链式思维（CoT）单次调用基线进行比较。所有方法均在相同优化预算下使用GEPA进行优化，并在相同的按难度分层基准条目上评估。此外采用探索性混合效应分析考察编排方法与模型、难度之间的交互作用。

Result: 编排带来中等但依赖基准的收益：每个基准内跨骨干模型的平均最大提升为相对于优化CoT推理的4.6个百分点和相对于仅任务推理的4.5个百分点，但所需总token数约为仅任务推理的2至4倍。人类定义的难度在所有三个基准中与更低的绝对准确率相关，但基准内分析未表明编排效果随任务难度增加而增强。混合效应分析显示编排方法与骨干模型在所有三个基准上存在强烈交互作用，表明编排有效性高度依赖于底层模型。

Conclusion: 编排决策应针对具体模型来进行，并需权衡中等准确率提升与额外推理成本之间的关系。更广泛而言，LLM编排评估应控制优化努力并报告模型特定的准确率-成本权衡，而非将额外推理时结构视为普遍有益。

Abstract: LLM orchestration is often assumed to improve reasoning by allocating additional inference-time computation, yet its gains may not justify its cost. Existing comparisons also frequently overlook differences in optimization effort, making it difficult to isolate the value of orchestration itself. We conduct a controlled evaluation of Self-Refine, Best-of-$N$, and Debate against task-only and chain-of-thought (CoT) single-call baselines across five LLM backbones and three domains: competitive programming, chess puzzles, and mathematics. For comparability, we optimize each method with GEPA under the same optimization budget and evaluate all methods on the same difficulty-stratified benchmark items. Orchestration yields moderate but benchmark-dependent gains: averaged across backbones within each benchmark, the largest improvement is 4.6 percentage points over optimized CoT inference and 4.5 points over task-only inference, while requiring approximately 2 to 4 times the mean total tokens of task-only inference. Human-derived difficulty is associated with lower absolute accuracy in all three benchmarks, but within-benchmark analyses do not indicate that orchestration effects increase with task difficulty. By contrast, exploratory mixed-effects analyses reveal strong interactions between orchestration method and backbone model across all three benchmarks, showing that orchestration effectiveness depends substantially on the underlying model. Our results suggest that orchestration decisions should be model-specific and account for whether moderate accuracy gains justify the additional inference cost. More broadly, evaluations of LLM orchestrations should control optimization effort and report model-specific accuracy--cost trade-offs rather than treating additional inference-time structure as uniformly beneficial.

</details>


### [51] [DGA$_2$D: Directed Graph-Guided Automated Algorithm Design with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00700)
*Jiale Zhao,Zimu Chen,Sirui Mao,Wentao Yang,Yuxiang Bai,Liyuanjun Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DGA$_2$D框架，将有向图引导的自动化算法设计应用于LLM驱动的NP-hard组合优化问题求解，通过将程序空间建模为有向图并使用一阶路径依赖的信用分配机制，在12个COP基准上将平均归一化间隙降低多达10.96个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的自动化启发式设计(AHD)方法受限于僵硬的求解器模板，搜索过程被降级为孤立模块调优；而完全自主的系统级算法设计面临三个核心挑战：生成算子可靠性低、搜索空间极大、信用分配低效。

Method: 提出DGA$_2$D（Directed Graph-Guided Automated Algorithm Design）框架：将开放程序空间结构化为有向图，节点代表可被多种候选代码实现实例化的功能算子，有向路径构成完整算法流水线；引入一阶路径依赖的信用分配机制，严格基于拓扑上下文评估代码变体。

Result: 在涵盖复杂调度到路由问题的12个不同COP基准上进行广泛实验，DGA$_2$D相对最先进的LLM基线将平均归一化间隙最多降低10.96个百分点，表现出一致的经验优势。

Conclusion: 通过有向图结构化和路径依赖的信用分配，DGA$_2$D有效克服了现有LLM驱动AHD方法在可靠性、搜索规模和信用分配方面的缺陷，为自动化算法设计提供了更系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of Large Language Models (LLMs) has opened new avenues for Automated Heuristic Design (AHD) for solving NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems (COPs). However, existing LLM-driven AHD methods are largely confined to rigid solver templates, relegating the search process to isolated module tuning. Transitioning to fully autonomous, system-level algorithm design is essential but fraught with low reliability of generated operators, extremely large search spaces, and ineffective credit assignment. To overcome these drawbacks, this paper proposes a Directed Graph-Guided Automated Algorithm Design framework, termed DGA$_2$D. It structures the open-ended program space as a directed graph, where each node represents a functional operator that can be instantiated using one of multiple candidate code implementations, while directed walks constitute complete algorithmic pipelines. A first-order path-dependent credit assignment mechanism is introduced to evaluate code variations strictly based on their topological context. Extensive experiments across 12 distinct COPs, ranging from complex scheduling to routing, demonstrate the consistent empirical advantages of DGA$_2$D. It reduces the average normalized gap by up to 10.96 percentage points compared to state-of-the-art LLM baselines.

</details>


### [52] [Tracing the Cascade: A Topology-Aware Evaluation Framework for Scientific Agent Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00711)
*Xinshun Feng,Ziqi Miao,Lijun Li,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCHEMA是首个证据支撑、拓扑感知的科学智能体幻觉评估框架，自动从基准种子和文献证据构建科学概念图，合成涵盖声明验证、多跳推理、开放解释和实验代码生成的任务，并通过轨迹幻觉流水线和多智能体反事实归因两个诊断模块进行评估。研究发现幻觉集中在少数高连接的知识枢纽上，且最终答案准确性与轨迹诚实性脱钩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉基准停留在表层，将事实孤立处理并使用统一准确率指标，忽略了科学知识内部的稠密拓扑连接结构；而在科学研究中，单一基础概念的错误声明会通过多步推理传播并破坏整个推理轨迹，因此需要拓扑感知的评估框架。

Method: SCHEMA框架包含三个核心组件：(1)从基准种子和文献证据自动构建科学概念图；(2)合成图基任务，涵盖声明验证、多跳推理、开放式解释和实验代码生成四类； (3)双诊断模块评估：轨迹幻觉流水线通过拓扑加权严重度分数审计中间推理，多智能体反事实归因模块定位选定失败的因果机制。

Result: SCHEMA发现幻觉集中在少数高连接的知识枢纽节点上，且最终答案准确率与轨迹诚实性脱钩——模型常常通过结构上有缺陷的推理得出正确结论。

Conclusion: 对高风险科学应用而言，终端准确率单独不足以作为智能体可靠性的信号，需要基于知识拓扑的机制级评估；SCHEMA代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in scientific research, where reliability is critical and the underlying knowledge is densely interconnected. In such settings, hallucinations are particularly damaging: a single erroneous claim on a foundational concept can propagate through multi-step reasoning and corrupt entire trajectories. Existing hallucination benchmarks largely operate at the surface level, treating facts in isolation and relying on uniform accuracy metrics that ignore this topological structure. We address this gap with SCHEMA, the first evidence-grounded, topology-aware evaluation framework for hallucinations in scientific agents. SCHEMA automatically constructs scientific concept graphs from benchmark seeds and literature evidence, synthesizes graph-grounded tasks spanning claim verification, multi-hop reasoning, open-ended explanation, and experimental code generation, and evaluates agents with two complementary diagnostics. A trajectory hallucination pipeline audits intermediate reasoning at scale via a topology-weighted severity score, while a multi-agent counterfactual attribution module pinpoints the causal mechanism behind selected failures. SCHEMA reveals that hallucinations concentrate at a small set of highly connected knowledge hubs, and that final-answer accuracy decouples from trajectory honesty; models often reach correct conclusions through structurally flawed reasoning. These results indicate that for high-stakes scientific applications, terminal accuracy alone is an insufficient signal of agent reliability, motivating mechanism-level evaluation grounded in knowledge topology. Code is available at https://github.com/circles-post/SCHEMA.

</details>


### [53] [AI-Based Thesis Assessment: An Empirical Study of Human Evaluation Priorities and Their Impact on Automated Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00717)
*Garv Vikram Gursahaney,Baskhad Idrisov,Thorsten Fröhlich,Tim Schlippe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究调查了84名论文导师对35个论文评估标准的权重分配，并与AI评估系统RubiSCoT的默认权重进行比较。将导师权重集成到多种校准配置中后，最佳配置仅将AI与导师评估的平均相对偏差从11.18%降至10.85%，且改进不具统计显著性。导师之间的一致性（平均相对偏差4.44%）远高于AI与导师之间的一致性，表明仅靠标准权重校准无法显著提升AI评估与人类评估的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Rubric的AI论文评估系统使用标准权重来分配不同评估标准的重要性，但这些权重通常由专家主观设定，缺乏关于论文导师实际如何优先排序评估标准的实证依据。因此有必要调查导师的实际权重偏好并检验其对AI评估系统的影响。

Method: 对四个学科领域的84名论文导师进行问卷调查，收集35个论文评估标准的权重数据。将导师来源的权重与AI评估系统RubiSCoT的默认权重进行比较，并将导师权重集成到多种校准配置中，在80篇德语论文语料库上评估性能。

Result: 导师权重与系统默认权重存在显著差异。最佳校准配置将AI与导师评估间的平均相对偏差从11.18%降至10.85%，但改进未达到统计显著性。人类导师之间的平均相对偏差仅为4.44%，远低于AI与导师之间的偏差。

Conclusion: 仅调整评估标准权重并不能实质性改善AI评估与人类评估之间的对齐程度。AI与人类评估之间的差距可能源于更深层的因素，而非单纯的标准权重设置问题。

Abstract: Rubric-based AI systems for thesis assessment use criterion weights to assign different levels of importance to evaluation criteria. These weights are typically defined through expert judgment, although little empirical evidence exists regarding how thesis supervisors actually prioritize evaluation criteria. Consequently, this study investigates supervisor-derived criterion weights in thesis assessment and evaluates their impact on AI-based assessment. We surveyed 84 thesis supervisors across four academic disciplines and collected weighting data for 35 thesis assessment criteria. Comparison with the default criterion weights of the AI assessment system RubiSCoT [1] revealed substantial divergences between supervisor-derived and default criterion weights. To evaluate the practical implications of these differences, the supervisor-derived weights were integrated into multiple calibration configurations and evaluated on a corpus of 80 German-language theses. The best-performing configuration reduced the mean relative deviation between AI-generated and supervisor-assigned evaluations from 11.18% to 10.85%, although the improvement was not statistically significant. Human supervisors showed substantially stronger agreement with each other, exhibiting a mean inter-supervisor relative deviation of 4.44%. The findings indicate that criterion-weight calibration alone does not substantially improve alignment between AI-generated and human assessments.

</details>


### [54] [Behavioral Grammar: Detecting Adaptive Malware via Tiny Language Model Priors and Second-Order Temporal Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00745)
*Zihan Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Behavioral Grammar检测架构，将主机运行时行为离散化为结构化语言，用0.88M参数的TinyGPT因果Transformer学习正常行为语法，通过逐槽位NLL统计计算异常分数，实现对自适应对抗代理（AAA）93%的检测率（3.84%误报率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端点检测存在根本性矛盾：基于签名的方案易被多态/自适应威胁绕过，而重型深度学习模型难以审计且无法大规模部署。需要在检测能力与可部署性之间找到平衡。

Method: 将系统事件离散化为8-token表示（事件类型、进程、参数骨架、路径类别、父进程、用户、目的地、事件间时序），用0.88M参数的TinyGPT因果Transformer以自监督方式学习正常行为条件分布，异常分数基于逐槽位负对数似然（NLL）统计，实现数学上有界的误报率。此外融合原型学习（已知攻击归因）、二阶时间分析（节奏检测）、自学习模式提取和五网络融合流水线。

Result: 对自适应对抗代理（AAA）达到93%检测率，3.84%上板误报率。最强判别信号来自事件间间隔的变异系数（CV）：AAA步进节奏CV=0.310对比良性睡眠间隔的9.786，约30倍分离。

Conclusion: 行为语法检测将逃避成本从规则规避（廉价）转移到分布匹配（昂贵），建立结构性不对称优势偏向防御方，并可用协同演化经济学模型解释。

Abstract: Modern endpoint detection systems face a fundamental tension: signature-based approaches are trivially evaded by polymorphic or adaptive threats, while heavy deep-learning models resist auditability and deployment at scale. This paper presents Behavioral Grammar, a detection architecture that treats host runtime behavior as a structured language and learns its grammar with a compact 0.88M-parameter causal Transformer (TinyGPT). Each system event is discretized into an 8-token representation spanning event type, process, argument skeleton, path category, parent process, user, destination, and inter-event timing. The model learns the conditional distribution of normal behavior in a purely self-supervised manner, and anomaly scores are derived from per-slot negative log-likelihood (NLL) statistics, yielding a mathematically bounded false-positive rate. We augment this prior with prototype learning for known-attack attribution, second-order temporal analysis for cadence-based detection, self-learning pattern extraction, and a five-network fusion pipeline. Against an Adaptive Adversarial Agent (AAA)--a threat that learns survival strategies under defensive pressure, performs behavioral mimicry, and matches host event rates--our system achieves 93% detection at 3.84% onboarding false-positive rate. The strongest discriminative signal arises not from any single event but from the coefficient of variation of inter-event intervals: the AAA stepping cadence exhibits CV=0.310 versus 9.786 for benign sleep intervals, a 30x separation reflecting a fundamental stealth-functionality trade-off. We frame these findings within a coevolutionary economics model, arguing that behavior-grammar detection shifts the evasion cost from rule circumvention (cheap) to distribution matching (expensive), establishing a structural asymmetry favoring the defender.

</details>


### [55] [FinDeepIndicator: Benchmarking Deep Research Agents in End-to-End Financial Indicator Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00764)
*Chaoqun Yang,Fengbin Zhu,Xinyu Lin,Long Bai,Xiaoluan Liu,Ke-Wei Huang,Roger Zimmermann,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinDeepIndicator，这是首个专门用于评估深度研究（DR）智能体端到端金融指标构建能力的基准。该基准覆盖指标构建的四个阶段（公式规范、数据收集、指标计算、答案生成），包含21个细分子类别、3,350个QA对、10年历史金融数据和800家上市公司数据。实验发现LLM在公式规范阶段表现良好，但在数据检索和数值执行阶段准确率显著下降；DR智能体虽优于基于搜索的LLM，但在真实金融分析场景中仍不够可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准大多只关注答案层面的准确性，并假设相关数据已预先提供，忽略了对指标构建这一中间过程的评估。金融指标是将原始金融数据转化为可解释度量的关键工具，但其构建过程（特别是中间环节）的评测在学术界仍属空白，亟需一个系统化的基准来填补这一缺口。

Method: 构建FinDeepIndicator基准，从四个维度评估DR智能体在金融指标构建中的表现：（1）四个构建阶段——公式规范、数据收集、指标计算和答案生成；（2）覆盖基本面、技术面和宏观经济三类指标，细分为21个子类别；（3）数据来源涵盖美国和中国市场，包含3,350个精选QA对、10年历史数据和800家上市公司。实验中对比了基于搜索的LLM与DR智能体在各项任务上的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）LLM在公式规范阶段表现普遍良好，但在数据检索和数值执行阶段准确率大幅下降；（2）DR智能体在各项指标上均优于基于搜索的LLM；（3）即使是最先进的DR智能体，在真实金融分析场景中仍不够可靠，存在明显的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: FinDeepIndicator揭示了金融指标构建中现有的AI系统在数据检索和数值执行环节的薄弱之处，为开发更强大、更可信的金融领域DR智能体提供了关键洞见和评测标准。

Abstract: Financial indicators are essential tools for transforming raw financial data into interpretable measures for various downstream tasks, such as valuation, risk assessment, and economic analysis. However, existing financial benchmarks largely focus on answer-level accuracy and often assume that relevant data are already provided, leaving the assessment of the intermediate process of indicator construction underexplored. In this work, we propose FinDeepIndicator, the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating Deep Research (DR) agents in end-to-end financial indicator construction. Specifically, FinDeepIndicator evaluates DR agents across four stages in indicator construction: formula specification, data collection, indicator calculation, and answer generation, and covers fundamental, technical, and macroeconomic indicators organized into 21 fine-grained sub-categories. It contains 3,350 curated question-answer (QA) pairs derived from both U.S. and Chinese markets, 10 years of historical financial data, and 800 listed companies. Extensive experiments on search-equipped Large Language Models (LLMs) and DR agents show that, while LLMs generally perform well in formula specification, their accuracy drops substantially during data retrieval and numerical execution. DR agents consistently outperform search-equipped LLMs, yet remain unreliable in realistic financial analysis settings. These findings provide insights for developing more capable and trustworthy DR agents in finance.

</details>


### [56] [Measurement Without Validity: The Compounding Reliability Problem in Agentic AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00794)
*William Caban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证分析揭示了基于Agentic AI的自动化基准评估分数在三个叠加层面（任务生成、用户模拟、人机一致性判断）上存在系统性可信度缺陷，并提出了基于心理测量学的八项改进处方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统的部署决策、安全认证和监管合规声明都依赖于自动化基准的评估分数，但现有实践对这些分数的可信度缺乏充分的检验。作者旨在证明这些分数"系统性不如当前实践所承认的那样可信"，并推动领域的测量标准改进。

Method: 研究方法包括三个方面：(1) 对十个流行基准进行审计，发现任务生成中的有效性缺陷和报告遗漏；(2) 通过校准研究量化LLM模拟器之间的差异（最高达9个百分点）及非标准美式英语使用者的系统性方向性误校准；(3) 对55篇论文进行结构化调查，发现约82%的论文使用了结构不匹配、不完整或缺失的评估者间信度（IRR）指标。此外，作者通过乘法复合模型（V_total ≤ V1 × V2 × V3）对三层失败进行数学形式化。

Result: 研究发现三层失败以乘法而非加法方式复合：在任务生成保留70%、模拟保留80%、判断保留65%有效信号的情况下，管道整体有效性最多仅为36%（经验估计范围0.22-0.54）。此外，当同一模型家族跨越三层运行时，相关失败会使该界限进一步收紧。

Conclusion: 作者从心理测量科学中推导出八项处方，包括：模拟校准下限ICC(A,1)≥0.70、按后果级别分层的信度阈值（α≥0.67/0.70/0.80）、基于管道设计的结构化IRR指标选择规则、以及将IRR设为强制报告字段。作者强调测量工具已经存在，关键在于领域共同应用它们。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are evaluated using automated benchmarks whose scores justify deployment decisions, safety certifications, and regulatory compliance claims. We present an empirical analysis demonstrating that these scores are systematically less trustworthy than current practice acknowledges.
  The problem operates at three compounding layers. First, tasks are increasingly generated by language models: audits of ten popular benchmarks found validity flaws in seven and reporting gaps in all ten. Second, human users are replaced by LLM simulators, but calibration studies document inter-simulator variance up to 9 percentage points and systematic directional miscalibration, particularly for non-Standard American English speakers. Third, our structured survey of 55 papers finds that approximately 82% apply structurally mismatched, incomplete, or absent inter-rater reliability (IRR) metrics.
  These failures compound multiplicatively rather than additively. Under independence, a pipeline retaining 70% of valid signal at task generation, 80% at simulation, and 65% at judgment is at most 36% valid against the intended construct; the bound spans 0.22--0.54 across the empirical estimate range. We formalize this as $V_{\text{total}} \leq V_1 \times V_2 \times V_3$ and show it tightens further under correlated failures when the same model family operates across all three layers.
  We derive eight prescriptions grounded in psychometric science: a simulation calibration floor of $\text{ICC}(A,1) \geq 0.70$; domain-stratified reliability thresholds ($α\geq 0.67$ / $0.70$ / $0.80$ by consequence level); structured IRR metric selection rules based on pipeline design; and IRR as a mandatory reporting field. The measurement tools exist; the field's task is to apply them.

</details>


### [57] [AgentSLABench: Evaluating and Benchmarking Agentic Systems Under Resource Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00805)
*Meher Bhaskar Madiraju,Meher Sai Preetam Madiraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentSLABench 是一个面向自主AI智能体的资源感知评估框架，在声明的资源预算（CPU/内存/时间/网络）下同时衡量正确性与延迟、成本、计算、内存和网络使用情况，提出Efficiency-Adjusted Success Rate (EASR)指标，并对9个智能体（5个通用基线+4个任务特化）在16个任务环境上进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（benchmark）仅报告正确率，忽视了AI智能体在实际生产中运行时的资源消耗问题。高准确率如果以无限成本为代价则不具备生产可行性，因此需要一个像系统性能剖析器（perf、pprof等）一样但将任务正确性作为一等维度的资源感知评估框架。

Method: 1) 构建16个任务环境，覆盖6个类别（5个核心任务：多跳QA、零售替换、代码生成、网页购物、旅行规划；11个扩展任务）；2) 使用隔离的Docker容器并提供声明的CPU/内存/时间/网络预算；3) 使用SHA256哈希封装密封测试集并制定标准化剖析协议；4) 提出EASR（Efficiency-Adjusted Success Rate）指标，即成功率按资源消耗相对于声明预算进行加权；5) 对5个通用基线智能体（ReAct、PlanAndSolve、Reflexion、CoT、Random）和4个任务特化智能体进行系统评测。

Result: 任务特化智能体在3/5核心任务（fact_qa、web_shopping、travel_planning）上取得100%成功率，在零售替换和代码生成上达到66.7%-83.3%；而通用基线在4/5领域任务上完全失败。EASR指标揭示了高准确率在无界资源成本下并非生产可行的关键洞察。

Conclusion: AgentSLABench将系统级资源剖析理念引入AI智能体评估领域，提供可复现、资源感知的评测基础设施（含完整代码、密封测试集和剖析结果），EASR为智能体生产部署提供了更实际的决策依据。

Abstract: We present AgentSLABench, a resource-aware evaluation framework for autonomous AI agents that measures correctness alongside latency, cost, compute, memory, and network usage under declared resource budgets. Unlike standard benchmarks that report only accuracy, AgentSLABench produces a multi-dimensional profile per agent per task - the same way systems profilers (perf, pprof, cProfile) measure resource consumption of code, but extended with task correctness as a first-class dimension. AgentSLABench provides 16 task environments across 6 categories (5 core: multi-hop QA, retail substitution, code generation, web shopping, travel planning; 11 extended) with isolated Docker containers, declared CPU/memory/time/network budgets, sealed test sets with SHA256 hashes, and a standardized profiling protocol. We profile 5 general-purpose baseline agents (ReAct, PlanAndSolve, Reflexion, CoT, Random) plus 4 task-specialized agents, finding that specialized agents achieve 100% success on 3/5 core tasks (fact_qa, web_shopping, travel_planning) and 66.7-83.3% on retail and code_gen, while general baselines fail entirely on 4/5 domain tasks. Crucially, we report the Efficiency-Adjusted Success Rate (EASR) - success weighted by resource consumption relative to declared budgets - revealing that high accuracy at unbounded cost is not production-viable. We release the full infrastructure, sealed test sets, and profiling results to enable reproducible, resource-aware agent evaluation.

</details>


### [58] [Large language models improve physician accuracy but lead to false reliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00817)
*Tirtha Chanda,Christoph Wies,Franziska Schramm,Carina Nogueira Garcia,Nicolas B. Merl,Martin J. Hetz,Jochen S. Utikal,Phillip Tschandl,Cristian Navarrete-Dechent,Alexander Thiem,Jakob N. Kather,Consortium,Titus J. Brinker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了名为CORA的检索增强型智能体大语言模型，探讨来源链接的AI辅助如何影响医生决策。在46名医生的研究中，CORA将诊断准确率从70.8%提升至82.6%，但发现引用证据在提升正确建议采纳率的同时，也显著削弱了医生对错误LLM建议的抵制能力，揭示了一种基于证据的潜在安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强型大语言模型有望为临床决策提供带来源链接的支持，但其价值取决于所展示的证据是引导而非误导医生的判断。现有研究缺乏对来源链接如何影响医生对AI建议的依赖和抵抗行为的系统性分析。

Method: 开发了CORA（agentic检索增强LLM系统），在一项包含46名医生的临床研究中，比较医生无辅助与使用CORA辅助时的诊断准确率；同时分析支持性引文对医生采纳正确建议和抵抗错误建议行为的影响。

Result: CORA保持基准性能，并在模型训练数据截止日期之后发表的病例中表现更佳。使用CORA后医生准确率从70.8%升至82.6%。支持性引文能预测正确回答（87.7% vs 65.5%），但产生重要不对称性：引文支持使正确建议采纳率从34%升至76.9%，但错误LLM答案有引文支持时，医生对其的抵抗率从92%骤降至34.8%。

Conclusion: 来源链接的LLM辅助能够提升医生诊断准确性，但引入了依赖证据支持的安全风险——医生可能因引文的存在而对错误建议产生过度信任，需要在系统设计中加以防范。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) promise source-linked clinical support, but their value depends on whether displayed evidence guides rather than distorts physician reliance. We developed CORA, an agentic retrieval-augmented LLM, to investigate how source-linked assistance affects physician decision-making. CORA maintained benchmark performance and achieved larger gains on cases published after the models' training-data cutoffs. In a study of 46 physicians, accuracy increased from 70.8% unaided to 82.6% with CORA. Supporting citations predicted correct answers (87.7% vs 65.5%), but citations created an important asymmetry: perceived support increased adoption of correct advice from 34% to 76.9% but when an incorrect LLM answer appeared citation-supported, physician resistance to it fell from 92% to 34.8%. These findings show that source-linked LLM assistance can improve physician accuracy while introducing a grounding-dependent safety risk.

</details>


### [59] [The Scaling Paradox in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00818)
*Anyan Qi,Mengxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个分析模型，研究AI规模扩展对人机协作系统整体性能的影响，发现人类对AI能力的准确感知是AI规模收益转化为协作绩效提升的关键条件；人类过度感知AI能力会产生"规模悖论"（AI规模越大整体性能反而下降），而低估AI能力则导致性能提升速度显著放缓。企业可通过成本内化和感知对齐等运营策略来管理这些偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规模定律揭示了AI系统能力随规模扩大而可预测提升的惊人模式，但在真实世界中AI很少孤立运行，而是常与人类协同工作。因此需要探究AI规模的收益是否能在人机协作场景中持续存在。

Method: 构建了一个分析性（解析）模型，考察AI规模扩展效益何时能转化为提升的人机联合系统性能。模型考虑了人类对AI能力的感知偏差（过度感知和低估）以及企业的运营策略（成本内部化、感知对齐）对协作绩效的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1）当人类准确感知AI能力时，人机系统性能随AI规模正向扩展；2）人类过度感知AI能力时出现"规模悖论"，更大AI规模反而降低系统整体性能和放大企业利润损失；3）人类低估AI能力时性能仍随规模提升但速度显著放缓；4）成本内部化和感知对齐等运营策略能有效管理这些偏差，其效果取决于AI部署的经济性和人类误判的方向。

Conclusion: 组织从管理人机界面中获得的收益可能超过单纯投资更大、更贵的AI系统。AI规模扩展不应仅被视为技术挑战，更应被视为行为与运营层面的挑战，AI规模能否创造价值最终取决于AI能力增强如何塑造人类信念与协作努力。

Abstract: The discovery of scaling laws has highlighted the extraordinary potential of AI systems with a striking empirical pattern: as AI systems scale, their capabilities tend to improve predictably. Yet, in real-world applications, AI rarely operates in isolation; instead, it often works alongside humans, raising the question of whether these gains persist in human-AI collaboration. In this work, we develop an analytical model to examine when the empirical scaling benefits of AI translate into improved human-AI joint system performance. We demonstrate that the performance of a human-AI system can scale positively as the AI scales up-provided that humans have an accurate perception of the AI's capabilities. Human misperception, however, can fundamentally alter this relationship: i) when humans over-perceive the AI's capabilities, a scaling paradox may arise, in which greater AI scale reduces overall system performance and amplifies firm-level profit losses, and (ii) when humans under-perceive the AI's capabilities, performance still improves with scale but at a substantially slower rate. We further show that firms can actively manage these distortions through operational policies such as cost internalization and perception alignment, whose effectiveness depends on the economics of AI deployment and the direction of human misperception. These findings suggest that organizations may benefit more from managing the human-AI interface than from simply investing in larger, more expensive AI systems. More broadly, our results suggest that AI scaling should be viewed not only as a technological challenge, but also as a behavioral and operational one, and caution against the view that larger AI systems will automatically lead to better operational outcomes. Whether AI scaling creates value ultimately depends on how increased AI capabilities shape human beliefs and collaborative efforts.

</details>


### [60] [Isotropy Cliffs: The Geometric Signature of Decision-Making in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00828)
*Okan S. Coskun,Florian Rottach,Carsten Eickhoff,William Rudman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从各向同性（isotropy）的角度研究多选题问答（MCQA）中的决策几何。通过分析五个开放权重模型在多种数据集上的表现，识别出决策关键过渡层，其特征是各向同性发生转变，同时伴随重大表征变化和任务相关聚类的出现。该同步几何行为与下游准确率强相关（r≈0.84），且对提示变化具有鲁棒性，表明其反映了模型行为的一般机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络在MCQA任务中如何做出决策是一个关键问题。现有研究较少从几何角度（特别是各向同性）系统性地分析决策过程，作者希望通过识别模型内部的"决策过渡层"来揭示模型决策的底层机制。

Method: 对五个开放权重语言模型在多个MCQA数据集上的内部表征进行各向同性分析，定位各层表征的各向同性变化点，并将其与表征变化、聚类涌现以及下游准确率进行关联分析。同时通过提示变体测试来验证该过渡现象的鲁棒性。

Result: 发现决策关键过渡层存在各向同性的显著转变，该转变与重大表征变化及任务相关聚类的涌现同步发生；该几何行为与下游准确率的相关系数约为0.84；过渡现象对提示变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 模型在MCQA中存在一个通用的"决策过渡层"机制，该机制在几何上表现为各向同性的转变，并且是模型成功决策的关键指标。这一发现为理解大规模语言模型内部决策过程提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: We investigate the geometry of decision-making in Multiple Choice Question Answering (MCQA) through the lens of isotropy. Analyzing five open-weight models across diverse datasets, we identify decision-critical transition layers characterized by a shift in isotropy, coinciding with a major representational change and the emergence of task-relevant clusters. We demonstrate that this synchronized geometric behavior is strongly correlated with downstream accuracy ($r\approx0.84$), displaying its relevance for successful decision-making. Furthermore, we show that this transition is robust to prompt variations, suggesting that it reflects a general mechanism of model behavior.

</details>


### [61] [Similarity Weighted Aggregation with Global Differential Privacy for Federated Brain Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00872)
*Muhammad Irfan Khan,Eero Lehtonen,Joni Obradovic,Elina Kontio,Esa Alhoniemi,Suleiman A. Khan,Mojtaba Jafaritadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-SimAgg，一个将相似性加权聚合与服务器端差分隐私机制相结合的联邦学习框架，用于医学影像（脑肿瘤分割）的隐私保护协作学习，在FeTS 2022数据集上验证了其能在提供隐私保障的同时保持有竞争力的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习适用于医学影像的跨机构协作训练，但面临两个关键挑战：各机构间数据分布异质（non-IID）导致模型性能下降，以及通过模型更新可能泄露敏感信息。现有的联邦学习方法未能同时有效解决这两大问题。

Method: DP-SimAgg框架包含三个核心组件：(1) L2剪裁（L2 clipping）约束协作方的更新范数；(2) 基于相似性的聚合权重计算，用以缓解non-IID数据分布的影响；(3) 在中央服务器端注入经校准的高斯噪声，在假定的敏感度界下提供逐轮（per-round）差分隐私保证。该框架基于Intel的OpenFL平台实现。

Result: 在FeTS 2022数据集（1251例多模态MRI脑肿瘤分割扫描）上进行了评估。在严格隐私预算下（每轮epsilon=1，20轮累计epsilon_total=20），ET、TC、WT区域的Dice分数分别为0.6357、0.5305和0.5274。在宽松预算下（每轮epsilon=10，累计epsilon_total=200），性能接近非隐私基线，同时通过每轮(epsilon, delta)-DP机制提供隐私保护。

Conclusion: DP-SimAgg在隐私保护与分割性能之间取得了良好平衡，证明了其作为医学影像领域隐私保护联邦学习方案的有效性和潜力。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training of machine learning models across multiple institutions without sharing sensitive data, making it particularly suitable for medical imaging applications. However, heterogeneous data distributions across institutions and potential information leakage through model updates remain important challenges. In this work, we propose DP-SimAgg, a privacy-preserving federated learning framework that integrates similarity-weighted aggregation with a server-side differential privacy mechanism. The proposed method applies L2 clipping to bound collaborator updates, computes similarity-based aggregation weights to mitigate the effects of non-IID data distributions, and injects calibrated Gaussian noise at the central server, providing per-round privacy guarantees under the assumed sensitivity bound. The framework is implemented using Intel's OpenFL platform and evaluated on the FeTS 2022 dataset consisting of 1251 multi-modal MRI scans for brain tumor segmentation. Experimental results demonstrate that DP-SimAgg maintains competitive segmentation performance while providing privacy protection. Under a strict per-round privacy budget (epsilon = 1, cumulative epsilon_total = 20 over 20 rounds), the method achieves Dice scores of 0.6357, 0.5305, and 0.5274 for the enhancing tumor (ET), tumor core (TC), and whole tumor (WT) regions, respectively. With a more relaxed per-round budget (epsilon = 10, cumulative epsilon_total = 200), performance approaches that of the non-private baseline while incorporating a central Gaussian mechanism with per-round (epsilon, delta)-DP accounting under the assumed sensitivity bound. These results highlight the potential of DP-SimAgg for enabling privacy-preserving collaborative learning in medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [62] [Assuming You Knew: Fixing an Epistemic Semantics for Flow Policies Using Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00882)
*David A. Naumann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对CSF 2018论文中关于信息流策略的认知语义框架的缺陷进行了修正，并借助智能体AI编程助手的帮助，在Rocq证明助手中完成了机器验证的修正形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高层安全需求通常涉及程序中允许的信息流动，但涉及选择性降级时难以精确表达。认知逻辑的语义方法虽有前景，但缺乏稳健的通用框架。CSF 2018的论文尝试提供统一框架，但其形式化存在缺陷，且会议期间公布了更正公告，因此需要修正和完善。

Method: 借助智能体AI编程助手，对原CSF 2018论文中关于关系注解的认知语义进行修正，并在Rocq证明助手中完成机器检查的形式化验证。

Result: 修正后的形式化已在Rocq证明助手中通过机器检查，形成了一个简洁且通用的框架。

Conclusion: 该框架的简洁性和通用性有助于比较不同的策略规范风格，并可利用现有技术加以实施。

Abstract: Many high-level security requirements are about the allowed flow of information in programs and are difficult to make precise because they involve selective downgrading. Notions from epistemic logic have emerged as a good approach to policy semantics but a robust general framework remains elusive. A paper appearing in CSF 2018, entitled ``Assuming You Know: Epistemic Semantics of Relational Annotations for Expressive Flow Policies'', attempted to provide a unifying framework---but the formalization was sketchy and a correction was announced during the conference presentation. With aid from an agentic AI coding assistant, a corrected formalization has been machine checked in the Rocq proof assistant. The simplicity and generality of the framework may help compare different policy specification styles and enforce them by leveraging existing techniques.

</details>


### [63] [Neuro-Evolved Heuristics for Variable Gapped Common Subsequence Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00888)
*Marko Djukanović,Christian Blum,Aleksandar Kartelj,Saso Dzeroski,Ziga Zebec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的神经进化框架，用于自动设计解决变间隙最长公共子序列问题（VGLCSP）的数据驱动启发式算法。神经网络引导迭代多源束搜索过程，并通过遗传算法优化权重，还引入将学习启发式与手工启发式相结合的集成方法，在合成和真实世界实例上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGLCSP的多序列版本通常使用基于束搜索的启发式方法求解，但手工设计的启发式往往缺乏稳健性，难以适应不同数据分布和问题实例。

Method: 采用神经进化框架：用预定义架构的神经网络表示启发式，通过遗传算法优化权重；学习过程在权重优化与评估之间交替进行，并集成到迭代多源束搜索框架中；进一步提出结合学习启发式与最佳手工启发式分数的集成方法。

Result: 在合成基准实例和带数据驱动间隙约束的新引入真实世界实例上，混合方法均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 神经进化的学习方法能够自动化设计更有效的数据驱动启发式，克服手工启发式设计的不稳健性问题，为VGLCSP提供更优的求解方案。

Abstract: This study addresses the Variable Gapped Longest Common Subsequence Problem (VGLCSP), a variant of the classical longest common subsequence problem with additional gap constraints and applications in sequence alignment and time-series analysis. While the two-sequence version has been widely studied using dynamic programming, the generalized multi-sequence form is usually solved with beam search-based heuristics, whose hand-crafted designs often lack robustness.
  To overcome this limitation, we propose a learning-based approach for automatically designing more effective data-driven heuristics. The heuristics are represented by a neural network with predefined architecture, whose weights are optimized by a genetic algorithm within a neuro-evolutionary framework. The learning process alternates between weight optimization and evaluation within an iterative multi-source beam search procedure, a state-of-the-art method for the problem. Rather than constructing solutions directly, the neural network learns to guide the search process, producing a neuro-evolved heuristic. We further introduce an ensemble heuristic that combines the scores of learned and the best-performing hand-crafted heuristic. Integrated into the iterative multi-source beam search framework, the resulting hybrid approach outperforms existing methods on both synthetic benchmark instances and newly introduced real-world instances with data-driven gap constraints.

</details>


### [64] [CADIR: A Cross-Backend Editable Intermediate Representation for Agentic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00891)
*Yu Liu,Jingzhe Ni,Yiming Chen,Junqi Huang,Ruofeng Tong,Min Tang,Peng Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CADIR，一种以智能体为中心的CAD可执行中间表示，基于OCCT几何内核构建，通过构造图记录建模操作、参数依赖、约束和拓扑选择，并引入几何签名匹配实现跨CAD后端的可编辑特征历史重建，同时支持基于构造图检索的文本/图像查询以辅助模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将CAD建模过程表示为后端特定的顺序脚本（隐式依赖）或静态几何，难以同时保持构建历史、稳定的拓扑参考和跨CAD系统的特征级可编辑性。

Method: 1) 基于OCCT几何内核（通过OCP）构建CADIR，提供显式组合建模操作和细粒度执行诊断；2) 在程序执行过程中将建模操作、参数依赖、约束和拓扑选择记录为构造图；3) 提出几何签名匹配方法，在参数变化和后端差异下识别对应边和面，使适配器能在FreeCAD、SolidWorks和Fusion 360中重建原生可编辑特征历史；4) 提出构造图检索方法，支持全文图和子图检索，供智能体充分利用完整模型和建模子结构。

Result: 实验表明：CADIR在几何保真度和执行可靠性上优于现有CAD表示；构造图检索进一步提升模型生成质量；跨后端编辑在多种CAD环境中实现了可靠的模型重建和编辑后重建。

Conclusion: CADIR同时实现了构建历史保留、稳定拓扑引用和跨系统特征级可编辑性，为CAD智能体提供了统一的中间表示，并通过构造图检索和几何签名匹配支撑高质量模型生成与跨平台编辑。

Abstract: Large language models have made it possible to generate executable computer-aided design (CAD) programs from natural-language descriptions or images. However, existing methods represent modeling processes as backend-specific sequential scripts with implicit dependencies or as static geometry, making it difficult to simultaneously preserve construction history, stable topological references, and feature-level editability across different CAD systems. We present CADIR, an agent-friendly executable intermediate representation for CAD generation and cross-backend editing. Built on the OCCT geometry kernel via OCP, CADIR provides explicit, compositional modeling operations and fine-grained execution diagnostics. During program execution, CADIR records modeling operations, parameter dependencies, constraints, and topology selections in a construction graph. To enable reliable cross-backend reconstruction, we introduce Geometric Signature Matching, which identifies corresponding edges and faces despite parameter changes and backend differences, allowing adapters to reconstruct native editable feature histories in FreeCAD, SolidWorks, and Fusion 360. Building on this representation, we further propose a construction-graph retrieval method for text and image queries that supports both full-graph and subgraph retrieval, enabling agents to leverage complete models and modeling substructures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CADIR achieves higher geometric fidelity and execution reliability than existing CAD representations, that construction-graph retrieval further improves model generation quality, and that cross-backend editing enables reliable model reconstruction and post-reconstruction editing across multiple CAD environments.

</details>


### [65] [Modeling Social Dynamics with an LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic (LAND) Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00929)
*Lynnette Hui Xian Ng,Kathleen M. Carley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用基于GhostField架构（一种混合LLM驱动的智能体与网络动态模型）构建的AuraSight社交模拟场景，在30天内模拟了314,244个异构网络社交智能体与人类参与者之间529,327条消息的交互，并通过四层分析（自我中心网络拓扑、语义网络演化、协调动态和影响动态）验证了生成式社交模拟能够产生真实的社交动态，且协调与影响动态是从网络拓扑与叙事交换的递归交互中涌现的，而非仅来自个体智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成式社交模拟是否能真实再现社交动态，并探究协调与影响等集体行为是如何从个体层面的交互中涌现出来的，以弥补传统社交模拟中缺乏复杂社会动力学建模的不足。

Method: 采用GhostField架构（混合LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic模型，简称LAND）构建AuraSight场景，模拟314,244个异构智能体与人类参与者围绕虚构国际歌曲创作大赛展开社交互动，从四个分析层面对社交动态进行分析：自我中心网络拓扑、语义网络演化、协调动态与影响动态。

Result: 结果表明生成式社交模拟确实能够产生社交动态；协调与影响的动态并非源于个体智能体的独立行为，而是源于网络拓扑与叙事交换之间的递归交互作用。

Conclusion: 生成式社交模拟能有效复现复杂社会动态，集体层面涌现的协调与影响力本质上来源于网络结构与叙事信息交换的递归耦合，而非个体智能体属性的简单叠加。

Abstract: Social dynamics encode the process in which individual network and discourse interactions aggregate into collective influence, narrative dominance and coordinate behavior. This paper uses the the GhostField architecture, a hybrid LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic (LAND) model as a social simulation framework to build the AuraSight scenario. In the AuraSight scenario, 314,244 heterogeneous cyber social agents and human actors exchange 529,327 messages over 30 days surrounding a fictional international song-writing contest. We methodologically examine emergent social dynamics across four analytical layers: ego-network topology, semantic network evolution, coordination dynamics and influence dynamics. Our results show how generated social simulations do also produce social dynamics, and how the dynamics of coordination and influence emerge not from individual agents but from the recursive interaction between network topology and narrative exchange.

</details>


### [66] [PMMC: Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation for Long-Term LVLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00962)
*Jingyu Sun,Yan Lin,Yuyang Xue,Yifan Wang,Zhengtao Yao,Rui Qian,Zefeng Xu,Jiachen Li,Xianyang Liu,Jiancheng Pan,Jingyuan Sun,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出前瞻式多模态记忆编译框架（PMMC），将记忆推理从查询时转移到记忆整合时，通过预测未来问题并预编译多模态记忆程序，提升回答质量、视觉证据召回率，同时降低查询时延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM智能体记忆系统将视觉体验简化为文本摘要，或依赖静态的"检索-推理"管线，在查询时效率低下，且在需要图文绑定、时间更新或视觉细节的问题上表现脆弱。缺乏将推理过程前置到记忆整合阶段的机制。

Method: 提出Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation（前瞻式多模态记忆编译）框架，包含三个核心组件：Questioner预测未来可能的问题候选，Planner编译问题条件化的多模态记忆程序，Doubter验证规划的证据路径能否支撑预测答案。验证通过的问答对形成结构化问题库，用于查询时的高效路由与证据检索。

Result: 在多模态长时记忆基准上，该方法提升了答案质量和视觉证据召回率，同时降低了查询时的token消耗和延迟成本。消融实验分析了自反馈、动态规划、原始图像访问及问题库覆盖率的影响。

Conclusion: 通过将推理从查询时转移到记忆整合时，前瞻式编译记忆策略能更有效地处理长时多模态交互中的一致性与信息整合问题，为LVLM智能体提供更高效、更鲁棒的记忆机制。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LVLM agents to maintain consistency and integrate information across extended multimodal interactions. Existing agent memory systems, however, often reduce visual experiences into textual summaries or rely on static retrieve-then-reason pipelines, which are inefficient at query time and brittle when questions require image-text binding, temporal updates, or visual details. We propose Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation, a framework that shifts part of the memory reasoning process from query time to memory consolidation time. Given accumulated multimodal interactions, a Questioner predicts future question candidates, a Planner compiles question-conditioned multimodal memory programs, and a Doubter verifies whether the planned evidence path can support the predicted answer. The verified question-program pairs form a structured question bank for efficient query-time routing and evidence retrieval. Experiments on multimodal long-term memory benchmarks show that our method improves answer quality and visual evidence recall while reducing query-time token and latency costs. Extensive ablations analyze the effects of self-feedback, dynamic planning, raw-image access, and question bank coverage.

</details>


### [67] [TrajWiki: Source-Grounded Memory Trajectories for Long-Horizon Dialogue Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00967)
*Jingyu Sun,Yuyang Xue,Mingyang Li,Zhengtao Yao,Jiachen Li,Yang Cui,Wenhao Cai,Haozhe Liu,Fangying Wang,Magdalene Katharina Montgomery,Syed Murtuza Baker,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajWiki是一种基于轨迹的记忆框架，用于长时程对话智能体。它将记忆表示为源证据可追溯的演化轨迹，通过不可变的场景快照和ADD/REVISE/DEPRECATE等声明级操作维护，并引入Memory Wiki中间层将对话历史增量编译为结构化、相互关联的wiki页面，支持分层检索和证据导向的答案生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体在长时程对话中缺乏可追踪、可更新且诊断透明的外部记忆。已有记忆增强方法通常将记忆存储为孤立记录或可覆写状态，难以保留信息的来源、演化过程、冲突关系以及信息随时间而过时的情况，导致鲁棒的长时程对话能力受限。

Method: TrajWiki将每条记忆表示为具有源证据基础的演化轨迹，通过不可变的场景快照和声明级操作（ADD、REVISE、DEPRECATE）维护。同时引入Memory Wiki持久化中间层，将对话历史增量编译为结构化且互连的wiki页面，捕获实体、事件、数量、主题和冲突。推理时，查询从相关wiki页面层级路由到记忆轨迹，再到快照和源消息，实现证据导向的答案合成。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MedMT基准上的实验表明，TrajWiki在开源和闭源LLM主干上均提升了长时程对话性能，同时提供了更强的可解释性和诊断可见性，包括记忆演化、检索失败和答案生成过程的可视化。

Conclusion: TrajWiki通过轨迹式记忆表示和wiki式中间层编译，有效解决了长时程对话中记忆来源可追溯性、可更新性和诊断透明度不足的问题，在提升对话性能的同时增强了系统的可解释性，为记忆增强型对话智能体提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong capabilities in generating coherent and contextually appropriate responses, yet robust long-horizon dialogue remains limited by the lack of external memory that is traceable, updatable, and diagnostically transparent. Existing memory-augmented agents often store memories as isolated records or overwritable states, making it difficult to preserve how information originates, evolves, conflicts, or becomes obsolete over time. We propose TrajWiki, a trajectory-based memory framework for long-horizon conversational agents. Instead of treating memory as static entries, TrajWiki represents each memory as a source-grounded evolution trajectory, maintained through immutable episodic snapshots and claim-level operations such as ADD, REVISE, and DEPRECATE. To reduce fragmentation and retrieval cost, TrajWiki further introduces Memory Wiki, a persistent intermediate layer that incrementally compiles dialogue history into structured and interlinked wiki pages capturing salient entities, events, quantities, topics, and conflicts. At inference time, queries are routed hierarchically from relevant wiki pages to linked memory trajectories, then to corresponding snapshots and source messages for evidence-grounded answer synthesis. Experiments on LoCoMo and MedMT show that TrajWiki improves long-horizon dialogue performance across both open-source and closed-source LLM backbones, while providing greater interpretability and diagnostic visibility into memory evolution, retrieval failures, and answer generation.

</details>


### [68] [PROGRESS: Coverage-guided RL to Train Search-augmented LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00969)
*Sudipta Paul,Vijay Srinivasan,Vivek Kulkarni,Aounon Kumar,Yashas Malur Saidutta,Wenbo Li,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PROGRESS框架，利用教师模型引导的覆盖奖励（coverage reward）来显式塑造检索增强LLM智能体的查询分解行为，在R1风格强化学习训练框架中提供轻量级指导，实验表明能显著提升整体任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强LLM智能体虽然使用强化学习训练，但主要依赖结果级奖励（outcome-level rewards），对搜索行为几乎没有监督，且忽视了智能体正确分解复杂查询的能力。这导致模型在搜索策略上的学习缺乏有效指导。

Method: 提出PROGRESS框架：在训练过程中使用冻结的教师模型将复杂查询分解为必要的搜索查询（essential search queries），利用这些查询来引导策略模型的搜索行为，通过教师引导的覆盖奖励（coverage reward）显式塑造策略模型的查询分解生成。该方法集成在R1风格训练框架中，提供轻量级指导而无需密集的过程级监督。

Result: 实验表明，覆盖奖励引导的强化学习显著提升了整体任务性能，验证了在智能体LLM中显式监督搜索策略的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式监督搜索策略（特别是查询分解行为）比仅依赖结果级奖励更有效，覆盖奖励机制提供了一种高效的轻量级训练范式。

Abstract: Existing search-augmented LLM agents are trained using Reinforcement Learning to boost its reasoning capabilities. However, these approaches primarily rely on outcome-level rewards, which provide little supervision over search behavior and overlook agent's ability to decompose complex queries properly. To mitigate this issue, we propose PROGRESS which utilizes teacher-guided coverage reward to explicitly shape decomposed query generation of the policy model. During training, frozen teacher models are used to decompose complex queries into essential search queries. These essential search queries are utilized to guide the search behavior of the policy model. Integrated into an R1-style training framework, our approach provides lightweight guidance over query decomposition decisions without dense process-level supervision. Experiments show that coverage-guided RL improves overall task performance, highlighting the importance of explicitly supervising search strategies in agentic LLMs.

</details>


### [69] [Search-GRT: Guided Retrieval Training of Search Agents to Optimize for Complex Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00974)
*Aounon Kumar,Sudipta Paul,Vivek Kulkarni,Vijay Srinivasan,Srinivas Chappidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRT是一种通过利用地面真值信息限制RL训练期间检索过程的方法，以增强LLM搜索代理在多跳问答任务中的性能，相比Search-R1可提升超过40%的效果，并提高训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答（MHQA）任务中，LLM需要将问题分解为子查询、检索相关信息并从多个来源综合答案，早期检索不佳会导致级联错误。虽然强化学习（RL）能改善LLM的搜索能力，但训练过程中奖励稀疏，阻碍了模型有效学习。

Method: 提出引导检索训练（GRT, Guided Retrieval Training）：在RL训练期间，利用地面真值信息限制检索过程，仅聚焦于一组精选的相关文档。通过这种约束，GRT为模型提供更强的学习信号，缓解稀疏奖励问题，提升模型生成准确子查询和综合正确答案的能力。

Result: 实验结果表明GRT在多种问答任务上相对于现有方法（如Search-R1）具有一致的性能改进。特别在多跳问答任务中，性能提升超过40%。此外，GRT以更少的训练步骤即可获得更好的问答性能，提高了训练效率。

Conclusion: GRT通过引导检索过程有效解决了RL训练中奖励稀疏的难题，在MHQA任务上取得了显著性能提升，同时提升了训练效率，是一种有效的搜索代理训练方法。

Abstract: The effective use of search engines by large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge, particularly in complex, multi-hop question-answering (MHQA) tasks. These tasks require the model to decompose questions into subqueries, retrieve relevant information, and synthesize answers from multiple sources, often leading to cascading errors due to poor retrieval in early stages. Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in improving LLMs' search capabilities, but it often suffers from sparse rewards during training, hindering the model's ability to learn effectively. To address these challenges, we introduce Guided Retrieval Training (GRT), a novel method that improves the performance of a search agent by restricting the retrieval process during RL training using ground truth information. By focusing on a curated set of relevant documents, GRT provides the model with a stronger learning signal, mitigating the problem of sparse rewards and improving its ability to generate accurate subqueries and synthesize correct answers. Our experimental results demonstrate that GRT achieves consistent performance improvements over existing methods, such as Search-R1, across a wide range of question-answering (QA) tasks. Notably, GRT excels in MHQA tasks, achieving over 40% improvements in performance. Additionally, GRT enhances training efficiency by achieving better QA performance with fewer training steps.

</details>


### [70] [Passing Coarse Marginal Checks Can Be Cheap: Persona Mixtures and Imprecise Treatment-Response Estimates in an LLM Persona Panel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00979)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究考察16个轻量级人格设定GPT-4.1配置组成的固定面板在重复策略博弈中的表现。面板在四个重复博弈单元中的三个通过预注册的宽泛参考条件均值标准，唯一未通过项仅比参考下限低0.011。变异强受提示索引影响，不同不确定性假设下提示间份额估计差异显著（47%-96%）。治疗联合改变了延续过程及其文本表征，标签冲突揭示了表征控制。原始p13结果未被预期家庭控制，应视为复制目标而非发现。外部审查发现家庭错误、依赖、构造和边界不确定性缺陷。研究结果仅涉及一个固定的模型-提示面板，不建立人类可替代性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型日益被用作合成研究参与者，通常通过其边际响应是否与人类数据相似来验证有效性。本研究旨在严格检验人格设定条件下的模型面板在重复策略博弈中是否满足预注册的参考标准，并深入剖析变异来源、治疗效应估计的稳健性以及结果的可复制性，从而推动对LLM作为研究参与者替代品的更严谨评估方法。

Method: 使用16个固定的人格条件化GPT-4.1配置面板，在重复策略博弈中进行实验。采用预注册的宽泛参考条件均值标准进行验证；通过对称Dirichlet敏感性分析（Jeffreys alpha=0.5和alpha=1）及有限机会插入估计来分解提示间变异份额；使用保守的同时间隔估计延续概率对比；通过词语-位置操作和标签冲突实验检测表征控制；进行事后裁决精确门控分析和零调用重分析；使用公共封装验证4,916次确证性Phase 3-5运行。

Result: 面板在四个重复博弈单元中的三个通过预注册标准（未通过项差0.011）。(1)变异份额：对称Dirichlet敏感性下提示间中位份额为63%-71%（alpha=0.5）和47%-53%（alpha=1），而有限机会插入估计为85%-96%。(2)延续概率对比为+0.083和+0.078，保守95%区间分别为[-0.171, +0.330]和[-0.181, +0.330]。(3)词语-位置操作将合作从0/40移至37/40。(4)原始p13结果未经预期家庭控制，事后裁决门控统计功效不足。(5)公共封装验证了4,916次确证性运行，无需实时模型调用。

Conclusion: 预注册的边际标准可以通过而不需要精确估计治疗响应对象；p13应被视为复制目标而非确立的发现；研究揭示的家族错误、依赖、构造和边界不确定性等缺陷表明，仅依赖边际响应相似性来验证LLM作为合成参与者是不够充分的，需要更严格的统计控制和更透明的报告实践。研究结论限定于一个固定的模型-提示面板，不能外推至人类可替代性的论断。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as synthetic research participants and are often validated by whether their marginal responses resemble human data. We study a fixed panel of sixteen lightweight persona-conditioned GPT-4.1 configurations in repeated strategic games. The panel met preregistered broad-reference condition-mean criteria in three of four repeated-game cells; the sole miss was 0.011 below the lower reference bound. Variation was strongly prompt-indexed, but its share depended on uncertainty assumptions: fixed-panel symmetric-Dirichlet sensitivities produced median between-prompt shares of 63%-71% under Jeffreys alpha=0.5 and 47%-53% under alpha=1, while finite-opportunity plug-in estimates were 85%-96%. Aggregate continuation-probability contrasts were +0.083 and +0.078, with conservative simultaneous 95% intervals [-0.171, +0.330] and [-0.181, +0.330]. The treatment jointly changed the continuation process and its textual representation. A separate wording-and-position operation shifted cooperation from 0/40 to 37/40 in the bare configuration, and a label conflict also revealed representation control. The original persona-level p13 result was not prospectively family-controlled, while a post-adjudication exact gate was structurally underpowered; p13 is therefore a replication target rather than a finding. The results concern one fixed model-prompt panel and do not establish human substitutability.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学系统宣称新能力时仅依赖基准提升、描述长度门槛或p值，但这些都无法区分真正的能力提升与额外的搜索努力、更换验证器或适应不可靠预言机带来的虚假增益。需要一个能严格分离这些混淆因素的审计框架。

Result: 单次不可靠预言机可将能力宣称膨胀至43/60（基线0/60），但换三个预测器后仅1/60存活；外部裁决者确认自身2/43对比最小自由能求解器26/43（p=8e-7）；六个前沿模型中两个无超时者在外部判决上达到0.293对比0.095（p=5e-5），用更少计算预算取得；审计达到两阶：外部裁决者下的差异（达成）、非计算购买（双向达成），但机制可迁移性未达（七种候选无一改变统计量）；自家系统匹配无方向搜索为零，84%的头条效应来自随机序列已能解决的目标。

Conclusion: 审计框架揭示了能力宣称审计的三级阶梯，并展示了无论对敌方系统还是己方系统都同样严格的原则；局限在于面板本身共享最近邻热力学参数，存在相关性偏差（kappa=0.673）。

Abstract: When a self-improving AI-for-science system claims a new capability, the evidence is usually a benchmark delta, a description-length gate, or a p-value. None separates a real gain from extra search, from a changed verifier, or from adaptation to a fallible oracle. We build a two-sided audit whose negative side is a formal fact: a pseudoknot-free oracle provably cannot represent a crossing base pair, so the prior verifier's range is bounded exactly, offline, before any run. "New" is relative to the agent's prior self, never to the base model. First, how far a single fallible oracle can inflate a capability claim. An invented, solver-free operator solves 43/60 crossing RNA targets under the predictor it optimizes, above a context-free floor of 0/60; under three predictors, 1/60 survives. Paired on the same 43 targets, a predictor the operator never saw confirms 2 of its designs against 26 for a minimum-free-energy solver (p = 8e-7). No statistic computed from the system and its own oracle sees that gap. Second, agent-written procedures can beat a human-written one under a judge no objective can flatter, at a fraction of the compute. Of six frontier models, the two whose operators ran without timeouts carry over at 0.293 against our 0.095 (n = 951 paired units, target-clustered [+0.108, +0.297], p = 5e-5) while spending 4.6-10x fewer oracle calls. Three rungs: difference under an outside adjudicator (reached), not bought with compute (reached, both directions), mechanism identified and transferable (not reached; seven candidates tested, none moves the statistic). The ceiling is the panel itself: its three predictors share nearest-neighbour thermodynamic parameters, two agreeing at kappa = 0.673. The audit is as unsparing about our own system: matched undirected search is an exact zero, and a search-free probe puts 84% of our headline effect on targets a random sequence already solves.

</details>

*Shrenil Shaun Sharma,Avi Sharma*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理调度问题时，面对语义相同但表面形式不同的自然语言描述是否仍能保持约束满足性和调度可行性，这一问题尚未得到系统评估。现有基准未关注NL表面形式变化对LLM调度求解的约束忠实度影响。

Abstract: This paper introduces SCHEDBench, a natural-language benchmark for evaluating combinatorial scheduling constraint faithfulness under surface-form variation. Grounded in canonical scheduling instances and solver-derived feasibility and optimality, SCHEDBench assesses whether large language models (LLMs) generate schedules with the same constraint-feasible behavior across varied natural-language (NL) surface forms. SCHEDBench spans 1,132 instances across job-shop scheduling problems (JSP), single and multi-mode resource-constrained project scheduling problems (RCPSP), nurse rostering/scheduling, and curriculum timetabling problems of varying difficulty. Instances are templated into natural language problems using domain-specific templates, themed entities, lexical-syntactic template rephrasing, and constraint-level surface-form variation, with reference solutions verified for feasibility and objective optimality. Across thirteen frontier and open-weight LLMs, we find that models are not reliably invariant to semantically equivalent renderings of the same scheduling problem. Surface-form variation reduces feasibility and induces above-noise shifts in per-instance hard-constraint violations on matched instances. Among the tested isolated axes, constraint reordering yields the clearest above-noise sensitivity.

</details>


### [73] [Judging Is Not Enumerating: Silent Omissions in LLM-Authored Acceptable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01000)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Peiji Long,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型作为"出题者"（而非"答题者"）时的能力，发现模型在一枪式贪婪创作（无测试时推理）的部署协议下，判断候选答案是否属于某集合的能力远优于自主创作该集合本身。通过四种参考构造实验，作者定位了缺陷根源：模型并非缺少知识或无法描述规范，而是无法将规范所蕴含的区域具体物化（materialise）。最核心的错误是"遗漏"（omission），它天然抵抗审查——过包含可以被审查者发现，而缺失成员本身即创作问题。研究还表明：将创作的验证器接入RLVR会损失准确度；门控已知正确的探针可大幅降低误拒率但保留率低；按参考执行结果重写错误期望值可将产出率提升3.3-10.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型正越来越多地从"应试者"转变为"出题者"，负责编写测试套件、答案键、评分标准和奖励函数。作者希望衡量这一角色所要求的能力，并验证在常用的部署协议（one-shot贪婪生成、无测试时推理）下模型是否胜任。

Method: 使用四个参考构造进行实验：(1)两种具有完全有限真理的构造；(2)具有加固可执行参考的HumanEval+/MBPP+；(3)具有显式不完备词汇参考的WordNet。比较模型判断候选归属与自主创作集合的能力，并通过判定谓词（predicate）而非其外延（extension）的控制实验定位缺陷。同时测试遗漏与过包含的检测不对称性，以及将创作验证器接入RLVR的影响和两种修复策略（门控与重写）。

Result: 在不可穷尽证明的算法构造上，创作与判断差距为+0.34到+0.29 F1（横跨24倍参数范围）且不收敛；在可执行代码上，模型判断F1为0.74-0.90，但创作的套件仅接纳19-42%的Oracle正确解。控制实验显示，要求输出谓词而非外延达到F1约0.99，说明失败不是知识缺失或无法指定，而是无法物化规范所诱导的区域。模型检测植入的过包含比遗漏多6-7倍；生产部署43,227项中遗漏优先失败比率为10:1。接入RLVR时，创作的答案键相对精确Oracle损失1.9点准确度、相对WordNet损失18.5点（六组配对种子，p=0.031）。门控修复将误拒从58-92%降至最高5%，但仅保留5-39%的套件；重写修复将四个创作家族的产出率提升3.3-10.6倍。

Conclusion: 语言模型作为"出题者"在标准部署协议下能力不足，核心缺陷是物化能力的缺失——能判断、能描述，但不能将规范完整转化为集合本身。主导错误是难以审计的遗漏而非可辨别的过包含。建议从门控验证（保守但低收益）转向重写修复（高效但需参考执行）来提升创作质量。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly promoted from examinees to examiners: they write the test suites, answer keys, rubrics, and reward functions that define correctness for other systems. We measure the capability that role assumes and find it lacking under the protocol the role is usually deployed with, one-shot greedy authoring with no test-time reasoning. Across four reference constructions - two with complete finite truth, one with a hardened executable reference (HumanEval+/MBPP+), one with an explicitly incomplete lexical reference (WordNet) - models judge whether a candidate belongs far better than they author the set itself. On the incompleteness-proof algorithmic construction the gap is +0.34 to +0.29 F1 over a 24x parameter range and does not close; on executable code, models judging at F1 0.74-0.90 author suites admitting only 19-42% of oracle-correct solutions. A control locates the deficit: asked to emit the predicate rather than its extension, the same models reach F1 about 0.99. The failure is not missing knowledge or an inability to specify, but an inability to materialise the region a specification induces. The dominant error is omission, which resists audit: an over-inclusion is a token a reviewer can challenge, a missing member an absence whose discovery is the authoring problem itself. Models detect planted over-inclusions 6-7x more often than planted omissions, and a production deployment of 43,227 items fails omission-first at 10:1. Wired into RLVR, an authored key costs 1.9 points of accuracy against an exact oracle and 18.5 WordNet-relative (six paired seeds, p=0.031). Gating authored verifiers on a known-correct probe cuts false rejection from 58-92% to at most 5%, but keeps only 5-39% of suites. Repairing them instead, by rewriting each wrong expected value to what a reference execution returns, raises yield 3.3-10.6x across four author families.

</details>


### [74] [From AI Technical Debt to Agentic Technical Debt: A Systematic Mapping of Root Causes and Manifestations in Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01001)
*Muhammad Tukur,Hayatullahi B. Adeyemo,Tao Chen,Nour Ali,Anis Zarrad,Marco Agus,Rick Kazman,Rami Bahsoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地将AI技术债务（AITD）扩展到自主智能体（Agentic AI）系统，提出"智能体技术债务"（AgTD）概念，并将其定义为因Agentic AI系统的自主性和协作性而产生、积累、传播和放大的技术债务。通过理论驱动的转换方法论，论文将既有AITD映射到智能体化表现，并探讨其对AI TRiSM的影响，建立了AgTD作为软件工程基础构念的分析框架、分类体系和未来研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI技术债务（AITD）的研究假设系统架构是静态且组件级别的，无法捕捉Agentic AI环境中动态和涌现的行为特征。随着Agentic AI系统（具备自主推理、多智能体协作、工具编排、自适应决策和持久记忆）的出现，传统AI流水线正向动态软件生态系统转变，亟需一个能够反映这一根本性转变的技术债务分析框架。

Method: 基于先前对31种AITD（分布在七个根因类别）的系统性范围综述（systematic scoping review），采用理论驱动的转换方法论（theory-informed transformation methodology），通过三种转换策略——直接转换（direct transformation）、情境转换（contextual transformation）和表现扩展（manifestation expansion）——将既有技术债务重新解释为Agentic AI环境中的表现形式。

Result: 首次提供了既有AITD到其Agentic化表现的系统性映射，展示常规债务如何演变为系统级负债，包括内存不一致、编排脆弱性、级联故障和不安全的自主决策。研究发现技术债务已超越软件工件本身，扩展到智能体行为、协调机制以及智能体、工具和执行环境之间的交互。此外，研究还分析了AgTD对AI信任、风险与安全管理（AI TRiSM）的影响，涵盖可信度、治理、安全、运营韧性和可持续性技术债务等方面。

Conclusion: 这项工作将AgTD确立为软件工程的基础性构念，提供了转换框架、分类体系和未来研究议程，用于管理自主多智能体AI系统中的技术债务，填补了AITD研究在Agentic AI环境中的空白。

Abstract: The emergence of Agentic AI systems, characterized by autonomous reasoning, multi-agent collaboration, tool orchestration, adaptive decision-making, and persistent memory, represents a fundamental shift from traditional AI pipelines to dynamic software ecosystems. While AI Technical Debt (AITD) has been widely studied in machine learning and software engineering, existing models assume static, component-level architectures and fail to capture the dynamic and emergent behaviors of agentic environments. To address this gap, this paper introduces Agentic Technical Debt (AgTD), defined as technical debt that emerges, accumulates, propagates, and amplifies due to the autonomous and collaborative nature of Agentic AI systems. Building on our prior systematic scoping review of 31 AITDs across seven root-cause categories, we employ a theory-informed transformation methodology to reinterpret these debts in Agentic AI through direct transformation, contextual transformation, and manifestation expansion. We present the first systematic mapping of established AITDs to their agentic manifestations, showing how conventional debts evolve into system-level liabilities, including memory inconsistencies, orchestration fragility, cascading failures, and unsafe autonomous decision-making. Our findings show that technical debt extends beyond software artifacts to encompass agent behaviors, coordination mechanisms, and interactions among agents, tools, and execution environments. We further examine its implications for AI Trust, Risk, and Security Management (AI TRiSM), highlighting impacts on trustworthiness, governance, security, operational resilience, and Sustainability Technical Debt. Overall, this work establishes AgTD as a foundational software engineering construct and provides a transformation framework, taxonomy, and research agenda for managing technical debt in autonomous multi-agent AI systems.

</details>


### [75] [Toward Fine-Grained Forgetting:Attribute Unlearning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01008)
*Junkai Lin,Junkai Chen,Siqi Hou,Yuhao He,Ruiqi Liu,Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出属性级多模态大语言模型（MLLM）机器遗忘任务，并构建了涵盖长文本、数值和短文本目标的基准数据集。作者提出CLRP（因果定位与保留感知投影）框架，通过激活修补定位目标属性泄露的因果层，再应用保留感知投影移除目标属性子空间同时保留同身份证据，在多种架构和参数规模的MLLM上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私导向的机器遗忘基准主要采用轮廓级（profile-level）删除，但实际请求往往更细粒度：模型应遗忘指定属性而保留同一身份的非敏感信息。属性级遗忘中，目标属性和保留属性共享身份特征和视觉证据，导致选择性遗忘容易出现残余泄漏或附带性退化，现有方法在此场景下表现出不稳定的遗忘-保留权衡。

Method: 提出CLRP（Causal Localization and Retain-Aware Projection）框架，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 使用激活修补（activation patching）定位因果调节目标属性泄露的层；(2) 应用保留感知投影（retain-aware projection），移除目标属性子空间的同时保留同身份的证据信息。该框架是轻量级的、无需训练的。

Result: 在多种广泛使用的、不同架构和参数规模的MLLM上进行了实验，验证了CLRP框架的有效性，表明其在属性级遗忘任务中能够改善遗忘与保留之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统地提出属性级MLLM遗忘任务，构建了专门的基准，并证明了现有方法在此细粒度场景下的不足；提出的CLRP框架作为训练无关的轻量解决方案，能有效解决属性级遗忘中的残余泄漏与附带性退化问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) exhibit strong vision--language capabilities but may also memorize and disclose sensitive information. Machine unlearning seeks to remove designated knowledge without retraining from scratch while preserving general utility. Existing privacy-oriented benchmarks primarily adopt profile-level deletion, whereas practical requests are often finer grained: a model should forget a specified attribute while retaining non-sensitive information about the same identity. We therefore introduce attribute-level MLLM unlearning as a finer-grained task and construct a benchmark spanning long-text, numeric, and short-text targets, multiple forget ratios, and diverse question types. Our evaluation reveals that target and retained attributes share identity-specific and visual evidence, making selective forgetting susceptible to residual leakage or collateral degradation; accordingly, existing methods exhibit unstable forgetting--retention trade-offs in this setting. To address this challenge, we propose Causal Localization and Retain-Aware Projection (CLRP), a lightweight training-free framework. CLRP uses activation patching to identify the layer that causally mediates target-attribute disclosure, then applies a retain-aware projection that removes the target-attribute subspace while preserving same-identity evidence. Experiments across multiple widely used MLLMs with distinct architectures and parameter scales demonstrate the effectiveness of CLRP.

</details>


### [76] [FactorJEPA: Factorizing Monolithic Futures into Layout-Agent-Interaction Channels for Crowded and Chaotic Global South Urban Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01049)
*Kapil Wanaskar,Gaytri Jena,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Vasu Sharma,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DENSEWORLD，一个包含1,000小时、22个城市、涵盖驾驶/步行/航拍视频的稠密城市环境数据集，并引入FactorJEPA架构——将世界结构分解为布局、实体和交互三个因子，通过可见性门控和分离子空间来保留部分可观测的智能体并避免跨因子捷径。FactorJEPA在未来潜在准确性、因果预测和视觉鲁棒性上均优于现有JEPA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）的评估多集中在低密度、有车道结构的环境中，忽略了一个重要的现实场景：全球南方（Global South）人口稠密、混乱且嘈杂的城市环境（称为DENSEWORLD）。这些场景具有软空间边界、极端智能体异质性、持续遮挡以及混合交通下的快速社会协商等独特挑战，而现有JEPA公式在异构和部分可观测条件下难以保持稠密的交互动态。

Method: 1) 构建DENSEWORLD数据集：1,000小时的驾驶、步行和航拍视频，覆盖22个城市；2) 提出FactorJEPA架构：将世界结构作为第一类预测原语，不再将未来编码为单一潜在表示，而是分解为布局（layout）、实体（entities）和交互（interactions）三个因子，采用可见性门控（visibility gate）和分离子空间（separated subspaces）以保留部分观测的智能体并防止跨因子捷径。在V-JEPA 2.1的2B和1B骨干上进行训练。

Result: FactorJEPA在三项指标上优于现有JEPA：(i) 未来潜在准确性（Future-frame L1）、(ii) 干预敏感预测（Causal L1）、(iii) 降低视觉证据时的鲁棒性（Mask-ratio slope），同时揭示了可复现的运动信息权衡（Motion cosine）。方法排名在2B和1B V-JEPA 2.1骨架上保持一致，Spearman相关系数rho=0.895至0.978。

Conclusion: FactorJEPA证明将世界结构分解为显式因子（布局、实体、交互）能够显著提升稠密、异构和部分可观测的城市环境中的世界模型预测能力。数据集和模型检查点已公开，为该领域提供了新的基准和资源。

Abstract: World models have attracted significant attention for their ability to capture and predict the structure and dynamics of the physical world. In this emerging landscape, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) offer a particularly compelling direction.
  We study a largely unexplored regime: populous, crowded, and chaotic Global South urban environments, which we call DENSEWORLD. Unlike the lower-density, lane-structured settings that dominate existing evaluations, these scenes exhibit soft spatial boundaries, extreme agent heterogeneity, persistent occlusion, and rapid social negotiation under mixed traffic. We introduce the first large-scale dataset for this regime: 1,000 hours of drive-through, walk-through, and aerial video across 22 cities. Existing JEPA formulations struggle to preserve dense interaction dynamics under heterogeneity and partial observability.
  We introduce FactorJEPA, which makes world structure a first-class predictive primitive. Rather than encoding the future in a monolithic latent, it composes layout, entities, and interactions, using a visibility gate and separated subspaces to preserve partially observed agents and discourage cross-factor shortcuts. FactorJEPA improves (i) future-latent accuracy (Future-frame L1), (ii) intervention-sensitive prediction (Causal L1), and (iii) robustness to reduced visual evidence (Mask-ratio slope), while exposing (iv) a reproducible motion-information trade-off (Motion cosine). Method rankings replicate across 2B and 1B V-JEPA 2.1 backbones, with rho = 0.895 to 0.978.
  We publicly release the DENSEWORLD-115k dataset (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/denseworld-115k) and the surgery-trained FactorJEPA checkpoints (https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymousML123/factorjepa-outputs/tree/main/outputs/full/vjepa_2_1_vitg_1B/train/m09c_surgery_3stage_DI_diheavy_encoder).

</details>


### [77] [Don't Offer What Can't Be Done: Deterministic Executability Gating for LLM Skill Selection at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01050)
*Ortal Ashkenazi,Vitalii Kloz,Mykhailo Ulianchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Helpmate（Wix客服助手）中部署的三阶段技能选择流水线：语义匹配→可执行性门控→LLM决策。通过确定性可执行性门控在语义匹配之后移除无法在当前账户状态下执行的技能候选，在生产分析中节省了90.5%的技能描述上下文，并通过反事实实验证明门控能防止不可执行候选影响模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的LLM代理从大型技能库中选择技能时面临一个局限：语义相关性不足以决定技能能否在用户当前账户状态下真正执行。一个技能可能在主题上匹配用户请求，但当前账户状态下无法执行，导致无效调用或错误行为。

Method: 提出三阶段选择流水线：(1) 召回导向的语义匹配器，不参考账户状态，识别与十个技能领域家族相关的消息；(2) 确定性可执行性门控，检查候选人内部硬停止条件，由于门控与技能评估相同的退出谓词，被阻止的候选在同一账户状态下必然无法完成；(3) LLM决定是否调用剩余候选技能。

Result: 在生产分析中（756.6K用户消息，267.6K会话），语义匹配保留23.1%消息；门控移除59.4%的技能-消息对，节省2.288亿技能描述token（降低59.1%后语义技能描述足迹）；整体上下文减少90.5%。反事实实验（1000个风险增强会话）显示，暴露所有技能时模型在7.8%的会话中选择了被生产环境阻止的技能，证明门控防止不可执行候选影响模型选择。

Conclusion: 确定性可执行性门控能有效防止非可执行技能影响LLM的模型选择，相比仅依赖语义相关性显著精简上下文。反事实分析表明门控不仅减小上下文规模，还实质性地改变了模型行为。同时作者谨慎指出该研究未声称对下游工具执行或客户结果的影响。

Abstract: Production LLM agents that select from large skill libraries face a limitation that semantic relevance alone cannot resolve: a skill may match a user's topic yet be impossible to execute in the current account state. We present a deployed three-stage selection pipeline for Helpmate, Wix's customer-care assistant. First, a recall-oriented semantic matcher identifies messages related to a ten-skill domain family without consulting account state. Second, a deterministic executability gate removes candidates whose internal hard-stop conditions hold. Because the gate and the skill evaluate the same exit predicates, every blocked candidate would be unable to complete under the same account state, provided predicate parity is preserved and both checks observe fresh authoritative state. Finally, the LLM decides whether to invoke one of the remaining candidates.
  In a post-launch production analysis of 756.6K user messages across 267.6K conversations, semantic matching retained 174,927 messages (23.1%). Within this matched stream, the gate removed 1,039,462 of 1,749,270 skill-message pairs (59.4%), saving 228.8 million skill-description tokens -- 59.1% of the post-semantic skill-description footprint. Together, semantic matching and executability gating reduced skill-description context by 90.5% relative to exposing all ten skills to every message.
  To test whether this pruning affects model behavior rather than context size alone, we replayed a risk-enriched cohort of 1,000 conversations with all ten skills exposed. The model selected a production-blocked skill in 78 conversations (7.8%). This counterfactual result shows that deterministic gating prevents non-executable candidates from influencing model selection, while not claiming downstream tool execution or customer-outcome effects.

</details>


### [78] [Control Under Compression: Reliability Frontiers for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01056)
*Yinghan Hou,Zongyou Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CompressAgent，一个基于环境验证的Agent控制上下文（ACC）压缩基准测试平台，涵盖9个ACC、3个任务族、3个Qwen API模型、6个保留上下文预算及15,525次运行，揭示压缩质量存在非线性、依赖方法的可靠性前沿，并将ACC压缩重新定义为运行时可靠性问题而非单纯的token缩减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型语言模型Agent不仅受任务提示词驱动，还受持续的系统端指令（指定工具、参数、策略、执行协议和恢复机制）控制。压缩这些Agent控制上下文（ACC）可降低输入成本和上下文占用，但现有提示压缩评估无法揭示压缩后的控制是否在操作层面仍然可靠，缺乏基于可执行结果验证的基准。

Method: 构建CompressAgent基准：包含9个独立构建的ACC、3个任务族、3个固定Qwen API模型标识符、6个保留上下文预算（涵盖75%到10%），共15,525次运行。在环境中验证压缩后Agent的实际工具执行成功率，对比不同压缩方法（通用重写、基于章节的压缩、义务感知压缩等）在不同保留预算下的可靠性表现。

Result: 发现非线性、方法依赖的可靠性前沿：在75%保留上下文时，通用重写和章节压缩分别达到92.7%和92.4%成功率，接近93.8%的全上下文基线；在50%-35%区间方法间差异急剧分化，35%时章节压缩、义务感知和通用重写分别为47.0%、39.0%和19.9%；在25%-10%预算下可执行协议变得脆弱。可靠性在不同ACC间差异显著，压缩失败主要表现为工具执行和动作解析错误。

Conclusion: ACC压缩应视为运行时可靠性问题，需通过可执行结果进行评估。通用压缩器排名不适用，应针对每个ACC进行资格认证（per-context qualification）。该工作将压缩评估从token缩减范式转向运行时可靠性范式。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents are governed not only by task prompts but also by persistent system-side instructions that specify tools, arguments, policies, execution protocols, and recovery. Compressing these agent control contexts (ACCs) can reduce input cost and context use, yet existing prompt-compression evaluations do not reveal whether the resulting control remains operationally reliable. We introduce CompressAgent, an environment-verified benchmark for ACC compression across nine independently constructed ACCs, three task families, three fixed Qwen API model identifiers, six retained-context budgets, and 15,525 runs. We uncover a nonlinear, method-dependent reliability frontier. At 75% retained context, generic rewriting and section-based compression achieve 92.7% and 92.4% success, close to the 93.8% full-context baseline. Between 50% and 35%, methods diverge sharply; at 35%, section-based, obligation-aware, and generic rewriting achieve 47.0%, 39.0%, and 19.9%. At retained-context budgets from 25% to 10%, executable protocols become fragile. Reliability also varies substantially across ACCs, making universal compressor rankings inappropriate and motivating per-context qualification. Failure analysis shows that compression primarily surfaces as tool-execution and action-parsing errors. These findings recast ACC compression from token reduction into a runtime-reliability problem that must be evaluated through executable outcomes.

</details>


### [79] [Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals: Separating Matching and Content Retrieval Across Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01075)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出角色解耦注意力残差（RD-AttnRes），将查询/键的深度路由与值的深度路由解耦，使注意力匹配和内容检索从残差层级中不同深度读取信息。在120M和343M参数的paired预训练实验中，所有10组对比均改进了验证集负对数似然，困惑度分别降低2.97%和2.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Block Attention Residuals使用单一的内容相关深度混合来构造查询、键和值的输入，这耦合了两个功能上不同的决策：查询和键决定注意力在哪里匹配，而值决定检索什么内容。作者质疑匹配和内容检索是否应该被迫从相同的深度读取，从而引出深度路由解耦的必要性。

Method: RD-AttnRes是一个最小扩展：查询和键共享一条深度路由，而值为同一残差源学习独立的深度路由。将两条路由绑定即可精确恢复父架构（Block Attention Residuals），解耦仅每层增加一个模型宽度向量，且不引入额外的token对token注意力操作。实验采用冻结的paired预训练协议，在FineWeb-Edu上使用5个匹配种子进行120M和343M参数的训练，预算为20亿token。

Result: RD-AttnRes在所有10组配对比较中均改进验证集负对数似然。120M和343M参数模型的平均减少分别为0.0301和0.0247，对应困惑度分别降低2.97%和2.43%。早期预算控制实验排除了参数量增加、重复路由执行或固定值路由等混淆因素。路由诊断显示查询-键深度分布与值深度分布持续存在分歧。
