To cross the modality gap, V-Mem organizes the conversation into rounds and returns the target-modality content from the same round as the match, without comparing across modalities. To close the similarity-relevance gap, it searches with an LLM-generated anchor that sits closer to the relevant evidence than the query does: a hypothetical caption for a text-only query seeking an image, and an enriched search anchor, the query text plus relevant keywords extracted from the query image, when the evidence is reachable only by combining the two. On Mem-Gallery, V-Mem reaches an LLM-judge score of 0.82 versus 0.56 for the second best, with the largest margin on questions carrying an image (0.87, no baseline above 0.47); on LoCoMo it scores 0.69 versus 0.58.

</details>


### [112] [Emergence Invariance: From Symbolized Thought to Interface Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01548)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"符号化-子结构论题"（Symbolization–Substructure Thesis），将语言模型视为从不完备认知子结构上生成人类似思维形式的结构化符号系统，通过引入"涌现不变性"和"接口信息论"形式化框架，证明规模扩展只能减小补偿差距C_s而无法消除接口底层R_φ^*，并通过DeepSeek V4-Flash实验验证了"在接口内扩展"与"精炼接口本身"之间的本质分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在规模扩展中表现出涌现能力（如上下文学习、多步推理、工具使用等），但其语言优先的概率架构相对人类认知存在实质性、基质性和高层级的不完备性。核心问题是：涌现能力能否补偿每一个缺失的认知区分？这一哲学前提需要被形式化检验。

Method: 1) 形式化哲学前提为Symbolization–Substructure Thesis，引入涌现不变性概念；2) 提出核心公式 R_s^* = R_φ^* + C_s，其中R_φ^*为接口底限（interface floor），C_s为补偿差距；3) 证明在固定输入定律下，一个接口普遍不比另一接口信息量少的充要条件是其完备信息σ-域细化另一个；4) 在DeepSeek V4-Flash API上进行匹配实验，包括指针追踪、观察双胞胎和决定性记忆恢复三项测试。

Result: 理论证明：总补偿恰好发生在接口底限和渐近补偿差距同时消失时。实验结果表明：思维链使指针追踪从0/16提升到14/16（当相关区分可用时）；精确观察双胞胎停留在50%构造底限；恢复决定性记忆使匹配性能从50%提升到100%。这些结果为"接口内扩展"与"精炼接口本身"的分离提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 规模扩展无法完全补偿语言优先架构相对于人类认知的固有不完备性；增强LLM能力需要超越单纯扩展规模，转向精炼接口本身（如增强记忆、区分能力等），即同时减小C_s和R_φ^*两个量。

Abstract: Language can be viewed as a formalized subset of thought: a consequence-governed symbolic structure projected from wider situated cognition. Large language models trained at scale exhibit compensatory emergence: sparse architectural primitives support in-context learning, multi-step reasoning, tool use, and chain of thought. Yet a language-first probabilistic architecture inherits substantive, substrate, and high-level incompletenesses relative to human cognition. Their coexistence makes an LLM a human-like thought-form generator that reconstructs increasingly human-like reasoning forms from an incomplete substrate. We ask whether emergence can compensate for every missing distinction. We formalize the philosophical premise as the Symbolization--Substructure Thesis and introduce emergence invariance. For a scale-indexed family acting through a shared task interface $φ$, $\mathcal{R}_s^*=\mathcal{R}_φ^*+C_s$: scale can reduce the compensation gap $C_s$, while a positive interface floor $\mathcal{R}_φ^*$ persists. We prove that, under a fixed input law, one interface is universally no less informative exactly when its completed information $σ$-field refines the other, and that total compensation occurs exactly when both the interface floor and asymptotic compensation gap vanish. The framework unifies existing results on grounding, memory, position, attention, Bayesian inheritance, scientific abduction, and reasoning control. In a matched DeepSeek V4-Flash API study, thinking improves pointer chasing from $0/16$ to $14/16$ when relevant distinctions are available; exact observational twins remain at their $50\%$ construction floor; and restoring decisive memory moves matched performance from $50\%$ to $100\%$. These results provide initial evidence for the predicted separation between scaling within an interface and refining the interface itself.

</details>


### [113] [Securing Agentic AI: From Per-Action Checks to Trajectory Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01558)
*Alireza Lotfi,Subangkar Karmaker Shanto,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地勾勒了基于LLM的自主智能体安全挑战的全景图谱，从单智能体、多智能体到整个智能体技术栈层面分析了攻击面与安全缺口，提出安全应成为治理智能体行为的架构、协议和运行时中可验证的属性，而非可选的指导层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体日益自主化并跨组织边界委派任务，其安全性不再由单个动作的正确性决定，而是由整体行为是否与系统规则、组织策略和监管要求保持一致来判定。传统"单点安全"视角已无法应对覆盖整个智能体技术栈的相互关联的安全挑战，因此需要在全局层面系统性地梳理这些挑战。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述与分析的方法，按智能体堆栈层次划分安全挑战：1）单智能体层面分析提示注入、记忆污染、检索知识攻击和工具接口等不受信任输入的攻击面；2）多智能体层面分析委派与通信引入的身份、信任、能力控制和决策透明度问题；3）关注底层模型路由与执行控制平面的操纵风险及模型来源验证问题；4）提出行为约束（behavioral containment）作为最根本的挑战——单个可允许动作的序列可能在集体层面违反系统级约束；5）在更宏观层面讨论供应链完整性、来源追踪、问责和端到端可观测性等开放问题。

Result: 本文识别出安全必须从可选的"指导层"转变为架构、协议和运行时中可验证的属性这一统一原则，并给出了通向可信自主智能体部署的路线图。文章系统性揭示了从单智能体到系统级、从技术层面到治理层面的完整安全挑战矩阵。

Conclusion: 自主智能体的安全是一个横跨整个智能体技术栈的相互关联的复杂问题，涵盖攻击面管理、身份与信任、行为约束、供应链完整性和可观测性等多个维度。核心结论是：要实现可信的自主智能体部署，必须将安全作为架构级可验证属性进行内生设计，而非事后附加的防护层。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly used to execute consequential tasks in environments governed by operational constraints, organizational policies, regulatory requirements, and technical standards. Their safety is therefore determined not by the correctness of individual actions, but by whether their overall behavior remains consistent with the rules and invariants of the systems in which they operate. As large language model (LLM)-based agents become more autonomous and increasingly delegate tasks across organizational boundaries, securing them evolves from a single challenge into a broad and interconnected landscape spanning the entire agentic stack. At the single-agent level, untrusted inputs through prompts, memory, retrieved knowledge, and tool interfaces create attack surfaces. In multi-agent settings, delegation and communication introduce challenges related to identity, trust, capability control, and decision transparency, while the underlying model routing and execution control plane remains vulnerable to manipulation and to unverified model provenance. Perhaps the most fundamental challenge is behavioral containment: sequences of individually permissible actions may collectively violate system-level constraints and safety invariants. At the broader level, supply-chain integrity, provenance, accountability, and end-to-end observability remain largely open problems. A common principle unifies these directions: security must become a verifiable property of the architectures, protocols, and runtimes that govern agent behavior, rather than an optional layer of guidance. Charting these challenges provides a roadmap toward trustworthy autonomous agent deployment.

</details>


### [114] [Does the Competitive Component of Adversarial Self-Play Improve Legal Reasoning? A Controlled Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01559)
*Miseog Shawn Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过精心设计的可验证"生存奖励"训练信号（学生引用的权威与对手的反权威均经过引文验证器检查）在合法推理任务中检验对抗性自我对弈的价值，四项独立测试和一项强化对手的试点均显示竞争性组件未带来可靠收益，报告为诚实的阴性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性自我对弈（学生起草论点、对手攻击、学生在攻击中存活则获奖励）被认为是合法推理任务的一种吸引力方案。作者希望回答一个狭义但重要的问题：竞争性组件本身（对手和生存奖励）相比于完全相同的非竞争性训练运行是否带来了额外增益。

Method: 设计可验证的"生存"奖励——学生的引用权威和对手的反权威均通过引文验证器检查，使"存活"基于经过验证的事实而非修辞，并自动中和捏造的引文。随后通过四项独立测试进行比较：bootstrap对比、双种子复制、逐案例配对对抗鲁棒性比较、盲审生成论点头对头判断，以及一项刻意强化自博弈对手的后续试点。

Result: 竞争性组件在所有测试中均未产生可靠收益：盲审胜率为49%（二项检验p约1.000）；强化对手试点胜率为50%（32:32，p约1.000）；早期表面上的+29%优势在小样本下反转消失。

Conclusion: 多教师课程的价值源于构建可验证的环境而非竞争本身，这一结论在合法推理领域得到再次确认，与同伴编码领域研究（Kim, 2026）的结论一致。论文价值在于可复现性和分享具体陷阱：一个最初有希望的指标在更多数据上反转，以及一个对抗鲁棒性指标在对手停止引用与金标准相同权威时悄然退化为普通召回率。

Abstract: Adversarial self-play is an appealing recipe for legal reasoning: have a student model draft an argument, have an adversary attack it, and reward the student when its argument survives the attack. We designed exactly such a training signal -- a verifiable "survival" reward in which both the student's cited authorities and the adversary's counter-authorities are checked by a citation verifier, so that survival is decided on verified grounds rather than rhetoric, and fabricated citations are automatically neutralized. We then asked a narrow but important question: does the competitive component itself -- the adversary and the survival reward -- add anything on top of an otherwise identical non-competitive training run? Across four independent tests -- a bootstrap comparison, a two-seed replication, a paired per-case adversarial-robustness comparison, and a blinded head-to-head judgment of generated arguments, plus a follow-up pilot with a deliberately strengthened self-play adversary -- the competitive component produced no reliable benefit. The blinded judgment gave a 49% win rate (binomial p approx. 1.000); the strengthened-adversary pilot gave a 50% win rate (32:32, p approx. 1.000). An early apparent +29% advantage reversed and proved to be a small-sample artifact. We report this as an honest negative result. The value of the paper is reproducibility and the sharing of concrete pitfalls: an initially promising metric that inverted on more data, and an adversarial-robustness metric that silently collapsed to plain recall once the adversary stopped citing the same authorities as the gold answer. This null is consistent with, and reconfirms in the legal domain, the conclusion of the companion coding-domain study (Kim, 2026, arXiv:2607.08255) that the value of multi-teacher curricula arises from constructing a verifiable environment rather than from competition itself.

</details>


### [115] [Is More Privileged Information Better? From Solution Traces to Problem-Solving Structure in Self-Distilled Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01589)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Zhihu Wang,Xu Caiyue,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PS-OPSD（问题空间引导的在线策略自蒸馏），用轨迹接地的问题空间引导（描述初始状态、目标条件、约束和状态转移路径）替代完整参考解作为教师视图的监督信号，在三个数学推理基准和1.7B-8B规模模型上取得最佳纯问题准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD中教师提供的token级目标可能依赖推理时不可用的参考解特定信息，导致学生视图与教师视图之间的信息不对齐，限制了蒸馏效果。

Method: 用描述初始状态、目标条件、约束及选定的状态转移路径的轨迹接地引导（trajectory-grounded guidance）替代完整参考解作为教师视图的监督目标，学生rollout和OPSD目标保持不变。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准和1.7B到8B的模型规模上，PS-OPSD在纯问题准确率上取得最高聚合结果；控制实验表明引导相关性和路径连贯性对增益有贡献。

Conclusion: 特权信息的表示方式（而非仅其内容）是OPSD中的一个重要设计选择，问题空间引导能更有效地传递教师知识。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by using a privileged view of a model conditioned on reference solutions to supervise a student view that observes only the question. However, the teacher-provided token-level targets may depend on reference-specific information unavailable at inference time. We propose Problem-Space-Guided OPSD (PS-OPSD), which replaces the complete solution with trajectory-grounded guidance describing the initial state, goal conditions, constraints, and a selected state-transition path. The student rollout and OPSD objective remain unchanged. Across three mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model scales ranging from 1.7B to 8B, PS-OPSD achieves the highest aggregate question-only accuracy among the compared methods. Controlled experiments further indicate that guidance relevance and path coherence contribute to these gains, highlighting the representation of privileged information as an important design choice in OPSD.

</details>


### [116] [Latent Thought Credit: Multi-Answer Credit Assignment for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01593)
*Xuyang Zhao,Liting Zhang,Zichen Xu,Yong Chen,Wenjia Zeng,Shiwan Zhao,Qicheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LTC（Latent Thought Credit），一种用于潜在推理（latent reasoning）的层次化信用分配框架。通过为每个提示采样多个潜在想法、固定上下文并基于多答案估计想法级期望奖励，LTC同时优化想法阶段和答案阶段，并在数学推理与STEM选择题任务上取得最优平均准确率，消融实验表明多答案估计可降低奖励估计误差并缓解想法级信用的模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理允许语言模型在连续潜在表示中执行中间推理，而非外部化为离散思维链。然而，从仅包含答案的奖励中为潜在想法分配信用十分困难，因为单一最终答案混合了想法质量与答案采样噪声，难以有效区分二者的贡献。

Method: LTC采用层次化信用分配：对每个提示采样多个潜在想法，在每个想法后固定上下文，并通过对该固定上下文生成多个答案的奖励取平均来估计想法级期望奖励。框架使用想法级优势优化潜在想法阶段，答案级优势优化答案阶段，并引入优势加权想法匹配目标以促使策略复现高信用潜在想法。该框架以GRPO风格的在线策略训练方式实现。

Result: 在数学推理和STEM多选题任务中，LTC相比对比方法取得最佳平均准确率。消融实验及固定上下文诊断表明，多答案估计能降低奖励估计误差，并缓解模糊或不正确的想法级信用分配问题。

Conclusion: LTC通过为潜在想法提供更可靠的信用估计，有效解决了潜在推理中从稀疏答案奖励进行层次化信用分配的难题，验证了多答案估计在降低奖励噪声和改善想法级信用分配方面的有效性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning allows language models to carry out intermediate reasoning in continuous latent representations rather than fully externalizing it as discrete chains of thought. However, assigning credit to such latent thoughts from answer-only rewards is difficult: a single final answer mixes thought quality with answer-sampling noise. We propose \textbf{Latent Thought Credit (LTC)}, a hierarchical credit-assignment framework for latent reasoning. For each prompt, LTC samples multiple latent thoughts, fixes the context after each thought, and estimates thought-level expected reward by averaging rewards over multiple answers generated from that fixed context. LTC uses thought-level advantages to optimize the latent-thought phase, answer-level advantages to optimize the answer phase, and an advantage-weighted thought-matching objective that helps the policy reproduce high-credit latent thoughts. We instantiate LTC in a GRPO-style on-policy training framework and evaluate it across mathematical reasoning and STEM multiple-choice tasks. LTC achieves the best average accuracy among the compared methods, while ablations and fixed-context diagnostics show that multi-answer estimation reduces reward-estimation error and mitigates ambiguous or incorrect thought-level credit.

</details>


### [117] [Post-Training on Office Work Improves Software Engineering: A Behavioral Account of Cross-Domain Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01604)
*Logan Ritchie,Sushant Mehta,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"目标导向执行"(GDE)概念——即代理在长时程、嵌套和分支任务中反复展现的四种行为（目标选择、任务相关状态构建、对高层目标的保真、对环境验证完成）。通过在363个长时程多工具代理(LHMTA)任务上微调Qwen3.5-122B-A10B，发现即使训练数据不含软件工程任务，模型在SWE-Bench Pro上的pass@1仍提升5.8个百分点，且匹配轨迹分析显示所有四种GDE行为在两个领域均有提升，支持长时程后训练产生跨领域迁移的行为学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务需要代理在嵌套和分支工作流中维持连贯的状态与目标，但现有工作缺乏对这一能力的行为学分解。作者提出GDE框架以刻画这一能力，并希望验证长时程后训练是否能跨领域强化这些行为。

Method: 定义GDE为四种反复行为的组合：目标选择、任务相关状态构建、对高层目标的保真维护、对环境完成性验证。在363个源自办公工作流的LHMTA任务上后训练Qwen3.5-122B-A10B，并通过匹配轨迹分析对比四种GDE行为的表现，同时用SWE-Bench Pro作为跨域评估基准。

Result: 模型在SWE-Bench Pro上的pass@1提升5.8个点，尽管训练数据不含软件工程任务。匹配轨迹分析显示所有四种GDE行为在办公工作流和软件仓库两个领域均有增益。SWE-Bench Pro的聚合统计也显示信息收集、实现和验证相关行为发生了相应变化。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种行为学解释：长时程后训练改变了模型跨任务组织和应用知识的方式，其效应超出了训练领域范围。GDE提供了一个可用于理解和评估长时程代理能力的行为框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require agents to maintain coherent state and goals across nested and branching work. We call this capability goal-directed execution (GDE): the repeated application of four behaviors, namely selecting goals, constructing task-relevant state, maintaining fidelity to higher-level objectives, and verifying completion against the environment. We hypothesize that long-horizon post-training strengthens these behaviors across domains. We test this by post-training Qwen3.5-122B-A10B on 363 Long-Horizon Multi-Tool Agent (LHMTA) tasks drawn from office workflows. The collection contained no software-engineering tasks, yet the model's pass@1 improved by 5.8 points on SWE-Bench Pro. Matched trajectory analysis shows gains in all four GDE behaviors in both office workflows and software repositories. Aggregate SWE-Bench Pro statistics showed related changes in information gathering, implementation, and verification. Together, the results support a behavioral interpretation in which long-horizon post-training changed how the model organized and applied knowledge across tasks, with effects extending beyond the training domain.

</details>


### [118] [When Memory Updates but Behavior Does Not: Repairing Implicit Stale Dependencies in Personalized Agent Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01619)
*Haofei Sun,Lin He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateAuditor，一种从存储状态到草稿方向审计的记忆增强智能体方法，通过LLM生成候选状态转换并由确定性代码验证引用来源和时间顺序，仅允许验证通过的转换触发修复。在STALE基准上相比冻结前代获得+5.0点VTA增益（0.736 vs 0.686），主要来自隐式策略适应（IPA）和前提抵抗（PR）；在独立跨家族偏好演化基准上获得有限外部增益，匹配对照实验表明增益主要来自草稿侧审计而非转换机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强智能体存在隐式策略适应（IPA）缺口：智能体即使知道用户的存储状态已过时，仍会围绕旧值进行规划。作者识别出一个结构性成因：草案锚定验证（draft-anchored verification）只检查回复内容，而在开放式回复中过时的依赖关系通常未被言明，因此需要反向审计。

Method: StateAuditor采用反向审计方向（从存储状态到草案）。LLM根据带时间戳的证据提出候选的"旧→新"转换；确定性代码将每条引用锁定到单一条目，检查新证据确实更新，且仅允许验证通过的转换触发修复。验证的对象是溯源和时间顺序，而非语义取代。完整流水线为草案-审计-修复（draft-audit-repair）。

Result: 在STALE完整协议（400个场景、50会话历史、每次查询独立响应）上，严格单查询VTA得分为0.736，对比冻结前代的0.686，配对增益+5.0点（95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]），几乎全部来自IPA和PR。第三方模型族的基准评判复现该增益（0.738 vs 0.680）。在HorizonBench上提升当前偏好准确率（用户聚类p<.01），但匹配对照显示大部分外部增益来自草稿侧审计本身；更难的作者生命周期集无增益。STALE上的匹配对照仅得0.692（+0.6，不显著），归因增益来自转换机制而非额外上下文或调用。

Conclusion: StateAuditor通过从存储到草案的反向审计和确定性验证的转换机制，有效弥合了隐式策略适应缺口，在STALE上取得显著且可复现的增益。匹配对照实验将增益精准归因于转换机制本身。但作者不声称该方法适用于通用智能体记忆，外部泛化增益有限，需限定主张范围。

Abstract: Memory-augmented agents can know that a user's stored state is outdated and still plan around the old value. The STALE benchmark calls this the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap. We identify one structural contributor: draft-anchored verification checks what a response says, and in an open-ended response the stale dependency is usually unsaid. StateAuditor therefore audits in the opposite direction, from stored state to draft. An LLM proposes candidate old-to-new transitions from timestamped evidence; deterministic code pins each quotation to a single entry, checks that the new evidence really is newer, and lets only these verified transitions trigger repair. What is verified is provenance and chronology - not semantic supersession. On STALE's full protocol (400 scenarios, 50-session histories, one independent response per query), strict single-query VTA scores .736 against .686 for our locked predecessor under the same judge: a +5.0-point paired gain (95% CI [+2.9, +7.2]) coming almost entirely from IPA and premise resistance (PR). The benchmark's own judge, from a third model family, reproduces the gain (.738 vs. .680). On an independent cross-family preference-evolution benchmark (HorizonBench), the full draft-audit-repair pipeline over a gold-derived structured store raises current-preference accuracy (user-clustered p<.01), though a matched control shows most of this external gain is the draft-side audit itself; a harder authored lifecycle set gives no gain, bounding the claim while false invalidation stays controlled. On STALE, by contrast, a matched control (same evidence, adapter, and call budget) scores only .692 (+0.6 over the predecessor, n.s.), attributing the STALE gain to the transition machinery rather than added context or calls. We make no claim about general-purpose agent memory.

</details>


### [119] [Salami Attack: Stealthy Collusive Memory Poisoning against OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01637)
*Zheng Lin,Yuzhe Huang,Zhenxing Niu,Xianmin Ye,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemCollusion，一个用于构造共谋式记忆投毒攻击的自动化红队框架。它利用"切片策略"将对抗目标分解为多个看似无害的记忆片段，这些片段单独无害但组合起来会产生有害行为。研究还开发了MoltLab评估环境，在OpenClaw上基于两种骨干模型在48个场景中验证了有效性，在最强的记忆节省设置下实现了81.3%的记忆保存率和75.0%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时记忆赋予LLM智能体跨会话保留有用信息的能力，同时也创造了攻击面，对手可通过投毒智能体的持久记忆来操纵其行为。现有记忆投毒攻击主要依赖单条恶意的记忆记录，忽视了组合性威胁：多条看似良性的记忆可能联合诱发不安全行为。这一研究空白促使作者提出针对组合式记忆投毒攻击的自动化框架。

Method: MemCollusion框架包含：(1)采用"切片策略"，将对抗目标分解为小块的、单独看似无害的记忆碎片；(2)通过四种设计约束来构造记忆联盟；(3)使用五种理论驱动的策略；(4)微调一个生成器来产出恶意记忆碎片。评估方面，开发了MoltLab——Moltbook的受控研究复现，模拟真实跨会话场景，要求精心构造的平台内容先被观察并提炼为持久记忆，再在另一会话中影响智能体行为。

Result: 在OpenClaw上使用两种骨干模型、覆盖48个场景进行评估。在最强记忆节省设置下，MemCollusion实现了81.3%的平均记忆保存率和75.0%的攻击成功率，并且在良性记忆稀释和记忆级防御下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: MemCollusion证实了组合式记忆投毒攻击的可行性，表明多个良性外表的记忆片段可以联合生成有害行为。该研究揭示了现有记忆投毒防御的一个盲区，即针对单条恶意记忆的防御无法应对多记忆协作式攻击，为LLM智能体记忆系统的安全性设计提供了新的视角和警示。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables LLM agents to retain useful information across sessions, but also creates an attack surface through which adversaries may poison an agent's persistent memory to steer its behavior. Existing memory poisoning attacks mainly rely on individually malicious records, overlooking a compositional threat: multiple benign-looking memories may jointly induce unsafe behavior. In this paper, we introduce MemCollusion, an automated red-teaming framework for constructing collusive memory poisoning attacks. MemCollusion applies salami tactics---a strategy that slices an adversarial objective into small, individually innocuous pieces---to generate memory fragments that are individually benign looking but collectively harmful. It constructs memory coalitions using four design constraints, five theory-informed strategies, and a fine-tuned generator. To assess collusive memory poisoning in a realistic cross-session setting, we develop MoltLab, a controlled research reproduction of Moltbook, in which crafted platform content must first be observed and distilled into persistent memory before influencing the agent's behavior in a separate session. We evaluate MemCollusion on OpenClaw using two backbone models across 48 scenarios. Under the strongest memory-saving setting, MemCollusion achieves an average Memory Save Rate of 81.3% and an Attack Success Rate of 75.0%, and remains effective under both benign memory dilution and memory-level defenses.

</details>


### [120] [GISAgentBench: A Practitioner-Sourced Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on GIS Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01645)
*Abhinav Pothuri,Zhe Jiang,Zelin Xu,Di Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GISAgentBench，一个包含349个多步骤GIS任务的基准测试集，任务源自GIS Stack Exchange并实例化于真实公共数据。每个任务附带可执行的参考轨迹和精确的真实输出文件，支持严格的容差感知输出匹配评估。评估六个LLM模型发现，最佳智能体在严格评分下仅完成32.7%的任务，揭示现实GIS工作流对LLM智能体仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GIS智能体基准数据集大多来自教科书、教程或LLM生成种子，规模有限且轨迹深度不足；更重要的是，没有任何现有基准提供真实输出标签，仅依赖代码相似度、轨迹匹配或LLM/VLM裁判等替代信号，这些可能混淆工作流相似性与任务正确性，无法可靠评估GIS智能体的真实能力。

Method: 从GIS Stack Exchange收集349个多步骤GIS任务，覆盖六个地理兴趣区域并在真实公共数据上实例化。每个任务配备可执行参考轨迹与精确的真实输出文件，采用严格、确定性、容差感知的输出匹配方法替代LLM裁判，并基于此评估六个LLM模型。

Result: 最佳LLM智能体在严格容差感知评分下仅完成32.7%的任务，尽管大多数模型生成的输出接近真实值，说明现实GIS工作流对当前LLM智能体仍具高度挑战性。

Conclusion: GISAgentBench填补了缺乏带真实输出标签的GIS智能体基准的空白，通过确定性输出匹配提供更可靠的评估方式。当前LLM智能体在真实GIS工作流上的表现仍不理想，该基准可作为推动该领域发展的重要评估资源。

Abstract: Geographic Information System (GIS) professionals rely on multi-step spatial analysis workflows to support decision-making in urban planning, disaster response, and environmental monitoring. The process is tedious, time-consuming, and error-prone. While recent large language model (LLM) agents equipped with external tools have the potential to automate geospatial analysis, their ability to perform realistic GIS workflows remains largely unexplored. Existing GIS agent benchmarking datasets are mostly drawn from textbooks, tutorials, or LLM-generated seeds and remain limited in size and trajectory depth. More importantly, none provides ground truth outputs. They therefore rely on surrogate signals such as code similarity, trajectory matching, or LLM and VLM judges, which can conflate workflow resemblance with task correctness. To address this gap, we introduce GISAgentBench, a benchmark of 349 multi-step GIS tasks curated from GIS Stack Exchange and instantiated on real public data across six selected geographic areas of interest. Each task ships with an executable reference trajectory and an exact ground truth output file, enabling strict, deterministic, tolerance-aware output matching beyond LLM judging. Evaluations of six LLM models reveal that realistic GIS workflows remain challenging: the best agent completes only 32.7% of tasks under strict tolerance-aware scoring, although most models produce outputs that are close to the ground truth.

</details>


### [121] [LongCat Sparse Attention: Taming the Lightning via Streaming-aware Hierarchical Cross-Layer Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01662)
*Wen Zan,Jiaqi Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Hong Liu,Cunguang Wang,Xiang Li,Duyue Ma,Guanyu Wu,Yifan Lu,Fengcun Li,Yerui Sun,Peng Pei,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongCat稀疏注意力（LSA），一种硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过三种正交策略（流式感知索引、跨层索引、分层索引）解决DeepSeek稀疏注意力（DSA）中的索引开销昂贵和硬件不友好的内存访问模式问题，支持高达百万token的原生训练，并开源了LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) 虽然通过Lightning Indexer实现了高效的长上下文建模，但其索引器存在两个系统级瓶颈：一是O(L²)的高昂评分开销，二是其输出导致的不连续内存访问模式对硬件不友好，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出三个互补且正交的策略：(1) 流式感知索引（Streaming-Aware Indexing）：将散落的KV条目转换为硬件对齐的连续布局，实现合并的HBM访问；(2) 跨层索引（Cross-Layer Indexing）：通过跨层蒸馏，复用单个层产生的索引结果到连续层，摊销索引开销；(3) 分层索引（Hierarchical Indexing）：采用从粗到细的评分方案，逐步缩小每个查询的候选集，大幅减少索引计算量。

Result: 在69B-A3B到560B-A27B的大规模扩展实验中，LSA在通用和长上下文基准上均达到与全注意力相当的性能；支持高达百万token的原生训练上下文；用于开发LongCat-2.0（1.6T-A48B）；开源了集成LSA的LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse（69B-A3B）模型。

Conclusion: LSA通过硬件-算法协同设计有效解决了DSA的系统级瓶颈，在保持与全注意力相当性能的同时显著提升了长上下文的训练和部署效率，并通过开源模型推动后续研究。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) enables efficient long-context modeling through its Lightning Indexer. However, practical deployment remains constrained by the indexer's expensive $O(L^2)$ scoring overhead and the hardware-inefficient, discontinuous memory-access patterns induced by its outputs. To address these system-level bottlenecks, we introduce LongCat Sparse Attention (LSA), a hardware-algorithm co-designed framework comprising three complementary and orthogonal strategies: (1) Streaming-Aware Indexing, which selectively converts scattered KV entries into hardware-aligned contiguous layouts to enable coalesced HBM access; (2) Cross-Layer Indexing, which amortizes indexing overhead by reusing the results produced by a single layer across consecutive layers, supported by cross-layer distillation; and (3) Hierarchical Indexing, which adopts a coarse-to-fine scoring scheme to progressively narrow the candidate set for each query, thereby substantially reducing indexing computation. Extensive scaling experiments, ranging from 69B-A3B to 560B-A27B models, demonstrate that LSA consistently achieves performance on par with full attention across both general-purpose and long-context benchmarks. Moreover, LSA supports native training with context lengths of up to one million tokens and underpins the development of LongCat-2.0 (1.6T-A48B). To facilitate further research, we also introduce and open-source LongCat-Flash-Lite-Sparse (69B-A3B), which integrates LSA into LongCat-Flash-Lite and incorporates an updated long-context training corpus.

</details>


### [122] [Allocation Before Ranking: Decoupled Token Compression for OmniLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01665)
*Zhenghui Guo,Yilin Yang,Yuanbin Man,Miao Yin,Weidong Shi,Rabimba Karanjai,Omprakash Gnawali,Chengming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Macer，一种无需训练的多模态token压缩方法，通过显式分配音频和视频预算并在各模态内进行分配归一化排序，解决了OmniLLM中共享top-K排序导致的音频偏向分配问题。在多种基准上以25%压缩率保留98.7%（Qwen2.5-Omni-7B）和97.3%（Qwen2.5-Omni-3B）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OmniLLM的token压缩被简单地建模为单一显著性排序问题（对所有多模态token打分并保留top-K）。但这种抽象是错误设定的：同一个注意力分数同时决定了两个维度——各模态获得的保留容量，以及模态内保留哪些token。共享的top-K规则因此继承了音频偏向的分配先验，在视频token有机会竞争之前，保留容量就被音频消耗。这意味着需要显式的模态预算分配而非隐式共享排序。

Method: Macer是一种无需训练（training-free）的压缩方法，其核心流程为：(1) 首先显式地为音频和视频分配各自的token预算；(2) 然后在每个模态内部执行分配归一化（allocation-normalized）的排序；(3) 在模态特定的浅层（modality-specific shallow layers）执行该压缩策略。

Result: 在25%保留率下，Macer在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上保留98.7%全量token性能，在Qwen2.5-Omni-3B上保留97.3%。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，25%设置即达到OmniZip在45%保留率时的性能，且使用更低的FLOPs。在OmniVinci-9B上，先分配后排序的原则相比共享top-K排序最高提升12.9个点。

Conclusion: Macer验证了"先分配预算、再模态内排序"的原则优于共享的单一排序策略。该方法在音频主导、音视频联合、视觉主导和视频中心等多种基准上均显著降低token成本并保持精度，证明了模态感知的预算分配是OmniLLM token压缩的更为正确的抽象。

Abstract: Token compression in OmniLLMs is typically posed as a single saliency-ranking problem: score each multimodal token, keep the top-K. We argue this abstraction is mis-specified. The same attention score simultaneously decides two things: how much retained capacity each modality receives, and which tokens within a modality are kept. A shared top-K rule therefore inherits this audio-favoring allocation prior, spending retained capacity on audio before video tokens have a chance to compete. We propose Macer, a training-free compressor that first assigns explicit audio and video budgets, then performs allocation-normalized ranking within each modality at modality-specific shallow layers. Macer significantly reduces token cost while preserving accuracy across audio-grounded, audio--video joint, visual-dominant, and video-centric benchmarks. At 25 % retention, Macer preserves 98.7 % of full-token performance on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and 97.3 % on Qwen2.5-Omni-3B. On Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, this 25 % setting reaches OmniZip-level performance at 45 % retention while using lower FLOPs. On OmniVinci-9B, the same allocation-before-ranking principle improves over shared top-K ranking by up to 12.9 points.

</details>


### [123] [TCPO: Turn-Level Credit Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01667)
*Sicong Liao,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TCPO，一种面向验证器引导的多轮强化学习的轮次级信用分配方法，通过将验证器分数转换为轮次级优势函数（包括回顾性信用、延迟信用和反事实估计），在数学推理、代码生成和智能体任务上优于或持平最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮强化学习设置中，模型每轮结束后获得一个验证器分数。虽然这些分数提供了密集的反馈（dense feedback），但并未直接提供密集的信用分配（dense credit）：分数衡量的是当前输出的质量，而信用应当衡量当前轮次如何改变后续的改进轨迹。现有方法无法有效区分哪些轮次真正推动了优化进程。

Method: TCPO将信用分配问题转化为"分数到信用"（score-to-credit）的转换，通过基于参考的对比构建三类轮次级优势：1）回顾性信用（retrospective credit）：捕获相对最佳历史状态的即时进步或退化；2）延迟信用（hindsight delayed credit）：识别当前轮次无改进但在后续产生回报的情况；3）选择性固定历史反事实估计（selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation）：对高意外度轮次在相同历史条件下进行反事实估计以细化信用。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld智能体任务上的实验表明，TCPO在不同模型规模、任务领域和验证器类型下均优于或持平最强基线。在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B上取得最佳或并列最佳的best-turn Pass@8，减少了达到成功所需的轮次数，并提升了多轮智能体性能。

Conclusion: 分数到信用的转换是验证器引导的多轮策略优化的核心要素，TCPO通过系统的信用分配方法有效提升了多轮推理和智能体任务的表现。

Abstract: Verifier-guided reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for improving LLM reasoning. In multi-turn settings, models receive a verifier score after each turn and iteratively refine their outputs. Although such scores provide dense feedback, they do not directly provide dense credit: a score measures the quality of the current output, while credit should measure how the current turn changes the refinement trajectory. We propose TCPO, a turn-level credit assignment method for verifier-guided multi-turn RL. TCPO casts credit assignment as score-to-credit conversion and constructs turn-level advantages through reference-based comparisons: retrospective credit captures immediate progress and regression relative to the best prior state; hindsight delayed credit identifies non-improving turns with later payoff; and selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation refines high-surprisal turns under the same history. Experiments on math reasoning, code generation, and AppWorld agent tasks show that TCPO improves or matches the strongest baselines across model scales, task domains, and verifier types. TCPO achieves the best or tied-best best-turn Pass@8 on Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, reduces turns to success, and improves multi-turn agent performance. These results highlight score-to-credit conversion as a central ingredient for verifier-guided multi-turn policy optimization.

</details>


### [124] [When Memory Becomes Authority: Benchmarking Authority Collapse at the Memory Consolidation Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01679)
*Qiuyang Zhan,Rui Zhang,Sheng Guo,Lepeng Zhao,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出权威崩塌（authority collapse）问题：持久化记忆系统在整合异构交互历史时，保留了信息内容但丢失了其来源权限约束，导致存储的记忆暗示超出源允许的权威。作者引入AuthMem-Bench基准，在49种配置中观察到48种存在权威崩塌，且可导致高达50.3%的未授权操作率；自动预测并持久化权限标签可将端到端未授权操作率从16.9%降至0.0%，同时保持良性任务成功率基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化记忆使LLM智能体能够跨任务自适应，但记忆整合过程隐式地设定了一道授权边界，决定存储信息后续可被用作用户事实、经验证观察或长期指令。现有系统往往只关注保留"什么"被学到，而忽视了保留"以何种权限"被复用，这可能导致存储信息被赋予超出其来源允许的权威，需要系统性地识别和量化这一问题。

Method: 作者提出AuthMem-Bench，一个受控配对基准，在保持核心主张与下游任务不变的情况下仅改变来源权威。评估覆盖三个层面：写入期崩塌检测、下游授权错误、自动权威保留。实验涵盖7种基于主流智能体记忆系统的整合器与7种LLM骨干模型，共49种配置，并进行了受控操作验证实验和端到端评估。

Result: 在49种评估配置中有48种观察到权威崩塌；受控操作验证中，无权限元数据的崩塌记忆平均未授权操作率为50.3%；端到端评估中，自动预测并持久化的权限标签将未授权操作率从16.9%降至0.0%，而良性任务成功率基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 记忆驱动的自适应不仅要保留学到了什么，还必须保留信息可被复用的权威条件。权限标签的自动预测与持久化是一种有效的缓解手段，能在不损害良性任务表现的情况下消除未授权行为。

Abstract: Persistent memory allows (self-evolving) LLM agents to adapt across tasks by consolidating heterogeneous interaction histories into reusable facts, preferences, observations, and rules. Yet consolidation also imposes an implicit authorization boundary: it determines whether stored information may later be consumed as a user fact, an attested observation, or a standing instruction. We identify authority collapse, in which consolidation preserves a claim while erasing the source constraints governing its authorized use, causing the stored memory to imply greater authority than its source permits. We introduce AuthMem-Bench, a controlled paired benchmark that holds the focal claim and downstream task fixed while varying only source authority. It evaluates write-time collapse, downstream authorization errors, and automatic authority preservation. Across seven consolidators based on widely used agent-memory systems and seven LLM backbones, we observe authority collapse in 48 of 49 evaluated configurations. In a controlled action-grounded evaluation, collapsed memories without authority metadata yield a mean unauthorized-action rate of 50.3%. In an end-to-end evaluation, automatically predicted and persisted authority labels reduce the observed unauthorized-action rate from 16.9% to 0.0%, while benign task success remains essentially unchanged. These findings show that memory-driven adaptation must preserve not only what was
  learned, but also the authority under which it may be reused.

</details>


### [125] [GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01684)
*Jiarui Tan,Zhongjian Zhang,YaBo Guo,Jiawei Liu,Yujie Xing,Muhan Zhang,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GABench，一个全面的智能体图分析基准，涵盖三种图类型、四类图分析任务（图检索、图理论、图机器学习、图开放式问答），提供84个可执行工具，并构建了10,400个具有可验证真实答案的任务。实验评估多个前沿LLM和智能体框架，发现现有智能体在复杂图分析任务上仍表现不足，框架选择显著影响性能，且图分析更依赖工具调用质量而非数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图分析基准对图任务和图类型的覆盖有限，且通常将图分析表述为基于文本的问答形式（图信息直接提供在提示中），限制了端到端智能体能力的评估。需要构建一个全面的基准来系统评估LLM智能体在图环境中的规划、工具使用和交互能力。

Method: GABench包含三个方面的设计：(1) 覆盖三类图类型和四类任务类别（图检索、图理论、图机器学习、图开放式问答）；(2) 提供84个可执行工具用于访问图数据和执行多样化图操作；(3) 构建智能体图分析任务生成管道，生成10,400个具有可验证真实答案的任务，从而支持端到端智能体能力的评测。

Result: 实验揭示了三个关键发现：(1) 现有LLM智能体仍然难以处理复杂的图分析任务；(2) 智能体框架（harness）的选择显著影响性能，但现有框架在复杂图任务上仍然有限；(3) 图分析的性能更依赖于工具调用的质量而非调用数量。

Conclusion: GABench为LLM智能体的图分析能力提供了全面且可靠的评估基准，实验结果揭示了当前智能体的不足和关键影响因素，为LLM智能体在图分析方向的发展与评估提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of planning, using tools, and interacting with external environments. They are typically supported by harnesses, which manage state and coordinate multi-step execution. Graph analysis provides a promising setting for evaluating their agentic capabilities, because it requires agents to access data and execute operations in a graph environment. However, existing graph benchmarks for LLMs provide limited coverage of graph tasks and graph types, making it difficult to comprehensively evaluate LLM agents. Moreover, they typically formulate graph analysis as text-based question answering, where graph information is directly provided in the prompt, limiting the evaluation of end-to-end agentic capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce GABench, a comprehensive benchmark for agentic graph analysis. GABench spans three graph types and covers four graph analysis task categories: graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, and graph open-ended question answering. GABench also provides 84 executable tools for accessing graph data and performing diverse graph operations. Building on these tools, we develop an agentic graph analysis task generation pipeline and construct 10,400 tasks with verifiable ground truth.Using GABench, we evaluate a range of frontier LLMs and agent harnesses. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (1) Existing LLM agents still struggle with complex graph analysis tasks. (2) Harness choice significantly affects performance, yet existing harnesses remain limited on complex graph tasks. (3) Graph analysis depends more on tool-call quality than quantity. Our findings provide practical insights into the development and evaluation of LLM agents for graph analysis.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01710)
*Jinghan Xu,Longze Fan,Zeyuan Wang,Xinjin Li,Hankai Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了工具型大语言模型智能体中的"语义重放"（semantic replay）安全问题：单一用户授权可被多次请求和执行（即使每次令牌是单次使用的），并提出CapLease——一个授权消费层，通过绑定确认与规范动作、强制事务性Issue-Prepare-Commit转换以及持久单调状态机，来防止重复准入和重复外部效应，证明durable authorization state而非token表示才是防重放系统需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体在工具使用中频繁出现重新规划、重试、委派、并发和崩溃恢复等行为，这些行为会导致同一用户授权在全新的令牌标识符下被多次请求和执行。现有"标识符局域化"（identifier-local）的单次使用令牌机制无法阻止新鲜令牌的重新签发，从而产生超出授权执行预算的"语义重放"漏洞。

Method: 提出CapLease授权消费层，采用三层设计：(1) proposal-和authority-级别防御；(2) 将经过认证的用户确认绑定到规范动作（canonical action）；(3) 强制执行事务性的Issue-Prepare-Commit状态转换。系统维护关于授权动作、确认事件和剩余执行预算的单调持久状态，防止相同语义内容以新令牌形式再次执行。

Result: 在LLM智能体的重新规划、重试、委派、并发、确认重放和崩溃恢复场景中，identifier-local令牌均允许新鲜语义重签发；而CapLease和同样有状态的Server Ledger能够阻止重复准入，配合幂等sink可阻止重复外部效应。实验验证了durable authorization state相较于token表示本身的优越性。

Conclusion: 防重放的智能体执行的关键系统需求是持久的授权状态（durable authorization state），而非令牌表示形式（token representation）本身。任何仅依赖单次使用令牌标识符的方案都无法从根本上防止语义重放，必须引入跨令牌实例的单调持久状态追踪。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model agents frequently replan, retry failed operations, delegate tasks, and resume after crashes. These behaviors can cause one user authorization to be requested and executed multiple times under freshly issued token identifiers, even when each individual token is single-use. We call this failure semantic replay: exceeding the execution budget of a token-independent authorization instance rather than merely reusing an old token identifier. We show that identifier-local token consumption cannot prevent fresh reissuance unless the issuer retains monotonic durable state over the authorized action, confirmation event, and remaining execution budget. We introduce CapLease, an authorization-consumption layer that follows proposal- and authority-level defenses, binds an authenticated user confirmation to a canonical action, and enforces transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit transitions. Across LLM-agent replanning, retry, delegation, concurrency, confirmation-replay, and crash-recovery scenarios, identifier-local tokens permit fresh semantic reissuance, whereas CapLease and an equally stateful Server Ledger prevent duplicate admission and, with an idempotent sink, duplicate external effects. Our results identify durable authorization state, rather than token representation alone, as the systems requirement for replay-resistant agent execution.

</details>


### [127] [Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01711)
*Lingbo Li,Anuradha Mathrani,Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可执行分析知识表示（EAKR），一种将结构化证据转化为可执行元分析所需的机器可行动的知识表示框架，并基于此构建MetaSynDec代理系统。在58个合成单元中，57个成功执行统计计算，75%实现精确证据集一致，置信区间重叠率达98.2%，显著优于直接大语言模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识驱动的科学分析中，结构化证据本身并不包含可执行计算所需的分析知识——证据分配、分析对比、结局和时间点对齐、效应量公式、方法学可接受性等决策必须在统计执行前显式化。现有自动化方法通常将这些决策嵌入模型输出、生成代码或工作流痕迹中，而非表示为可独立验证的知识，导致缺乏形式化验证与可追溯性。

Method: 提出EAKR（Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation），一种机器可行动的知识表示，涵盖证据、关系、数值输入、约束、来源和未解决问题。将其操作化为MetaSynDec，一个代理式框架：大语言模型提出结构化更新，确定性服务负责基于模式和合约的验证与执行。

Result: 在58个合成单元中，MetaSynDec构建了全部EAKR，57个进入统计执行。56个具有足够信息定义参考分析对象的单元中，38个（67.9%）实现完全对象保真度，42个（75.0%）实现精确证据集一致，平均Jaccard相似度为0.909。55个单元中54个（98.2%）的生成置信区间与已发表区间重叠。在参考综合结构一致性方面，MetaSynDec优于直接LLM生成（57/58对比23/58，p<0.001）；在23个共同完成的单元中，精确参考公式一致性为23/23对比1/23（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 研究提供了可行性证据，表明EAKR能够支持形式化验证、可追溯性、统计执行，并在方法学一致性方面优于直接LLM生成，代表了知识驱动的科学分析中可验证知识表示的重要进展。

Abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [128] [LaCache: Robust Semantic Caching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01718)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LaCache，一种通过额外检查查询前k个推测解码token的缓存命中来抵御缓存碰撞攻击的新型语义缓存方案，在提供正式安全保障的同时还提升了缓存检索的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存方案基于嵌入相似性复用响应，但本质上容易受到缓存碰撞攻击——攻击者通过注入精心构造的查询污染缓存，从而破坏后续合法请求的响应。已有方案对这一安全漏洞缺乏有效防御。

Method: LaCache的核心洞察是：攻击者能完全控制恶意查询本身，但对其响应控制力有限，因为响应必须同时满足多个语义约束。LaCache不仅检查查询的缓存命中，还检查其前k个（推测性）解码token的缓存命中。这种双重检查设计使恶意查询无法同时触发恶意响应又与良性查询发生碰撞。此外，增强的索引提供了额外的语义上下文，改善了缓存检索的相关性。

Result: 作者从理论上证明了LaCache对缓存碰撞攻击具有形式化的弹性保证，即不可能构造既能引发恶意响应又能与良性查询碰撞的对抗性查询。实验在多种LLM和基准测试上验证了其安全性和效率优势。

Conclusion: LaCache为鲁棒的语义缓存提供了有前景的方向，在安全性和效率之间取得了良好的平衡，有望成为LLM服务中语义缓存的实用安全方案。

Abstract: Semantic caching, which reuses responses to semantically similar requests via their embeddings, has seen growing adoption in LLM serving, offering faster responses and reduced costs. Yet existing schemes are fundamentally vulnerable to cache-collision attacks, wherein an adversary pollutes the cache by injecting crafted queries, corrupting responses to subsequent legitimate requests. We present LaCache, a novel semantic caching scheme that addresses this vulnerability through a conceptually simple yet principled redesign. The key insight is that while the adversary has full control over the adversarial query, it has far less control over its response, which must simultaneously satisfy multiple semantic constraints. Rather than checking only the cache hit of a query, LaCache additionally checks the cache hit of its first k (speculatively) decoded tokens. This design yields two concrete benefits. First, it provides formally guaranteed resilience against cache-collision attacks: we prove that it is impossible to craft adversarial queries that simultaneously elicit malicious responses and collide with benign queries. Second, the enriched index supplies additional semantic context for cache retrieval, improving response relevance. Empirical evaluation across diverse LLMs and benchmarks validates both LaCache's security guarantees and efficiency gains, pointing to a promising direction for robust semantic caching.

</details>


### [129] [DAPD: Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01735)
*Jianyu Wu,Yizhou Wang,Encheng Su,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD)框架，通过双路径锚定和双源锚定来解决on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）中的"特权幻觉"问题，即学生模型学习了依赖训练时特权信息的不可复现行为。实验表明DAPD在Qwen3-4B上平均提升+2.00点，且在4B和32B规模下分别达到+2.69和+2.78的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD通过在训练时引入特权信息来增强教师模型，但会产生"特权幻觉"：学生模型学习到依赖训练时特权信息的、无法在推理时复现的行为，却表现得仿佛特权信息仍然可用，最终导致性能下降。作者识别出这一失败的根本原因是特权教师与推理时学生之间存在信息不对称。

Method: 提出DAPD统一框架，包含两个层面的锚定：(1) 双路径锚定（DPA）引入自条件桥接，在两条信息匹配的路径上对齐参考和rollout行为，防止特权依赖行为被传递到推理时的学生；(2) 双源锚定（DSA）在reference-to-rollout和rollout-to-reference两个方向上应用这些路径，减少对特权参考指导的依赖同时保留正确性监督。

Result: 大量实验表明DAPD能显著缓解特权幻觉，在Qwen3-4B上平均超越OPSD达+2.00点。增益在不同规模上持续存在：4B规模达到+2.69，32B规模达到+2.78。

Conclusion: DAPD通过解决教师-学生之间的信息不对称问题，有效缓解了OPSD中的特权幻觉现象，且其性能增益在多个模型规模上均保持稳定，为语言模型后训练中的自蒸馏方法提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: On-policy (self) distillation (OPSD) is increasingly adopted for language-model post-training. It strengthens the teacher with privileged information but can induce a privilege illusion: the student learns privilege-dependent behavior it cannot reproduce from its inference-time context, yet behaves as if the training-time privileged information remained available, ultimately degrading performance. In this paper, we identify information asymmetry between the privileged teacher and the student at inference as the root cause of this failure in OPSD. To resolve this asymmetry, we propose Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD), a unified framework with two levels of anchoring. Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) introduces a self-conditioned bridge and aligns reference and rollout behavior along two matched-information paths, preventing privilege-dependent behavior from being transferred to the inference-time student. Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA) applies these paths in both reference-to-rollout and rollout-to-reference directions, reducing reliance on privileged reference guidance while preserving correctness supervision. Extensive experiments show that DAPD significantly alleviates privilege illusion, outperforming OPSD on Qwen3-4B by +2.00 points on average across tasks. Notably, its gains persist across scales, reaching +2.69 at 4B and +2.78 at 32B.

</details>


### [130] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEvo-Mem是一个闭环框架，通过交替更新检索策略（轻量级残差路由器在线修正冻结LLM生成的路由先验）和记忆库（轨迹条件化反馈更新记忆值与图关系），实现检索与记忆的共同演化，在七个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期LLM代理方法将查询特定检索与持续记忆精炼分开优化，要么优化记忆访问（如迭代查询精炼或自适应检索策略），要么优化记忆演化（如结构化更新），忽视了检索与记忆之间的基本反馈回路：检索决定哪些记忆获得使用信号，而更新的记忆库又会重塑未来的检索结果。

Method: CoEvo-Mem采用闭环框架：(1) 对每个查询，冻结LLM生成路由特定的查询改写和路由先验，由轻量级残差路由器在线修正；(2) 检索到的上下文作为两个学习过程的耦合接口，任务结果将报酬分配给路由决策，轨迹条件化反馈更新记忆值和图关系；(3) 通过交替优化——固定记忆库更新路由器，固定检索策略演化记忆库——来缓解耦合导致的非平稳性问题。

Result: 在七个多样化基准上，CoEvo-Mem实现了最先进的性能，验证了检索-记忆共同演化的有效性。

Conclusion: 检索策略与记忆库的共同演化对长期LLM代理的性能至关重要，闭合反馈回路的端到端协同优化优于以往分离优化的方法。

Abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations. These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed. Across seven diverse benchmarks, \textbf{CoEvo-Mem} achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the importance of retrieval-memory coevolution.

</details>


### [131] [MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01742)
*YuFei Luo,Xiucheng Xu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemSIF（Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts）框架，通过结构化交互记忆（Structured Interaction Memory）和双轨事实记忆（Dual-Track Fact Memory）来解决LLM智能体长期交互中存在的时序-结构错位（TSM）和延迟效用显现（DUM）两个核心问题。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S两个基准上、跨五个骨干LLM的实验中，MemSIF在所有设置下均取得最高Total ACC，较最强基线在LoCoMo上提升2.29%-8.79%，在LongMemEval-S上提升2.87%-6.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程交互中依赖长期记忆，但现有记忆系统存在两个反复出现的错位模式：（1）TSM——时间邻近性无法可靠地与主题或事件级相关性对齐；（2）DUM——写入时的显著性无法可靠地预测未来查询的效用。这两个问题严重限制了记忆系统的有效性和智能体在长时程任务中的表现。

Method: MemSIF包含两大核心组件：（1）Structured Interaction Memory：将原始交互组织为Topical Segments（保持局部主题连贯性）和Event Trajectories（维持跨时间的事件连续性）；（2）Dual-Track Fact Memory：包含CoreFact记忆（在写入时整合稳定的、由模式指导的信息）和ActiveFact记忆（按需生成事实，并提升那些被多个历史来源支持且具有反复查询需求的事实的复用优先级）。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S两个基准上，跨五个骨干LLM（包括GPT系列等）的实验中，MemSIF在所有设置下均取得最高的Total ACC。相比最强基线，在LoCoMo上提升2.29%-8.79%，在LongMemEval-S上提升2.87%-6.15%。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了将Structured Interaction Memory与Dual-Track Fact Memory相结合能有效缓解TSM和DUM问题，显著提升LLM智能体的长期记忆能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-term memory is critical for LLM agents operating over long-horizon interactions. However, several persistent limitations of existing memory systems can be traced to two recurring misalignment patterns in long-term interaction settings: Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM). TSM arises when temporal proximity does not reliably align with topical or event-level relatedness, whereas DUM arises when write-time salience does not reliably predict future query utility. To mitigate these misalignment patterns, we propose MemSIF (Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts), a structured interaction-to-fact memory framework. Structured Interaction Memory organizes raw interactions into Topical Segments that preserve local topical coherence and Event Trajectories that maintain cross-time event continuity. Dual-Track Fact Memory uses two complementary tracks: CoreFact memory consolidates stable, schema-guided information at write time, whereas ActiveFact memory forms facts on demand and promotes those supported by multiple historical sources and recurring query demand for reuse. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs show that MemSIF achieves the highest Total ACC in all settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.29%-8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%-6.15% on LongMemEval-S. These results support the effectiveness of combining Structured Interaction Memory with Dual-Track Fact Memory to mitigate TSM and DUM. Code is available at https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF.

</details>


### [132] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-Lock，一个基于强化学习的框架，用于生成互锁装配体（interlocking assemblies），无需手工设计的搜索启发式规则。该框架结合结构化动作分块与MCTS引导的策略-价值学习，在复杂的组合搜索空间中高效求解3D体素网格的互锁分解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁装配体仅通过几何排列连接部件、无需外部连接件，在现实中有广泛应用。现有方法将互锁装配体生成建模为形状分解问题，但依赖手工设计的搜索启发式，在处理复杂情况时耗时长甚至失败。作者观察到该问题本质上是一个序列决策问题，因此引入强化学习来解决。

Method: RL-Lock将互锁装配体生成建模为序列决策问题，其中代理反复决定每个体素应分配给哪个部件。框架采用结构化动作分块（structured action chunking）来减少决策空间，并结合蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）引导的策略-价值学习来高效探索庞大的组合搜索空间。

Result: 实验表明RL-Lock能够有效生成互锁装配体，尤其对于现有方法耗时过长甚至无法找到有效解的挑战性案例，RL-Lock表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: RL-Lock是首个不依赖手工搜索启发式的互锁装配体生成强化学习框架，为后续相关研究提供了新的范式。

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [133] [Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for Verifiable Reasoning in Autonomous Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01755)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Kaixuan Wang,Guli Zhang,Hongyan Xie,Yakun Zhu,Hao Geng,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA自动驾驶模型中常见的CoT标注流程会让教师模型"看到"真实轨迹，导致轨迹锚定偏差（trajectory anchoring bias），使CoT缺乏因果性且幻觉更严重。为此提出AD-MCQ（将规划转化为轨迹候选的多项选择）和DEFT-RLVR（将未来轨迹从决策前锚点转为决策后验证目标），在不需开放生成轨迹的前提下实现可验证的轨迹级驾驶决策，实验证明该方法提升驾驶推理能力且不损害甚至增强通用视觉能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型使用CoT监督来提升推理能力，但标注流程将真实未来轨迹暴露给教师模型，导致教师模型"事后合理化"而非基于场景证据推理，产生因果不忠实的CoTs和更严重的幻觉。需要消除这一捷径，同时避免开放式轨迹生成带来的高维纠缠（决策推理与几何合成、低层动力学的耦合）。

Method: 1) AD-MCQ：将规划任务重构为在显式轨迹候选中进行选择的多项选择题，使轨迹级决策可验证且无需开放生成；2) DEFT-RLVR：通过"延迟暴露未来轨迹"机制，将未来轨迹从决策前锚点转变为决策后的验证目标，配合RLVR（基于规则的验证）框架。

Result: 实验表明DEFT-RLVR在提升AD推理能力的同时保持甚至增强通用视觉能力。AD-MCQ支持VLM-only推理，且可通过候选构造控制难度，具有灵活性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: AD-MCQ为可验证的AD推理提供了灵活、可扩展、可延伸的研究基础，通过消除轨迹锚定偏差和避免开放轨迹生成，实现了更可信的自动驾驶推理。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD) increasingly utilize chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision to enhance the reasoning capabilities of their Vision-Language Model (VLM) components, yet existing annotation pipelines commonly expose the teacher model to the logged ground-truth (GT) future trajectory. We empirically show that this induces trajectory anchoring bias: teacher models rationalize the revealed outcome rather than infer a decision from scene evidence, producing less causally faithful CoTs and substantially more severe hallucinations, especially in causally challenging scenes. Removing the GT trajectory eliminates this shortcut, but open-ended trajectory generation entangles high-level decision-making with precise geometric synthesis and low-level dynamics. To make trajectory-level driving decisions verifiable without requiring open-ended trajectory synthesis, we introduce Autonomous-Driving Multiple-Choice Question (AD-MCQ), which casts planning as selection among explicit trajectory candidates. Taking this a step further, we propose Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for RLVR (DEFT-RLVR) to transform future trajectories from pre-decision anchors into post-decision verification targets. Experimental results show that DEFT-RLVR improves AD reasoning while preserving or even enhancing general visual capabilities. With VLM-only inference and controllable difficulty through candidate construction, AD-MCQ provides a flexible, scalable, and extensible foundation for future research on verifiable AD reasoning.

</details>


### [134] [Leveraging AI for fine-grained food safety risk forecasting in sparse data conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01767)
*Dongqi Wang,Weiwei Chen,Han Zhou,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的框架，通过整合超过1100万条食品检查记录与人口、经济、环境等辅助指标，实现对城市级食品安全的细粒度风险预测。采用三阶段预训练设计（结合Wilson区间部分监督和半监督标签精炼），在2022年数据上显著优于基线，并在浙江省市场监督管理局的实地实验中提升了检出率和检查资源配置效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品安全是关键的公共卫生挑战，尤其在检查资源有限、区域采样数据稀疏的情况下，传统方法难以准确识别细粒度的食品安全风险。现有方法依赖反应性措施，缺乏能够提前、精准识别风险的数据驱动框架。

Method: 提出Transformer-based框架，统一超过1100万条检查记录，并融合来自统计年鉴的人口、经济和环境指标。采用三阶段预训练设计：(1)利用Wilson区间计算的安全与风险排名提供部分监督；(2)结合半监督标签精炼机制；(3)充分利用历史记录，即使在本地样本量不足的情况下也能有效训练。

Result: 在2022年数据上的实验评估中，该方法显著优于各基线方法。与浙江省市场监督管理局合作的实地实验表明，相比人工制定的检查计划，该方法实现了更高的检出率和更高效的检查资源配置。

Conclusion: 大规模公共检查数据、Wilson区间置信度建模与先进深度学习的严格整合，可以促进更早期、更细粒度的食品安全威胁识别，减少对反应性措施的依赖，推动全球食品供应链的前瞻性数据驱动监管。

Abstract: Ensuring food safety represents a critical public health challenge, particularly when inspection resources are limited and regional sampling data are sparse. This study proposes a Transformer-based framework capable of forecasting fine-grained, city-level food safety risks by unifying over 11 million inspection records with supplemental demographic, economic, and environmental indicators extracted from the Statistical Yearbook. A three-stage pretraining design leverages partial supervision from the Wilson interval (capturing both safety and risk rankings), together with semi-supervised label refinement, to effectively utilize historical records even when local sample sizes are insufficient. Experimental evaluations on data from 2022 show that the proposed approach outperforms baselines significantly. A subsequent field experiment in collaboration with the Zhejiang Provincial Administration for Market Regulation further demonstrates improved detection rates and more efficient allocation of inspection resources compared to a manually developed plan. Observations of regulatory decision-making reveal a threshold-based heuristic employed by inspectors, hinting that additional training or decision-support interfaces could further enhance the impact of AI-generated risk scores. Overall, these findings underscore that a rigorous integration of large-scale public inspection data, Wilson interval-based confidence modeling, and advanced deep learning can facilitate earlier and more granular identification of food safety threats. By reducing reliance on reactive measures alone, the proposed framework has the potential to advance proactive, data-driven oversight of the global food supply.

</details>


### [135] [FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01772)
*Xuhui Wang,Ruoqi Shu,Chen Dan,Tianhua Xu,Mengxi Luo,Yanming Mai,Bo Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FRAMES是一个闭环框架，从现有资产冷启动可部署技能，通过共识变异、基于准确率和成本的Pareto选择以及防回归保证来持续演化技能，同时保持可审计性。在内部生产系统上取得了最优的准确率-成本权衡，且在tau-bench上复现了相同收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地运行受策略约束的企业工作流（如文档审计），需要一致地应用规则、验证每个值并保持可审计性。改进这些智能体面临三大难题：运营反馈稀疏且无标签、修改一条规则可能使无关案例回退、准确率提升不能以推理成本增加或丧失可审计性为代价。

Method: FRAMES采用闭环框架：先从现有资产冷启动可部署的技能，然后通过共识变异（consensus-based mutation）演化技能，结合准确率和成本的双目标Pareto选择，并引入防回归保证机制，全过程保持可审计性。

Result: 在内部生产系统部署中，FRAMES在准确率-成本权衡上优于所有基线方法，并在tau-bench基准上复现了相同的性能提升，验证了框架的泛化能力。

Conclusion: FRAMES证明了在稀疏且无标签的运营反馈条件下，通过闭环的技能冷启动与变异演化框架，可以在保持低推理成本和可审计性的前提下显著提升策略约束型LLM智能体的准确率，为生产环境中的智能体持续改进提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly run policy-bound enterprise workflows such as document auditing, where they must apply rules consistently, ground every value, and stay auditable. Improving these agents is hard: operational feedback is sparse and unlabeled, edits to one rule can regress unrelated cases, and accuracy must improve without inflating inference cost or losing auditability. We present FRAMES, a closed-loop framework that cold-starts deployable skills from existing assets and then evolves them through consensus-based mutation, Pareto selection over accuracy and cost, and an anti-regression guarantee, all while preserving auditability. Deployed on our internal production system, FRAMES attains the best accuracy-cost trade-off among baselines, with the same gains reproduced on tau-bench.

</details>


### [136] [REFLEX: Rethinking MoE Inference as Refinement-Aware Compute Allocation in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01784)
*Xiang Xia,Cheng Yan,Yiming Zhang,Jiazheng Liu,Hongyu Zhang,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REFLEX是一种无需训练的专家分配方法，针对扩散语言模型中的MoE推理，根据token的细化需求灵活分配专家计算资源，在平均减少15%专家计算的同时保持甚至提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）中，每个去噪前向过程会共同处理所有token位置，但不同token的细化需求差异显著。然而默认的固定token选择路由为每个token分配统一的专家预算，导致专家计算与细化需求不匹配，形成计算资源浪费。

Method: REFLEX是一种无需训练的方法，保持默认路由器不变，围绕细化过程重组专家计算：(1) 引入粗到细的层次结构进行专家预算分配，使计算与块相对细化角色对齐；(2) 使用Frontier-Progress Score解决活动块优先级问题。

Result: 在LLaDA-MoE和LLaDA2.0-mini两个代表性MoE扩散语言模型及多个广泛使用的基准上，REFLEX平均减少15%的专家计算分配，同时在大多数基准上保持或提升生成质量。与自回归式可变专家路由方法相比，REFLEX产生更一致的"质量-计算"权衡。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型中的MoE推理应被视为跨异构token细化状态的"细化感知计算分配"问题，而非简单的固定token选择路由。根据每次去噪前向中暴露的异构细化需求来分配专家计算至关重要。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models increase parameter capacity by activating only a small subset of experts for each token. This conditional-computation paradigm has enabled autoregressive language models to scale model capacity without a proportional increase in per-token computation. In diffusion language models (DLMs), however, each denoising forward jointly revisits all token positions despite their sharply different refinement demands, while the default fixed token-choice routing assigns them a uniform expert budget, creating a mismatch between expert computation and refinement demand. We argue that MoE inference in DLMs should therefore be viewed as refinement-aware compute allocation across heterogeneous token refinement states. We propose REFLEX (\textbf{RE}finement-aware \textbf{FLEX}ible expert allocation), a training-free method that keeps the default router unchanged while reorganizing expert computation around the evolving refinement process. Specifically, REFLEX introduces a coarse-to-fine hierarchy for expert-budget allocation that aligns computation with block-relative refinement roles while using the Frontier-Progress Score to resolve active-block priorities. Across multiple widely used benchmarks on two representative MoE-based DLMs, LLaDA-MoE and LLaDA2.0-mini, REFLEX reduces allocated expert computation by 15\% on average while preserving or even improving generation quality on most benchmarks relative to default routing. Compared with autoregressive-style variable-expert routing methods, REFLEX also yields a more consistent quality--computation trade-off, further supporting the importance of allocating expert computation according to the heterogeneous refinement demands exposed within each denoising forward.

</details>


### [137] [Can You Trust the Confidence? ConfBench for Vision-Language Models on Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01792)
*Priyashree Roy,Sujitha Martin,Mohammad Rostami,Spencer Romo,Renhao Xue,Bob Strahan,Diego A. Socolinsky,Boyi Xie,Md Mofijul Islam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ConfBench，这是首个专门针对关键信息抽取（KIE）任务的校准评估基准。通过20种受控退化流程生成1,346个文档变体和70K+实体级评估，覆盖完整精度谱系。评估了四种专有和三种开源VLM，在三种输入模态下使用语言化和对数概率置信度估计方法，发现OCR+图像模态置信度更准确、模型能力是置信度质量的主导因素、校准质量因模型而异，并引入ECARB运营预算指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档基准以清洁高质量样本为主，低精度区域样本稀疏，无法有效评估模型置信度校准效果。而IDP系统依赖置信度分数来在自动化与人工审核之间路由提取任务，因此需要一个专门针对校准评估的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建ConfBench：对多样化文档集施加20种受控退化流程，生成1,346个变体和70K+实体级评估，覆盖完整精度谱系。评估7个VLM（4个专有+3个开源），采用语言化（verbalized）和对数概率（log-probability）两种置信度估计方法，三种输入模态（OCR、图像等），并引入ECARB作为基于审核预算的运营指标。

Result: 主要发现：(i) OCR+图像模态产生更准确的置信度估计；(ii) 模型能力是主导因素——Claude家族内置信度质量随能力单调提升，而跨家族参数数量不能预测置信度质量；(iii) 校准质量从接近完美到严重过度自信差异巨大，逐模型的事后校正可将绝对置信度值重新缩放以用于阈值路由，而不改变基于排序的运营指标；(iv) 对数概率方法中，首token聚合优于均值token和边际聚合。

Conclusion: ConfBench作为首个KIE校准专用基准，为系统性研究置信度估计器和校准方法提供了公共平台，ECARB指标则量化了判别力提升在运营层面的实际节省，有助于推动可信赖的IDP应用部署。

Abstract: Intelligent document processing (IDP) with vision-language models (VLMs) hinges on confidence scores trustworthy enough to route extractions between automation and human review. Existing document benchmarks are dominated by clean, high-quality samples, leaving low accuracy regions too sparse for calibration assessment. We introduce ConfBench, the first calibration-specific benchmark for key information extraction (KIE), built by applying 20 controlled degradation pipelines to a diverse document set, yielding 1,346 variants and 70K+ entity-level evaluations spanning the full accuracy spectrum. We evaluate four proprietary and three open-weight VLMs under verbalized and log-probability confidence estimation methods across three input modalities, and find: (i) OCR+Image modality results in more accurate confidence estimates; (ii) model capability is the dominant factor: within the Claude family confidence quality scales monotonically with capability, while across families parameter count is a poor predictor; (iii) calibration quality varies widely across models, from near-perfect to severely overconfident, and per-model post-hoc correction rescales these absolute confidence values for threshold-based routing without altering ranking-based operational metrics; and (iv) log-probability with first-token aggregation consistently outperforms mean-token and margin aggregations. We also introduce ECARB, a review-budget metric translating discriminative gains into operational savings. We release ConfBench publicly to enable systematic study of confidence estimators and calibration methods for trustworthy IDP application deployment.

</details>


### [138] [CoNav-UAV: Cooperative Dual-Altitude Aerial Navigation via Stackelberg Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01802)
*Junru Song,Wenhao Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuekai Qiu,Feifei Wang,Weien Zhou,Tingsong Jiang,Ying Wen,Yang Li,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoNav-UAV提出了一种用于空中平台目标导向视觉语言导航的双无人机Stackelberg博弈框架，通过迭代Stackelberg学习使高空领航员和低空跟随者协同适应，在不依赖外部辅助信息的情况下显著优于现有单双智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地面VLN方法直接迁移到低空无人机时探索效率低下且依赖外部辅助；已有的双无人机方案虽然尝试互补但依赖特权信息且独立训练两个智能体，缺乏相互适应。任务同时需要全局探索、目标匹配和无碰撞的近距离接近，这两个互锁过程难以在单一智能体中调和。

Method: 将双无人机目标导航任务建模为Stackelberg博弈：高空领导者负责高层视觉语言推理（通过基于记忆的上下文学习优化），低空跟随者负责精确运动控制（通过DAgger风格专家蒸馏更新）。两者交替优化直至达到Stackelberg均衡，系统仅依赖机载视觉和语言输入。

Result: 在AerialVLN基准的三个高保真城市场景中，CoNav-UAV一致优于单双智能体基线。学习场景下成功率提升最高达30.8个百分点，跨场景迁移下提升9.0个百分点，同时使用约3倍少的适应数据。消融分析验证了领导者与跟随者更新的互补增益，并揭示了不同VLM骨干下的稳健增益和不同学习动态。

Conclusion: CoNav-UAV通过Stackelberg博弈框架有效解决了双无人机协作目标导航中全局探索与近距离接近难以调和的问题，迭代Stackelberg学习使两智能体实现真正的相互适应和协作，在效率和性能上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Target-oriented vision-and-language navigation (VLN) on aerial platforms is attracting growing attention for missions such as disaster rescue, infrastructure inspection, and security patrol. In this task, an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) needs to locate targets given only a concise description of their appearance and surroundings. This requires global exploration and grounding as well as collision-free close-range approach, two interleaved processes difficult to reconcile within a single agent. Most existing methods transfer the ground VLN paradigm to a low-altitude UAV and compensate for its inefficient exploration with external assistance. A recent attempt deploys two UAVs at complementary altitudes yet still relies on privileged information and trains its two agents independently, precluding any mutual adaptation essential for cooperation. Here we propose CoNav-UAV, which explicitly models the task as a Stackelberg game between a high-altitude leader and a low-altitude follower, with the system operating on onboard visual and linguistic inputs alone. To solve this game, we introduce Iterative Stackelberg Learning. The leader's high-level vision-language reasoning is refined via memory-based in-context learning, while the follower's precise motion control is updated via DAgger-style expert distillation. The alternation drives both agents toward a Stackelberg equilibrium. CoNav-UAV consistently outperforms single- and dual-agent baselines across three high-fidelity urban scenes from the AerialVLN benchmark. Success rate improves by up to 30.8 points on the learning scene, and 9.0 points under cross-scene transfer while using about 3x less adaptation data. Further analyses validate the complementary gains of the leader and follower updates and reveal robust gains yet distinct learning dynamics across VLM backbones.

</details>


### [139] [CockpitHAT: Dependency-Graph-Driven Hierarchical Attribution for Embodied Multi-Agent Cockpits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01805)
*Wei Wang,Shuanghe Liu,Zhu Zhuo,Jiaqi Zhong,Xiaozhao Zhao,Xiaojie Zuo,Jie Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CockpitHAT，一个面向LLM多智能体系统的分层归因框架，通过依赖距离阈值替代位置窗口、多通道证据集成以及安全感知的置信加权分析来解决"正确性崩溃"问题，并发布包含212条标注失败轨迹的CockpitBench基准。实验表明其在多个基准上显著超越文本-only的SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统存在"正确性崩溃"问题：高任务级准确率掩盖了严重的流程级失败，这在汽车座舱等安全关键场景中尤为危险——词汇正确的表达可能触发危险的物理操作。现有归因方法仅依赖文本轨迹，忽视了依赖结构、多通道证据和安全感知评估，导致无法可靠诊断故障根因。

Method: CockpitHAT采用三层创新：(1) 用交互DAG的依赖距离阈值替代位置窗口进行分层归因；(2) 通过具身适配器（embodied adapter）集成多通道证据（对话、车辆状态、环境、记忆）；(3) 在置信加权分析师共识中对高风险失败应用安全提升（safety-uplift）。同时发布CockpitBench基准，包含212条标注失败轨迹，每条由三位专家依据ISO 26262 ASIL严重性分级标注。

Result: 在Who&When基准上，CockpitHAT在Hand-Crafted分割上达到77.9%（agent级）/37.8%（步骤精确）准确率，在Algorithm-Generated分割上达到86.5%/46.0%，比文本-only SOTA ECHO最高提升17.6/16.7个点。在CockpitBench上达到78.3% agent级和38.2%步骤精确准确率。

Conclusion: 依赖感知、多通道、风险校准的归因范式是现实世界具身LLM多智能体系统中可靠故障诊断的有效方案，为安全关键场景下的流程级错误归因提供了新的研究方向和实用基准。

Abstract: LLM multi-agent systems suffer from Correctness Collapse, where high task-level accuracy conceals severe process-level failures. This is especially hazardous in safety-critical embodied settings such as automotive cockpits, where lexically correct utterances may trigger dangerous physical operations. Existing attribution methods rely on text traces alone, missing dependency structure, multi-channel evidence, and safety-aware evaluation. We introduce CockpitHAT, a hierarchical attribution framework that replaces positional windows with dependency-distance thresholds from interaction DAGs, integrates multi-channel evidence via an embodied adapter, and applies a safety-uplift to high-risk failures during confidence-weighted analyst consensus. We further release CockpitBench, a benchmark of 212 annotated failure traces spanning dialogue, vehicle-state, environmental, and memory channels, each labeled with ISO 26262 ASIL severity via three-expert consensus. On the public Who&When benchmark, CockpitHAT achieves agent-level / step-exact accuracies of 77.9% / 37.8% on the Hand-Crafted split and 86.5% / 46.0% on the Algorithm-Generated split, surpassing the text-only SOTA ECHO by up to 17.6 / 16.7 points. On CockpitBench, it attains 78.3% agent-level and 38.2% step-exact accuracy. These results establish dependency-aware, multi-channel, risk-calibrated attribution as an effective paradigm for reliable failure diagnosis in real-world embodied LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [140] [SearchMaster: Grounded and Regulated Self-Play for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01822)
*Wentao Tan,Qiong Cao,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchMaster是一个自博弈框架，通过让单个LLM在本地搜索环境中自我生成、求解和验证搜索任务来训练搜索智能体。它通过证据链生成器（ECG）、搜索深度奖励（SDR）和过度打开惩罚（OOP）三种机制解决自生成数据中的伪多跳问题、难度估计偏差和浅层浏览问题，在六个深度搜索基准上将Qwen3.5-9B的平均准确率从38.19%提升到51.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练基于LLM的搜索智能体需要高质量搜索数据，但现有管线通常依赖人工编写任务、专家示范或更强的教师模型。作者希望打破这种对人工标注和外部强模型的依赖，让单个LLM通过自博弈方式自主生成高质量的训练数据。

Method: SearchMaster采用三组件控制系统：(1) 证据链生成器（ECG）通过显式的跨文档证据链来引导任务生成，减少伪多跳问题；(2) 搜索深度奖励（SDR）依据成功rollout的搜索深度而非单纯成功率来评估任务难度，确保保留的任务具有真正的搜索密集性；(3) 过度打开惩罚（OOP）通过惩罚过多的文档打开行为来规范工具使用，避免长而浅的浏览。验证过的Proposer和Solver rollout通过GRPO进行联合优化。

Result: 在六个深度搜索基准上，SearchMaster将Qwen3.5-9B骨干模型的平均准确率从38.19%提升至51.52%，其中在BrowseComp-Plus上获得30.1个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 实验证明，有约束和受调节的自博弈方法能够在不依赖人工标注QA对或专家示范的情况下，提供有效的搜索智能体训练数据，展示了自生成数据的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Training LLM-based search agents requires high-quality search data: tasks that demand genuine multi-hop retrieval and trajectories that use search tools effectively. Existing pipelines often depend on human-written tasks, expert demonstrations, or stronger teacher models. We present SearchMaster, a self-play framework that trains a single LLM from search tasks it generates, solves, and verifies in a local search environment. The key challenge is that self-generated tasks and rollouts can yield misleading signals: pseudo multi-hop questions, success-rate difficulty estimates that ignore search depth, and rollouts with excessive opening but little targeted evidence acquisition. SearchMaster addresses these failure modes with three controls. An Evidence-Chain Generator (ECG) grounds task generation in explicit cross-document evidence chains to reduce pseudo multi-hop questions. A Search-Depth Reward (SDR) scores task difficulty by the search depth of successful rollouts rather than success rate alone, keeping retained tasks search-intensive. An Over-Opening Penalty (OOP) regulates tool use by discouraging excessive document opening, avoiding long but shallow browsing. Verified Proposer and Solver rollouts are then jointly optimized with GRPO. Across six deep-search benchmarks, SearchMaster improves a Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 38.19% to 51.52% average accuracy, with a 30.1-point gain on BrowseComp-Plus. These results show that grounded and regulated self-play can provide effective search-agent training data without human-labeled QA pairs or expert demonstrations. The code is available at https://github.com/WentaoTan/SearchMaster.

</details>


### [141] [Rewriting or Reweighting? A Geometric Account in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01835)
*Juntong Wang,Shengkun Yang,Xiyuan Wang,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"行为流形分析"(Behavioral Manifold Analysis)方法，通过选取稀疏行为关联坐标并将其嵌入低维局部图表，分别在激活空间(ACT)和贡献空间(NOC)中刻画模型行为几何。跟踪后训练过程发现：监督微调(SFT)会重写继承的行为几何，而奖励优化(RL)则主要保留底层图表结构、仅重新加权权重。这一几何视角为理解两种训练目标间的机制性差异提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练会显著改变语言模型行为，但聚合行为率无法揭示训练究竟是移除了既有机制、创建了新机制，还是改变了继承机制的使用方式。作者希望从机制层面区分训练目标对模型内部结构的影响。

Method: 提出行为流形分析：通过选择稀疏行为关联坐标并将其提升到低维局部图表中；构建两个互补空间——ACT(激活运行时状态)与NOC(量化模型将功能信息流路由通过共享行为关联子空间的程度)。在受控后训练设置下跟踪这些图表的变化。

Result: 多个模型家族中，所得图表高度压缩且部分可跨架构对齐；贡献空间图表展现出更强的架构鲁棒共享核心，而激活空间图表保留更强的家族特定结构。跟踪受控后训练发现一致的不对称性：SFT显著改变继承的行为几何，而奖励优化改变行为但基本保留底层图表。

Conclusion: SFT倾向于重写行为几何，奖励优化则主要重加权。该几何视角为理解两种训练目标的机制性差异提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Post-training can substantially alter language-model behavior, yet aggregate behavior rates do not reveal whether training removes an existing mechanism, creates a new one, or changes how an inherited mechanism is used. We study this question through two mechanistically distinct failures, repetition as a decoding-attractor pathology and sycophancy as a preference-related alignment failure. We introduce behavioral manifold analysis, which isolates behavior-specific geometry by selecting sparse behavior-associated coordinates and lifting them into low-dimensional local charts. We construct these charts in two complementary spaces. ACT captures runtime activation states, while NOC quantifies how strongly the model routes functional information flow through the shared behavior-associated subspace. Across multiple model families, the resulting charts are highly compressed and partially alignable across architectures. Contribution-space charts expose a more architecture-robust shared core, whereas activation-space charts retain stronger family-specific structure. Tracking these charts through controlled post-training reveals a consistent asymmetry. Supervised fine-tuning substantially alters the inherited behavioral geometry, whereas reward optimization changes behavior while largely preserving the underlying chart. This geometric perspective provides a unified framework for understanding the mechanistic distinction between the two objectives. SFT tends to rewrite behavioral geometry, whereas reward optimization primarily reweights it. Code is available at https://github.com/ronglingze/Manifold-Analysis

</details>


### [142] [PCSD: Persistent Consistency for Self-Distillation in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01837)
*Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Junlin Liu,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Xinming Wang,Jinyang Wu,Guoren Wang,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PCSD（持久一致性自蒸馏）方法，通过从教师偏好信号的局部持久性中派生token级蒸馏权重，为LLM智能体的强化学习提供密集的token级监督，并与GRPO联合优化，在ALFWorld等任务上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂交互任务中的RL常受稀疏奖励困扰（长轨迹仅获单一结果级信号）。OPSD虽提供密集token级监督，但特权教师并非在每个位置都可靠。现有方法要么依赖孤立token差异（对噪声敏感），要么分配共享的step级权重（忽略位置差异），需要更精细的蒸馏权重设计。

Method: PCSD从教师偏好信号的局部持久性中派生token级蒸馏权重。具体包含三个核心组件：1）使用自适应窗口与指数衰减聚合来捕获持久的相对教师支持；2）应用趋势感知调制来衰减局部下降的支持信号；3）通过sigmoid门控产生连续权重。最终目标函数与GRPO联合优化，结合密集教师引导与稀疏环境反馈。

Result: 在无需推理时技巧的情况下，PCSD在ALFWorld Overall上基于两个主干网络均取得最优结果，超越GRPO分别达15.6和13.3分，超越SDAR达6.2和5.5分；在WebShop上保持竞争力，并在未见ALFWorld分割上超越GRPO达15.8分。

Conclusion: PCSD通过捕捉教师支持的局部持久性来生成更可靠的token级蒸馏权重，有效解决了稀疏奖励和教师不可靠问题，在多个基准任务上验证了其优越性，为LLM智能体RL提供了一种更稳健的密集监督训练范式。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong potential in complex interactive tasks, yet their reinforcement learning (RL) is often hindered by sparse rewards, as a long multi-turn trajectory may receive only a single outcome-level signal. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher, but the teacher may not be reliable at every position. Existing methods commonly rely on isolated token-level discrepancies, which can be sensitive to noise, or assign a shared step-level weight that may overlook positional variation. We propose Persistent Consistency Self-Distillation (PCSD), which derives token-level distillation weights from the local persistence of teacher-favoring signals. PCSD combines adaptive windows with exponentially decayed aggregation to capture persistent relative teacher support, applies trend-aware modulation to attenuate locally declining support, and produces continuous weights through sigmoid gating. The resulting objective is jointly optimized with GRPO, combining dense teacher guidance with sparse environmental feedback. Without inference-time skills, PCSD achieves the best ALFWorld Overall results among all baselines on both backbones, exceeding GRPO by 15.6 and 13.3 points and SDAR by 6.2 and 5.5 points, while remaining competitive on WebShop and gaining 15.8 points over GRPO on unseen ALFWorld split.

</details>


### [143] [FOCUS: FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01847)
*Xianglong Yan,Hong Liu,Chengzhu Bao,Tianao Zhang,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FOCUS是一个面向大规模语言模型（LLM）的FP4后训练量化框架，通过耦合放松缩放（CRS）和双粒度缩放（DGS）实现端到端的尺度学习，在MXFP4和NVFP4硬件格式下达到最先进的FP4精度，且不引入额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽然性能出色但由于体量巨大导致部署成本高昂。FP4量化（如MXFP4和NVFP4）在现代加速器上有原生硬件支持，是极具吸引力的解决方案。然而在FP4精度下保持准确率仍然困难，关键瓶颈在于尺度优化：现有方法将量化尺度和反量化尺度紧密耦合，迫使两者都遵从硬件要求的离散低精度格式（如MXFP4中的E8M0）。但实际上量化尺度从不存储，无需受此约束，这暗示了巨大的未开发优化空间。

Method: FOCUS提出两个核心组件：（1）耦合放松缩放（CRS）：通过引入可学习的全精度系数来放松量化尺度与反量化尺度之间的紧密耦合，在不破坏硬件兼容性的前提下实现更有效的优化；（2）双粒度缩放（DGS）：在更细的子块粒度上细化量化尺度，使其能更精确地适应局部权重分布。两者共同构成端到端的尺度学习后训练量化框架。

Result: 在多个LLM家族和基准测试上的实验表明，FOCUS在MXFP4和NVFP4两种格式下均达到最先进的FP4准确率，同时不引入任何额外的推理开销。

Conclusion: 通过解耦量化与反量化尺度的约束并引入双粒度细化策略，FOCUS有效挖掘了FP4量化的尺度优化空间，在保持硬件兼容性的同时显著提升量化精度，为低比特LLM部署提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance but are expensive to deploy due to their enormous size. FP4 quantization, with formats such as MXFP4 and NVFP4, offers an appealing solution with native hardware support on modern accelerators.
