However, maintaining accuracy under FP4 precision remains difficult. A key bottleneck lies in scale optimization: existing methods tightly couple the quantization and dequantization scales, forcing both to conform to the discrete low-precision format required by hardware, such as E8M0 in MXFP4. Yet the quantization scale is never stored and need not obey this constraint, suggesting a significant untapped optimization space. In this work, we propose FOCUS, a post-training quantization framework with end-to-end scale learning for FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling. Coupled-Relaxation Scaling (CRS) relaxes the tight coupling between quantization and dequantization scales with a learnable full-precision coefficient, enabling more effective optimization without breaking hardware compliance. Dual-Granularity Scaling (DGS) further refines the quantization scale at a finer sub-block granularity, allowing more precise adaptation to local weight distributions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and benchmarks show that FOCUS achieves state-of-the-art FP4 accuracy under both MXFP4 and NVFP4 formats, while introducing no additional inference overhead. Code and quantized models will be released at https://github.com/tencent/AngelSlim.

</details>


### [144] [Exploring and Bridging Knowledge Holes in Unlearned Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01849)
*Junxiang You,Junkai Chen,Yuhao He,Ruiqi Liu,Zhetao Guo,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态大模型（MLLM）机器遗忘评估范式的关键盲区——难以捕捉"知识空洞"（对良性邻近输入的严重性能退化），并提出SPAR方法（选择性保护+锚定正则化）来缓解该问题，在SafeEraser上恢复超过98%的原始响应质量，同时保持0%攻击成功率和有竞争力的模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM机器遗忘评估方法存在关键盲区：模型效用的评估基准与遗忘集表示距离较远，无法捕捉"知识空洞"——即模型在共享遗忘集通用模式的良性相邻输入上出现的严重退化。这是一种系统性问题，需要新的评估基准和缓解方法。

Method: 作者构建了一个能够捕捉遗忘集共享通用模式的良性输入上意外退化的基准；在此基础上提出SPAR（Selective Protection with Anchored Regularization），包含两个核心组件：通过锚定激活过滤保护通用模式，以及通过实体抽象增强来强化这些模式。

Result: 在SafeEraser上的实验表明，SPAR能够恢复超过98%的原始响应质量（标准基线仅为50%以下），同时实现0.00%的攻击成功率并保持具有竞争力的模型效用。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了更细粒度评估对可信MLLM机器遗忘的必要性，SPAR方法为兼顾遗忘精度与模型效用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning offers a promising approach to remove unsafe content from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), yet ensuring the precision of unlearning remains a persistent challenge. One reason is that current MLLM unlearning evaluation paradigms suffer from a critical blind spot: they assess model utility through benchmarks whose representations are distant from the forget set, failing to capture knowledge holes---severe degradation on benign adjacent inputs. To probe knowledge holes in unlearned MLLMs, we construct a benchmark that captures unintended degradation on benign inputs sharing generic patterns with the forget set, and confirm through controlled experiments that they are a systematic consequence of commonly used approaches. Furthermore, to bridge this gap, we propose Selective Protection with Anchored Regularization, which protects generic patterns via anchored activation filtering while reinforcing them through entity-abstracted enhancement. Our experiments on SafeEraser demonstrate that SPAR recovers over 98% of vanilla response quality compared to below 50% for standard baselines---while achieving 0.00% attack success rate and competitive model utility. These results underscore the necessity of more fine-grained evaluation for trustworthy MLLM unlearning.

</details>


### [145] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Complex Eigenfrequency Identification and Mode Structure Reconstruction of the Ground-State ITG Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01850)
*Dengdi Sun,Bingbing Zhang,Xiao Wang,Zikang Yan,Yuqiang Tao,Qingquan Yang,Guosheng Xu,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合傅里叶特征编码、复值特征传播和三阶段训练的物理信息神经网络框架，用于联合识别托卡马克等离子体中ITG漂移波的复本征频率和二维复值模场，在稀疏观测约束下优于代表性PINN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高约束模式托卡马克台基区存在陡峭梯度，ITG漂移波的本征频率和模场联合识别对理解等离子体约束和边缘输运至关重要。然而该区域局部高频振荡、实虚部强耦合以及模场与本征频率的非线性耦合，对PINN的表示能力和联合优化构成严峻挑战。

Method: 提出一种物理信息神经框架，包含三个关键组件：1）傅里叶特征编码以捕捉高频振荡；2）复值特征传播机制以处理模场的实虚部耦合；3）三阶段训练策略以应对本征频率与模场联合优化的非线性耦合难题。在稀疏观测和物理约束下，联合求解基态ITG分支的复本征频率与二维复值模场。

Result: 实验表明该框架能够准确重构目标复本征频率和二维复值模场，在性能上优于代表性PINN基线方法。此外，该框架为分析高阶及多分支漂移波模式提供了基础。

Conclusion: 所提出的物理信息神经网络框架有效解决了台基区ITG漂移波联合识别中的高频表示和联合优化难题，为等离子体物理中复杂波动模式的分析提供了新的工具和思路。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) combine sparse observations with physical equations, providing an important approach for modeling complex plasma processes and inferring unknown physical quantities. The steep-gradient pedestal of high-confinement-mode tokamaks is closely linked to plasma confinement and edge transport. Analyzing ion-temperature-gradient (ITG) drift waves in this region requires jointly identifying complex eigenfrequencies and reconstructing two-dimensional complex-valued mode fields. Localized high-frequency oscillations, strong real-imaginary coupling, and nonlinear coupling between the mode field and eigenfrequency challenge PINN representation and joint optimization. To address these challenges, we propose a physics-informed neural framework combining Fourier feature encoding, complex-valued feature propagation, and three-stage training. Under sparse observations and physical constraints, it jointly solves for the complex eigenfrequency and mode field of a representative ground-state ITG branch. Experiments show that the framework accurately recovers the target complex eigenfrequency and two-dimensional complex-valued mode field and outperforms representative PINN baselines. It also provides a basis for analyzing higher-order and multiple-branch drift-wave modes.

</details>


### [146] [EchoChange: A Diffusion Language Model with Dual Pass Remasking for Factual Remote Sensing Disaster Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01856)
*Dongwei Sun,Bowen Yao,Yujie Zhang,Pei Liu,Jing Yao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EchoChange 是一个多模态离散扩散语言模型，将双时相遥感灾害变化描述生成建模为迭代掩码去噪而非自回归生成，通过反复修正整个描述来消除级联事实错误，在RSCC基准上优于各基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变化描述方法均采用自回归解码范式，一旦早期对变化对象、事件或空间关系的解读出错，就会成为后续文本生成的不可逆前提，将视觉模糊性放大为级联的事实错误。因此需要一种能重新审视不确定内容并纠正中间错误预测的解码方式。

Method: 提出EchoChange，一种多模态离散扩散语言模型，将变化描述生成建模为迭代式掩码token去噪（而非从左到右生成）。包含三大关键技术：(1) draft-aware dual-pass training（草稿感知双通道训练）；(2) progressive masking curriculum（渐进掩码课程）；(3) confidence-guided remasking（置信度引导的重新掩码），使训练与迭代推理过程对齐。

Result: 在RSCC基准上的大量实验表明，EchoChange在词汇和语义指标上均显著优于通用型及遥感专用的基线方法。

Conclusion: EchoChange通过迭代式扩散去噪范式有效解决了遥感变化描述中自回归解码导致的级联误差问题，为双时相遥感灾害场景的变化理解与描述提供了更可靠的新思路。

Abstract: Bi-temporal remote-sensing disaster change captioning often needs to identify sparse and spatially localized changes across large pre- and post-event scenes and then translate them into coherent, factual descriptions. However, existing change captioning methods always follow an autoregressive decoding paradigm to generate the change description and thus an early misinterpretation of the changed object, event, or spatial relation becomes an irreversible premise for subsequent text, amplifying visual ambiguity into cascading factual errors. To address this limitation, we propose EchoChange, a multimodal discrete diffusion language model that formulates change captioning as iterative masked-token denoising rather than left-to-right generation. By repeatedly revising the entire caption while conditioning on the image pair, EchoChange can reconsider uncertain content and correct imperfect intermediate predictions. We further introduce draft-aware dual-pass training, a progressive masking curriculum, and confidence-guided remasking to align training with iterative inference. Extensive experiments on the RSCC benchmark show that EchoChange substantially outperforms both general-purpose and remote-sensing-specific baselines across lexical and semantic metrics. The EchoChange Project is at https://github.com/sundongwei/EchoChange_Project

</details>


### [147] [Wnuan: Staged Post-Training for Question Answering over Proprietary Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01862)
*Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Wenxin Ma,Qian Kou,Yiming Pan,Longbin Yu,Ying Liu,Haiping Wang,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wnuan，一个三阶段企业问答适配流水线（文档构造任务监督→带通用数据回放的SFT→对残差错误的RL），在707题WnuanBench上将有可接受答案率(AAR)从适配前的52.76%提升至SFT后的80.06%和RL后的91.51%，但通用基准平均下降5.17分，主要集中于指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级问答系统需要在获取专有知识的同时不牺牲模型的通用能力，这是一个关键的权衡问题。现有方法缺乏系统化的分阶段适配流程，也缺少对适配过程中能力增益与通用能力损耗的量化刻画。

Method: Wnuan是一个三阶段流水线：（1）从企业文档中构建面向任务的监督信号；（2）进行带通用数据回放的监督微调（SFT）；（3）针对残差错误应用强化学习（RL）。其核心创新在于使用残差错误采样策略来挑选RL训练样本。

Result: 在707题WnuanBench上，32B主路径将AAR从52.76%提升至80.06%（SFT后）和91.51%（RL后）。在100次更新的匹配协议下，残差错误采样比全池采样和规模匹配的随机采样分别高3.11和2.97个百分点。自助法置信区间均包含正区间，同域验证集保持排序。通用基准平均下降5.17分，集中体现在指令跟随。自动评估集成与权威领域专家在90.5%的分层抽样样本上一致。

Conclusion: 论文特征化地刻画了分阶段企业适配在领域能力提升与通用能力损耗两方面的综合效果：残差错误采样是RL阶段样本选择的有效策略，但适配过程存在可量化的通用能力下降，主要集中于指令跟随能力上。

Abstract: Enterprise question answering requires models to acquire proprietary knowledge without discarding general capabilities. We present Wnuan, a three-stage pipeline that constructs task-oriented supervision from documents, performs supervised fine-tuning with general-data replay, and applies reinforcement learning to residual errors. On the 707-question WnuanBench, the primary 32B route raises acceptable-answer rate (AAR) from 52.76% before adaptation to 80.06% after SFT and 91.51% after RL. Under a matched 100-update protocol, residual-error sampling outperforms full-pool and size-matched random sampling by 3.11 and 2.97 points, respectively. Source-cluster bootstrap intervals remain above zero for both contrasts, and a same-domain validation set preserves the ordering. The general-benchmark average decreases by 5.17 points across the route, concentrated in instruction following. The automatic evaluation ensemble agrees with an authoritative domain expert on 90.5% of a stratified Wnuan-Inst response sample. These results characterize both the gains and the general-capability cost of staged enterprise adaptation.

</details>


### [148] [ReasonCast: Towards Explainable Time Series Forecasting with Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01875)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出任务融合的时间序列模型范式，将数值预测与可解释推理融入单一自回归响应中，并配套提供了基准ReasonTS-Bench和微调方案ReasonCast，实验证明其在预测精度和因果推理可验证性上均优于现有LLM与专用TS模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型要么专注于理解（返回文本答案），要么专注于生成（返回数值预测）。即便是近期出现的统一模型，也仍以任务分离的路径输出两种结果，无法在同一条连贯响应中同时给出预测及其原因解释。作者主张应构建任务融合（task-fused）模型，在同一响应中联合产出预测与自解释。

Method: 论文包含两大贡献：(1) 提出ReasonTS-Bench基准，识别出时间序列的五种基本模式，支持对理解与生成两个任务的联合评估；(2) 提出ReasonCast微调方案，可对任意LLM进行微调，使其在单次自回归传递中同时生成推理链与数值预测。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReasonCast在预测精度上优于现有LLM和专用时间序列模型，同时能够生成可验证的因果推理。

Conclusion: 任务融合范式是时间序列领域的一个重要方向，将数值预测与可解释推理统一于单一模型响应中，既提升了预测精度，也增强了模型输出的可解释性与可验证性。

Abstract: Most time series (TS) models are specialized for a single task, either understanding (i.e., returning text answers about a TS) or generation (i.e., returning a numeric forecast). Only recently have unified models begun to handle the two within a single architecture. Even these models, however, produce the two outputs as task-separated paths and cannot predict a series and explain why that prediction arises within a single coherent response. In this paper, we argue for a task-fused model that jointly produces 1) prediction (generation) and 2) selfexplanation (understanding), thereby integrating 1) numerical TS forecasting and 2) interpretable text reasoning within a single response. To enable the systematic study of this capability, we present both a benchmark and a recipe that jointly address the two tasks. The benchmark, ReasonTS-Bench, identifies five fundamental patterns underlying TS and enables the joint evaluation of both tasks. ReasonCast, our recipe for finetuning any LLM to perform both tasks jointly, yields a model that generates a reasoning chain and a forecast together in a single autoregressive pass. Extensive experiments show that ReasonCast outperforms both LLMs and TS models on prediction accuracy while producing verifiable, causal reasoning. Code is available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/reasoncast.

</details>


### [149] [CoEvoKG: Co-Evolving Knowledge Graphs with Self-Evolving Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01904)
*Zhaoyang Li,Zenghuang Fu,Qiuyuan Ai,Ping Jiang,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Jie Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEvoKG 是一个将知识图谱同时用作可验证训练任务来源和持久化证据记忆的框架，用于智能体自我进化。它联合训练任务生成器与搜索智能体，将成功搜索中获得的证据写入知识图谱，形成闭环进化。在六个 QA 基准上，相比基础模型在三个骨干模型上分别提升 +11.2、+10.1、+11.6 个宏平均准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可以通过强化学习提升搜索智能体的能力，但现有的自对弈智能体在生成任务时不断重复，却丢弃了成功搜索过程中获得的知识，导致学习效率低下、知识无法积累。

Method: CoEvoKG 联合训练一个任务生成器和一个搜索智能体：生成器从知识图谱中采样的实体链创建多跳问题；智能体根据答案正确性和实体路径是否被图证据支持的奖励进行学习。当搜索成功时，框架对检索证据进行验证和去重，并将其写回对应的图节点和边。后续轮次复用增强后的图进行任务生成和奖励计算，形成模型自我进化与知识积累之间的闭环。

Result: 在六个 QA 基准（NQ、TriviaQA、PopQA、HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA 和 Bamboogle）上的实验表明，CoEvoKG 在 Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 和 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 三个骨干模型上分别比基础模型提升了 +11.2、+10.1 和 +11.6 个宏平均准确率点。在匹配训练预算下，相比竞争性自对弈和 RL 基线，在三个骨干模型上额外提升 +2.6 至 +3.7 个宏平均准确率点。

Conclusion: CoEvoKG 验证了将知识图谱作为任务源和证据记忆的双重角色能有效促进搜索智能体的自我进化，通过知识积累与模型自学习的闭环机制显著提升多跳问答性能。

Abstract: Large language models can improve with reinforcement learning for search agents, yet existing self play agents repeatedly generate tasks while discarding the knowledge gained during successful searches. We introduce CoEvoKG, a framework that turns a
  knowledge graph into both a source of verifiable training tasks and a persistent evidence memory for agent evolution. CoEvoKG jointly trains a task generator and a search agent: the generator creates multihop questions from entity chains sampled
  from the knowledge graph, while the agent learns from rewards for answer correctness and search trajectories whose entity paths are supported by graph evidence. When a search succeeds, CoEvoKG verifies and deduplicates the retrieved evidence, then
  writes it back to the corresponding graph nodes and edges. Future rounds reuse this enriched graph for task generation and reward computation, closing the loop between model self evolution and knowledge accumulation. Experiments on six QA benchmarks
  (NQ, TriviaQA, PopQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and Bamboogle) with three backbone models show that CoEvoKG improves macro average accuracy over the corresponding base models by +11.2, +10.1, and +11.6 points on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct,
  Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, respectively. Under matched training budgets, CoEvoKG further improves over competitive self play baselines and RL baselines for search agents by +2.6 to +3.7 macro average points across the three
  backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/lazzy1225/CoEvoKG.

</details>


### [150] [Diagnosing Search Behavior and Failure Modes in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01913)
*Qi Liu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过轨迹级诊断方法，系统分析了长时程深度搜索智能体的搜索行为与失败模式。研究发现搜索努力与答案质量仅呈弱相关，答案质量更取决于累积检索召回率而非搜索次数或上下文量。有用证据常出现较早，但智能体会继续产生长尾低收益搜索步骤。研究提出了检索缺口与利用缺口的失败分解框架，并为构建更好的深度研究系统指明了实用方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体通过迭代发出搜索查询来收集证据以回答复杂信息寻求问题，但当前尚不清楚更大程度的搜索努力是否以及如何带来更好的答案。作者希望通过轨迹级诊断来系统性地理解这一问题，为改进深度研究系统提供依据。

Method: 使用人工标注的文档级相关性判断，评估每个搜索步骤中检索到的证据质量，将智能体行为分离为两个阶段：检索了什么证据以及如何有效利用证据。基于此将失败分解为检索缺口（从未找到必要证据）和利用缺口（检索到相关证据但未正确使用）。在固定检索模型和评估框架的条件下，对六个智能体在BrowseComp-Plus上进行对比，并在BrowseComp上结合开放网页搜索API进行验证。

Result: 在所有设置中均发现：搜索努力与答案质量仅弱相关；答案准确性与检索证据质量（尤其是累积检索召回率）的相关性高于搜索次数或上下文消耗量；有用证据往往在轨迹早期出现，但智能体倾向于继续搜索并产生长尾低收益检索步骤；探索性查询改写仍然有用，但表现最好的智能体发出的冗余查询明显更少。

Conclusion: 通过系统刻画长时程搜索智能体的搜索行为和失败模式，本文为构建更好的深度研究系统指出了实用方向，包括更强的查询构建、更有效的证据选择和上下文管理，以及基于是否已检索到足够支持证据的停止标准。

Abstract: Deep search agents answer difficult information-seeking questions by iteratively issuing search queries to gather supporting evidence, but it remains unclear whether and how greater search effort leads to better answers. We study these questions through a trajectory-level diagnosis of long-horizon search agents. Using human-annotated document-level relevance judgments, we evaluate the evidence retrieved at each search step and separate two stages of agent behavior: what evidence an agent retrieves and how effectively it uses that evidence. This distinction further allows us to decompose failures into retrieval gaps, where the necessary evidence is never found, and utilization gaps, where relevant evidence is retrieved but not used correctly. With the retrieval model and evaluation harness held fixed, we compare six agents on BrowseComp-Plus and further validate our findings on BrowseComp with an open-web search API. Across settings, we find that search effort and answer quality are only weakly aligned. Answer accuracy is better correlated with the quality of retrieved evidence, especially cumulative retrieval recall, than with the number of searches or the amount of context consumed. Useful evidence often appears early in the trajectory, yet agents tend to continue searching, producing a long tail of low-yield retrieval steps. At the query level, exploratory reformulations remain useful, but the best-performing agents issue far fewer redundant queries. Overall, by systematically characterizing the search behavior and failure modes of long-horizon search agents, this work points to practical directions for building better deep research systems, including stronger query formulation, more effective evidence selection and context management, and stopping criteria based on whether sufficient supporting evidence has been retrieved.

</details>


### [151] [ProWorld: Progress-Aware Hyperbolic World Models for Long-Horizon Visual Goal Reaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01926)
*Zihan Liu,Yuzhe Zhuang,Yuanzu Li,Wanshuang Gou,Jiahong Liu,Min Zhou,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProWorld是一种基于双曲几何的进度感知视觉世界模型，通过目标条件进度排序（goal-conditioned progress order）组织潜空间动态，利用双曲蕴含学习和未来判别来缓解长程视觉目标规划中局部一致但全局漂移的问题，在四个视觉目标到达任务上平均成功率比LeWM提升9.67个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA式视觉世界模型通过下一步表示预测学习局部转移一致性，但在长程任务中存在两个关键问题：1) 多步推演可能在局部合理但偏离目标相关轨迹；2) 局部相似但长期进度不同的未来状态难以在仅针对局部一致性优化的潜空间中区分。

Method: 1) 引入目标条件进度排序，定义状态相对目标的进展顺序，该顺序具有非对称的由粗到细结构（早期状态保留更广泛未来可能性，后期状态集中于特定目标区域），适合双曲几何建模。2) 提出ProWorld模型：利用目标条件进度排序组织视觉潜空间动态；通过双曲蕴含学习（hyperbolic entailment learning）维持轨迹内的方向性进度；通过双曲未来判别（hyperbolic future discrimination）缓解局部相似未来状态间的进度歧义。3) 设计进度感知规划目标，通过联合考虑接近目标的程度和中间状态的持续进度来对候选推演评分。

Result: 在四个视觉目标到达任务上的实验表明，ProWorld相比LeWM取得了平均9.67个百分点的绝对成功率提升。

Conclusion: ProWorld通过将双曲几何与目标条件进度排序相结合，有效解决了长程视觉规划中局部一致性不足以保证全局目标进展的问题，验证了进度感知的层次化组织在视觉世界模型中的有效性。

Abstract: JEPA-style visual world models offer an effective paradigm for visual goal planning by predicting future latent representations. Existing methods typically learn local transition consistency through next-step representation prediction. However, in long-horizon tasks, accurate local prediction alone need not ensure sustained progress toward the goal. First, multi-step rollouts can remain locally plausible while drifting away from goal-relevant trajectories. Second, locally similar future states can correspond to substantially different long-term progress, making them difficult to distinguish in a latent space optimized mainly for local consistency. To address these challenges, we introduce goal-conditioned progress order, a relative ordering of states according to how they advance toward a given goal. This order exhibits an asymmetric, coarse-to-fine structure: early states retain broader future possibilities, while later states concentrate on more specific goal-relevant regions. Such a structure is well suited to hyperbolic geometry. Motivated by this observation, we propose ProWorld, a progress-aware hyperbolic visual world model. ProWorld leverages goal-conditioned progress order to organize visual latent-space dynamics, maintains directional progress within trajectories via hyperbolic entailment learning, and mitigates progress ambiguity among locally similar future states via hyperbolic future discrimination. Furthermore, we design a progress-aware planning objective that scores candidate rollouts by jointly considering proximity to the goal and sustained progress across intermediate states. Experiments on four visual goal-reaching tasks demonstrate that ProWorld achieves an average absolute success-rate gain of 9.67 over LeWM. The code will be released after the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [152] [A Contractualist Argumentation Framework for Moral Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01937)
*Luis Marcos-Vidal,Giulio Antonio Abbo,Tony Belpaeme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Scanlon契约主义的道德决策形式化框架，利用ASPIC+结构化论证框架并结合基于价值的过滤机制，构建了一个契约主义论证框架，使智能体在共享环境中能对相互冲突的利益进行道德推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在共享环境中需要做出影响多个利益相关者的决策，而这些利益往往是相互冲突的。现有方法缺乏一个能系统性地评估决策道德可允许性的形式化框架，因此需要一种基于成熟伦理理论的形式化方法。

Method: 基于Scanlon的契约主义伦理理论（评估行为可允许性的标准是：是否存在任何人都无法合理拒绝的原则），使用ASPIC+结构化论证框架作为基础，并扩展了基于价值的过滤机制，用以建模每个智能体的价值观如何决定哪些理由在道德上是相关的。最终形成契约主义论证框架，通过论证语义对智能体的理由进行形式化表示、比较和评估。

Result: 通过一个家庭场景的实例演示了该框架的运作方式，并讨论了与现有基于价值的论证方法的联系与区别。

Conclusion: 该框架为自主智能体的道德决策提供了形式化的契约主义推理基础，能够在多智能体冲突利益场景中进行规范性的道德判断。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in shared environments must make decisions that affect multiple individuals with potentially conflicting interests. We propose a formal framework for moral decision-making grounded in Scanlon's contractualism, an ethical theory that evaluates the permissibility of actions in terms of principles that no one could reasonably reject. To operationalise contractualist reasoning, we use ASPIC+, a structured argumentation framework, extended with value-based filtering to model how each agent's values determine which reasons are morally relevant in the first place. The result is a Contractualist Argumentation Framework in which agents' reasons are formally represented, compared, and evaluated through argumentation semantics. We illustrate the approach through a worked example in a domestic setting and discuss its relation to existing value-based argumentation approaches.

</details>


### [153] [Long-Horizon Autonomous Architecture Research with a Language-Model Agent: A Behavioural Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01995)
*Aon Safdar,Mohamed Saadeldin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究单个通用大语言模型作为唯一研究者进行长时间跨度的神经架构设计任务的自主科研行为。在约100个顺序实验中，模型从弱基线将非标准Vision Transformer提升为小基准上的高效模型和ImageNet-1K上的可用（但未达SOTA）模型，并揭示了四方面发现：产出呈阶段结构、早期单一假说贡献最大、贪心渐进式假说偏好主要由工作流诱导、以及独立重新发现既有结论并推翻标准设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索单个通用大语言模型作为唯一研究员在长时间跨度自主科研中的真实行为模式，特别是识别哪些因素（智能体能力vs工作流设计）对科研产出具有更大影响，为未来自主科研系统的设计提供指导。

Method: 构建一个包含实验管理、实验追踪、文献访问和持久记忆等研究工具面的自主智能体系统，分三个阶段（渐进扩展工具面或问题规模）对非标准Vision Transformer进行约100个顺序实验的长时间自主设计。采用人工定义阶段转换，全程记录密集行为痕迹以供分析。

Result: 四项关键发现：(i)产出呈清晰阶段结构——快速早期增益、数十假说的饱和墙、以及通过扩展行动面而非改变底层模型触发的恢复；(ii)单个早期假说对精度增益贡献最大，后续改进呈长尾分布；(iii)贪心渐进式假说偏好主要源于提交-丢弃评估规则（与贪心爬山同构）及风险厌恶和文献锚定；(iv)智能体独立重新发现既有成果，并在纯通道注意力这一不熟悉领域中推翻了标准设计选择。

Conclusion: 工作流设计的实际影响力至少不亚于智能体能力。建议未来自主科研探索多样化搜索、预算化的大胆假说（moonshot）、显式分叉路径及情境感知的重新验证（regime-aware re-validation）作为可测试的研究方向。

Abstract: We study what happens when a single general-purpose large language model acts as the sole researcher on a long-horizon neural architecture design problem. The agent receives a scientific question, an initial hypothesis and motivation, a compute budget, and research affordances (source and experiment management, experiment tracking, literature access, and persistent memory), then autonomously proposes, implements, evaluates, and records experiments over an extended period. The study comprises three phases, separated by human-declared transitions, that progressively expand the agent's tool surface or problem scale. Across approximately 100 sequential experiments, the agent improves a non-standard Vision Transformer from a weak baseline to a stronger, efficient model on small benchmarks and a usable but sub-SOTA model on ImageNet-1K, while producing a dense behavioural trace. We report four findings.(i)Productivity exhibits a clear phase structure: rapid early gains, a multi-dozen-hypothesis saturation wall, and recovery, with recovery triggered by expanding the action surface rather than changing the underlying model.(ii)A single early hypothesis contributes more to accuracy gain, with later improvements long-tailed.(iii)The preference for greedy, incremental hypotheses is largely workflow-induced: a commit-or-discard evaluation rule is isomorphic to greedy hill-climbing; the remainder reflects risk aversion after bold failures and anchoring on familiar literature. (iv)The agent independently rediscovers established results and, in the unfamiliar regime of pure channel attention, overturns a standard design choice. We conclude that workflow design was at least as influential as agent capability in this study and propose diversified search, budgeted moonshot hypotheses, explicit forks, and regime-aware re-validation as testable directions for future autonomous research.

</details>


### [154] [Evolving in the Agent Jungle via History-Informed Opponent Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02005)
*Zhaofeng Zhang,Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OASE（对手感知的选择性演化）框架，用于在动态多智能体环境中实现稳定的策略适应。OASE通过基于对手策略历史快照的同条件配对比较来评估技能修订的收益，仅当估计收益增益超过接受阈值时才采纳候选技能。实验表明，在一级价格拍卖和私人成本古诺竞争两个场景中，OASE相比Reflexion风格基线达到更低的最终均衡距离，同时接受更少的技能修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过修订技能库来实现行为适应，但在多智能体环境中，对手会同时更新策略导致环境本身持续演化。为静态环境设计的技能修订方法在这种动态环境中相当于针对过时的参考进行更新，难以有效应对持续变化的对手策略。

Method: OASE在动态多智能体环境中进行候选技能与现有技能间的配对比较，比较过程在由对手策略历史快照锚定的相同条件下进行，仅当候选技能的估计收益增益超过接受阈值时才予以采纳，从而实现有证据支撑的选择性技能更新。

Result: 在一级价格拍卖和私人成本古诺竞争两个决策场景中，相比Reflexion风格基线，OASE在两个环境中均达到更低的最终均衡距离，同时接受的技能修订数量显著更少，有效抑制了缺乏足够收益支撑的策略变更。

Conclusion: OASE用证据锚定的选择性机制取代盲目更新，使智能体即使在对手持续演化的环境中也能实现稳定而高效的策略适应，为动态多智能体环境下的策略演化提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Learning to adapt strategies through interaction is a key step toward more general and autonomous LLM agents. Existing approaches typically achieve behavioral adaptation by revising skill libraries. However, in multi-agent environments, opponents may simultaneously update their strategies, causing the environment itself to evolve continuously. Applying skill-revision methods designed for static environments in such settings therefore amounts to updating against an obsolete reference. To address this challenge, we introduce OASE (Opponent-Aware Selective Evolution), which identifies and adopts genuinely beneficial skill revisions in dynamic multi-agent environments. Specifically, OASE conducts paired comparisons between a candidate skill and the incumbent under identical conditions anchored by historical snapshots of opponent strategies, and adopts the candidate only when its estimated payoff gain exceeds an acceptance threshold. We evaluate OASE in two decision-making scenarios: first-price auctions and private-cost Cournot competition. Experimental results show that, compared with a Reflexion-style baseline, OASE achieves a lower final equilibrium distance in both environments while accepting substantially fewer skill revisions, thereby suppressing strategy changes that lack sufficient payoff support. OASE therefore replaces blind updating with evidence-anchored selection, allowing agents to adapt stably and efficiently even as opponents continuously evolve.

</details>


### [155] [HALT: Verification-Aware Stopping for Retrieval-Augmented Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02009)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HALT是一种轻量级、基于验证的策略，用于检索增强型搜索代理（RAG agents）的停止问题。它通过监控证据覆盖率而非生成器置信度来决定何时停止检索，在多跳QA基准上减少了冗余搜索并保持了精确匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索代理在回答复杂问题时反复发出搜索查询并积累证据，但存在停止问题：一旦必要证据已出现，继续检索只会增加成本、延迟和干扰性上下文。现有方法依赖生成器置信度来判断是否停止，但置信度并非可靠的停止信号。

Method: 将停止问题重新定义为证据覆盖率问题而非生成器置信度问题。提出HALT，一个验证感知的轻量级策略，不修改底层搜索代理。给定预期的跳数声明（hop claims），HALT仅在累积证据支持每个必要声明时才停止。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上，HALT减少了冗余搜索同时基本保持了精确匹配。可部署设置（从问题生成声明）带来较小但仍保持精确匹配的节省；使用金标准支持事实注释的诊断性上界显示更大的节省。对比基线和消融实验证实行为由声明-证据对齐驱动，而非通用充分性、固定停止位置或词汇重叠驱动。开放语料初步实验表明HALT在覆盖率无法可靠验证时选择弃权。

Conclusion: 证据覆盖率可作为检索增强代理的实用运行时控制信号，在不重新训练或修改宿主代理的情况下提升效率，为检索增强系统提供了一种轻量、可部署的改进方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented search agents answer multi-hop questions by repeatedly issuing search queries and accumulating evidence. This creates a stopping problem: after the necessary evidence has appeared, further retrieval often adds cost, latency, and distracting context rather than useful information. We frame stopping as evidence coverage rather than generator confidence, and introduce HALT, a lightweight verification-aware policy that leaves the search agent unchanged. Given expected hop claims, HALT stops only when cumulative evidence supports each required claim. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, HALT reduces redundant search while largely preserving exact match. We separate a deployable setting, where hop claims are generated from the question, from a diagnostic upper bound that uses gold supporting-fact annotations: generated claims give smaller but still exact-match-preserving savings, while gold claims show the larger savings available when hop targets are clean. Baseline comparisons and ablations show that this behavior is driven by claim-evidence alignment rather than generic sufficiency, fixed stop positions, or lexical overlap. Open-corpus pilots further suggest that HALT abstains when coverage cannot be reliably verified. Overall, evidence coverage provides a practical runtime control signal for improving retrieval-augmented agents without retraining or modifying the host agent.

</details>


### [156] [Before Reasoning Fails: Pre-Evidence Procedural Failures in Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02011)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体RAG系统中"检索后不阅读证据就直接作答"的失败模式，将其分解为证据前纪律失败和读取黄金证据后失败两种类型，并提出Read-Gate运行时不变式强制智能体在检索后必须阅读，实验表明该方法能显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体RAG系统可能在证据条件推理被测试之前就已经失败——智能体检索了候选片段但未检查就直接给出最终答案。现有评估未能区分这种过程性失败与答案侧的推理失败，因此需要将证据收集视为独立的轨迹级控制问题来研究。

Method: 利用保存的工具调用轨迹、检索证据、阅读片段和最终答案，将错误答案分解为证据前纪律失败（检索后跳过阅读）和读取黄金证据后失败（阅读了但推理错误）。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上收集12000条配对轨迹，使用regex和spaCy两种实体提取器分析两类失败的重叠率。提出Read-Gate，一个最小的运行时不变式，要求智能体在搜索之后、最终作答之前必须先阅读。

Result: 两类失败在很大程度上是非冗余的，共同触发率在[11.2%, 13.1%]之间。Read-Gate强制阅读使LLM-Acc在原本会跳过阅读的轨迹上提升14.9-19.9个百分点，在完整最小推理单元上提升3.2-9.4个百分点。额外诊断显示，更大的隐藏思维预算并不一定增加证据检查。

Conclusion: 证据收集应被视为独立的轨迹级控制问题，与答案侧推理分开评估。Read-Gate等运行时不变式能有效缓解检索后不阅读的失败，且更大的计算预算不能替代强制阅读的纪律约束。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can fail before evidence-conditioned reasoning is tested: an agent may retrieve candidate snippets but finalize without inspecting them. We study this failure mode as a procedural property of the agent trajectory, decomposing wrong answers into pre-evidence discipline failures and post-gold-read failures using saved tool-call traces, retrieved evidence, read passages, and final answers. Across 12,000 paired trajectories on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, the two failure types are largely non-redundant: the both-trigger rate is in [11.2%, 13.1%] across regex and spaCy entity extractors. We then evaluate Read-Gate, a minimal runtime invariant requiring an agent to read after search and before finalization. Forced reading improves LLM-Acc by 14.9-19.9 points on trajectories that would otherwise skip reading and by 3.2-9.4 points on full minimal-reasoning cells. Additional diagnostics show that larger hidden thinking budgets do not necessarily increase evidence inspection. Together, these results indicate that evidence-gathering should be evaluated as a trajectory-level control problem, separately from answer-side reasoning.

</details>


### [157] [EduZone: A Framework for Evaluating LLM Safety for K-12 Students and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02024)
*Junyeong Park,Jieun Han,Haneul Yoo,So-Yeon Ahn,Jinsung Yoon,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EduZone，一个面向K-12教育场景的LLM安全评估框架，系统地结合学生/教师使用场景、细粒度课程概念以及6个风险类别和28个子类别来生成上下文相关的对抗性交互，评估十个LLM在四层安全级别上的表现，发现教育特定风险和动态多轮交互下模型更脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在K-12教育中的应用日益广泛，但现有的安全评估很少考察学生或教师与LLM互动中出现的有害或不适当内容，缺乏针对教育场景的系统性安全评估框架。

Method: EduZone框架通过三个维度系统组合生成上下文相关的对抗性交互：(1)面向学生和教师的两类LLM使用场景；(2)细粒度课程概念；(3)覆盖常规和教育特定危害的6个风险类别及28个子类别。交互构建在三种设置中：单轮请求、静态多轮对话和动态多轮对话。评估采用四个安全级别：拒绝、安全协助、带有安全指导的风险协助以及完全风险协助。

Result: 对十个LLM的评估结果表明，模型在教育特定风险和动态多轮交互场景下表现出更高的脆弱性，现有的安全护栏无法充分应对这些风险。

Conclusion: EduZone提供了一个自动化、可扩展的评估框架，推动了LLM在K-12教育中的安全发展，支持更安全LLM的开发和部署。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used across diverse tasks in K-12 education, yet existing safety evaluations rarely examine how harmful or inappropriate content appears in interactions between LLMs and students or teachers. To address this, we present EduZone, an evaluation framework for LLM safety across diverse educational scenarios. Our framework systematically combines (1) student- and teacher-facing LLM usage contexts, (2) fine-grained curriculum concepts, and (3) 6 risk categories and 28 subcategories spanning both conventional and education-specific harms to generate contextually grounded adversarial interactions. We construct these interactions in three settings: single-turn requests, static multi-turn conversations, and dynamic multi-turn conversations. Using these interactions, we evaluate ten LLMs using four safety levels: refusal, safe assistance, risky assistance with safety guidance, and fully risky assistance. Our results reveal greater vulnerability to education-specific risks and dynamic multi-turn interactions, while existing safety guardrails fail to adequately address these risks. EduZone advances LLM safety in education by providing an automated, scalable evaluation framework that supports the development and deployment of safer LLMs in K-12 education.

</details>


### [158] [HPFA: Hypergraph-Based Paired Failure Attribution for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02026)
*Runchuan Zhu,Hongbin Lai,Bowen Jiang,Junrui Zhang,Zhangheng LI,Ostap Kilbasovych,Junyuan Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于超图的配对失败归因框架（HPFA），通过比较失败推理路径与成功参考路径的超边来定位根因，并训练轻量级归因模型提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理中的反射机制依赖于准确将失败归因到具体推理步骤，但现有模型缺乏此能力。已有方法要么需要昂贵的逐步反事实测试（随轨迹长度扩展性差），要么将推理轨迹视为扁平序列而忽略其非线性逻辑依赖关系。

Method: HPFA框架利用超图表示推理轨迹，通过对比失败推理路径与参考成功路径的超边来归因失败根因，缩小搜索空间；进而合成归因数据，通过监督微调和强化学习训练轻量级归因器模型。

Result: 在数学推理和智能体编码任务上，HPFA显著提升了归因准确率和效率，训练出的归因器在测试时持续提升推理准确率，优于缺乏图结构或配对分析的基线方法。

Conclusion: 超图结构与配对分析相结合能有效提升失败归因的准确性与效率，且训练出的轻量归因模型可泛化应用于测试阶段提升推理性能。

Abstract: Reflection is a powerful mechanism for LLM reasoning, yet its effectiveness hinges on accurately attributing failures to specific reasoning steps, a capability that current models notably lack. Existing failure attribution methods either require expensive step-by-step counterfactual testing that scales poorly with trajectory length, or treat reasoning traces as flat sequences that ignore the inherent non-linear logical dependencies. We propose a hypergraph-based paired failure attribution (HPFA) framework that attributes the failure root cause by comparing the hyperedges of the targeted failure reasoning path against a reference successful path. By reducing the search space, our method efficiently localizes root causes and enables scalable synthesis of attribution data for training a lightweight attributor model via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic coding tasks demonstrate that HPFA can dramatically increase attribution accuracy and efficiency, and the trained attributor consistently improves reasoning accuracy at test time, outperforming baselines that lack graph structure or paired analysis.

</details>


### [159] [Cross-Fitted Residual Utility for Primary-Preserving Cognitive Decision Correction in Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02063)
*Linzhuo Han,Zongyong Cui,Houbiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种认知无线电接收机中的后推断决策框架，通过交叉拟合残差效用与"保持主预测"的认知决策策略，在多个调制分类数据集上将总体准确率提升约2-3个百分点，并在11种信道退化条件下保持稳健增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动调制分类研究大多聚焦于表示学习精度，但认知接收机还需解决一个被忽视的后推断问题：当异构证据足以推翻某个可信的默认预测时，系统应如何决策。本文旨在研究并解决这一默认预测与证据之间的权衡问题。

Method: 采用交叉拟合残差效用（cross-fitted residual utility）作为学习目标，设计"保持主预测"的认知决策策略。使用结构化KAN-Fourier分类器输出默认预测概率，神经网络与非神经网络候选提供观测证据。利用训练集折外预测学习候选特定的残差效用，再用独立验证集冻结动作阈值、获批转移、条件路由与统一风险掩码，最后在留出集上评估。整套系统在RMLA、RMLB和HISAR数据集上进行验证，并开展对照实验、paired bootstrap与Holm校正的McNemar检验，以及冻结策略下的信道退化压力测试。

Result: 完整系统在RMLA上准确率从63.632%提升至66.332%，RMLB从65.161%提升至66.168%，HISAR从77.769%提升至79.867%。对照实验表明孤立效用目标并不均匀优于其他折外元学习器，稳定增益来自完整"证据+动作"策略。压力测试中11种条件下的增益均为正，paired 95%置信区间全部高于零。

Conclusion: 仅优化表示精度或仅改进效用估计都不足以获得稳定增益；真正的提升源于将残差效用估计与完整的证据融合及动作决策策略结合。该框架在载波频率偏移、I/Q不平衡及衰落信道等退化条件下仍保持稳健正向收益，支持其作为认知调制分类接收机的有效决策范式。

Abstract: Automatic modulation classification research has largely emphasized representation accuracy, but a cognitive receiver must also decide when heterogeneous evidence justifies overriding a trusted default prediction. We study this post-inference problem through cross-fitted residual utility and a primary-preserving cognitive decision policy. A structured KAN-Fourier classifier supplies the default probability, while neural and non-neural candidates provide observable evidence. Candidate-specific residual utility is learned from train-split out-of-fold predictions, and a disjoint validation split freezes action thresholds, approved transitions, conditional routes, and a unified risk mask before held-out evaluation. On RMLA, RMLB, and HISAR, the complete system improves overall accuracy from 63.632% to 66.332%, 65.161% to 66.168%, and 77.769% to 79.867%, respectively. Controlled comparisons show that the isolated utility target does not uniformly dominate alternative out-of-fold meta-learners; the consistent gain comes from the complete evidence-and-action policy. Paired bootstrap and Holm-corrected McNemar analyses support the controlled gains. A frozen-policy stress test under carrier-frequency offset, I/Q imbalance, and synthetic Rayleigh/Rician fading yields positive gains in all 11 conditions, with every paired 95\% confidence interval above zero.

</details>


### [160] [Instruction-Conditioned Exploration with Asymmetric Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02087)
*Jim Dilkes,Vahid Yazdanpanah,Sebastian Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出指令条件化探索（ICE）方法，通过在训练时用不同的指令补充任务提示来增强LLM在强化学习训练中的行为覆盖范围，并配合同步提出的非对称RL/SD训练目标（结合强化学习和自蒸馏）将探索到的行为迁移到无条件测试策略上。实验表明，ICE+Asymmetric-RL/SD在数学推理任务上使Qwen3-1.7B的pass@1（4K响应长度）相对DAPO基线提升5.0%，且改进在8K上下文中依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的强化学习后训练虽然重要，但其动作空间结构与传统RL不同，给探索机制带来了独特挑战。需要新的方法来利用预训练LLM的广泛知识和灵活性，在训练时主动生成多样化的经验，以提升模型能力。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：1) Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE)——在训练时用多种不同的指令补充任务提示，增加模型尝试行为的覆盖范围；2) Asymmetric-RL/SD——一种结合强化学习和自蒸馏的训练目标，用于将探索到的行为迁移到无条件的测试时策略。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，ICE配合Asymmetric-RL/SD目标使Qwen3-1.7B在4K响应长度的held-out pass@1指标上相对DAPO训练提升5.0%，且在更长的8K上下文下改进仍然持续。

Conclusion: 通过指令条件化探索并配合非对称RL/自蒸馏目标，可以有效扩大LLM在强化学习训练中的探索覆盖面，并将探索到的新行为成功迁移到实际测试时的无条件策略中，验证了该方法在不同上下文长度下的有效性。

Abstract: Post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important tool for improving model capabilities, but the LLM action-space structure introduces challenges distinct from classical RL, with implications for inducing exploration. New methods are required that leverage the broad knowledge and flexibility of pre-trained LLMs to deliberately generate diverse experience at training time. We propose Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE), which supplements task prompts during training with one of several distinct instructions, increasing the coverage of behaviours attempted. To facilitate ICE, we propose Asymmetric-RL/SD, a combined Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation training objective, to transfer explored behaviours to the unconditioned test-time policy. ICE with the Asymmetric-RL/SD objective improves Qwen3-1.7B held-out pass@1 performance at $4$K response length on mathematical reasoning tasks by $5.0\%$ relative to training with DAPO, with improvement persisting at a longer 8K context.

</details>


### [161] [Fetch-then-Explore: Decoupling Selection from Extraction over a Persistent Workspace for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02097)
*Qi Liu,Yiqun Chen,Zidan Chen,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Fetch-then-Explore，一种将页面选择与证据提取分离的搜索代理文档接口。它把页面存储在文件系统的每问题工作区中而非上下文窗口，按需延迟提取证据，并允许代理在后续轮次重新访问页面。在BrowseComp和WideSearch两个开放网页基准上，跨三个代理骨干，该方法在BrowseComp上全面领先，在WideSearch上持平或超越现有基线，其收益主要来源于对已离开页面的再次访问能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理的文档接口存在根本性缺陷：visit-and-read在获取页面时固定读取内容，无法预知代理后续需要的事实；stateful browsing虽可按需提取，但一次只保留一个页面，切换后即释放。两种方式都导致后续真正重要的页面需要重新获取并渲染进上下文，造成计算浪费和证据丢失。

Method: Fetch-then-Explore将页面选择与证据提取解耦：选中页面持久化到文件系统的每问题工作区中（而非上下文窗口或临时会话），证据在代理明确知道需要什么之后再按需提取，且可随假设演变重复提取。页面在代理转向其他页面后仍被保留，证据在轨迹中持续累积。在统一的ReAct框架和固定搜索条件下，与snippet-only、visit-and-read和browsing基线进行比较。

Result: 在BrowseComp和WideSearch两个基准上，跨三个代理骨干，Fetch-then-Explore在BrowseComp上所有骨干均取得最高准确率，在WideSearch上普遍匹配或超越基线。行为分析将收益归因于工作区的核心特性：对已离开页面的多次重新访问，这使首次浏览遗漏的证据仍可在后续恢复。

Conclusion: 将页面选择与证据提取分离、并将页面持久化存储而非绑定在上下文窗口或会话中，能显著提升搜索代理的文档利用效率。工作区式设计让页面可跨轨迹持续复用，即便代理初次提取遗漏证据，也能在之后返回补取，这是该方法相对瞬态接口的核心优势。

Abstract: Search agents now answer questions that take dozens of searches to settle, yet how such an agent reads a page has drawn far less attention than how it finds one. Nearly all of them use one of two document interfaces, and both tie a page to the moment it is opened. \emph{Visit-and-read} injects a reading of the page into the message history at fetch time, fixing that reading before the agent knows which fact it will need. Stateful \emph{browsing} instead extracts on demand from the page in hand, but holds one page at a time and releases it as soon as the agent opens another. Either way, a page that turns out to matter many turns later has to be fetched and rendered into context all over again. We propose \textbf{Fetch-then-Explore}, which separates page selection from evidence extraction and keeps what it selects: pages are recorded in a per-question workspace on the filesystem rather than the context window or a transient session, and evidence is pulled from them on demand later. Selection becomes almost free, extraction can wait until the agent knows what to look for and be repeated as its hypothesis sharpens, and pages are not released when the agent moves on, so evidence accumulates across the trajectory. In a unified ReAct harness with fixed search, we compare Fetch-then-Explore against snippet-only, visit-and-read, and browsing baselines on two open-web benchmarks, BrowseComp and WideSearch, across three agent backbones. It leads BrowseComp accuracy at every backbone and generally matches or exceeds the baselines on WideSearch, and a behavioral analysis traces the gains to the workspace's defining move: returning to a page after leaving it, which it does far more than any transient interface, so evidence missed on a first pass can still be recovered later.

</details>


### [162] [MemArbiter: Decision-Time Memory Arbitration for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02113)
*Jiajun Dong,Yutao Hu,Fengrui Fan,Shihan Dou,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemArbiter，一个功能感知的记忆仲裁框架，用于解决LLM智能体在长时程任务中的"记忆-行动差距"问题——即信息虽可访问但未能有效指导当前决策。MemArbiter将交互历史分解为原子条目，组织为五个功能性记忆库，并通过库级需求、条目级相关性、焦点-环境表示和时间展示门动态控制记忆显著性。在ALFWorld上，MemArbiter在500和750 token预算下分别达到82.8%和92.5%的成功率，比最强基线高出20.9和25.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体方法主要提升记忆的可访问性，但即使信息可访问，若形成、组织、优先级排序或呈现方式不佳，仍可能无法指导当前决策。这种"访问后失败"被称为记忆-行动差距。作者旨在解决由记忆管理不善导致的这部分差距。

Method: MemArbiter通过四个核心设计解决记忆-行动差距：(1) 将交互历史分解为原子条目；(2) 组织为五个功能性记忆库；(3) 结合库级需求、条目级相关性、焦点-环境表示进行动态选择；(4) 引入时间展示门控制记忆显著性。整个框架在统一的逐步骤记忆预算下运行。

Result: 在ALFWorld上的实验，使用开源权重行动生成模型，MemArbiter在500-token预算下成功率达82.8%，在750-token预算下达92.5%，分别比最强基线高出20.9和25.4个百分点。同时改善了失败后恢复，减少了失败的重复动作和状态-动作重复率。

Conclusion: 功能感知的记忆仲裁使可访问的信息能更有效地指导智能体行动，验证了记忆管理的功能化组织与动态显著性控制对于弥合记忆-行动差距的关键作用。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain and use cross-step information to act coherently in long-horizon tasks. Existing methods improve memory accessibility, yet action-relevant information may still fail to guide the current decision because it is poorly formed, organized, prioritized, or presented. We call this post-access failure the Memory-Action Gap. We propose MemArbiter, a function-aware memory arbitration framework that addresses the memory-management-induced component of this gap. MemArbiter decomposes interaction histories into atomic items, organizes them into five functional Memory Banks, and combines bank-level demand, item-level relevance, focal-ambient representations, and a temporal presentation gate to dynamically control memory salience. We evaluate MemArbiter on ALFWorld against Flat Retrieval and Flat Recency under unified per-step memory budgets. With an open-weight action-generation model, MemArbiter achieves success rates of 82.8% and 92.5% under 500- and 750-token budgets, outperforming the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 25.4 percentage points, respectively. It also improves post-failure recovery and reduces failed-action repetition and state-action recurrence. These results show that function-aware memory arbitration enables accessible information to guide actions more effectively.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Solution-Centric Search: Adaptive Inquiry and Knowledge Revision for Autonomous ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02143)
*Shaokang Fu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Jiarong Zhao,Qiming Shi,Tianjun Pan,Haonan Li,Chengyu Wang,Jia Wu,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息范式（information paradigm）以替代传统的解中心搜索（solution-centric search）来组织长时程自主研究任务。作者将其实例化为Iris系统——一个"探究-修订"循环，通过演化信息状态指导解改进，在MLE-Bench上以64.9%的获奖率超越现有方法，并展现了跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的智能体通常通过树、图或链结构组织候选解的改进过程，即搜索过程决定了信息的获取与管理方式（解中心搜索）。然而长时程自主研究任务（如机器学习工程）需要在有限预算下做出相互依赖的决策，这种范式存在局限，因此需要一种以信息为中心的、更符合人类科研认知过程的设计范式。

Method: Iris采用探究-修订循环（inquiry-revision loop），包含两个核心环节：（1）信息获取：从当前信息状态生成局部行动计划，使用认知性动作（epistemic actions）探测决策关键未知量，而不修改已保留的解；（2）信息管理：将跨实验的观测综合为任务知识，由具有明确范围和状态的可修订声明（revisable claims）构成；随着新证据的到来更新知识，并按所需细节层次从原始证据、结构摘要或任务知识构建每个决策上下文。

Result: 在MLE-Bench基准上，Iris在12小时预算下实现了64.9%的任意奖牌率（any-medal rate），为所有对比系统中最高；涵盖四个任务（包括框架工程和模型后训练）展现了跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 信息范式相比解中心搜索更有效地支持长时程自主研究任务，Iris系统验证了该范式的可行性与优越性，为未来自主科研智能体的设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research tasks such as machine learning engineering require systems to make interdependent decisions under a limited budget. Existing LLM-based agents typically organize candidate-solution improvement through tree, graph, or chain structures, meaning that the search process determines how information is acquired and managed. We call this design solution-centric search and propose instead the information paradigm, in which an evolving information state represents the system's understanding of the task and guides solution improvement. We instantiate this paradigm in Iris, an inquiry-revision loop. For information acquisition, Iris generates local action plans from the current information state and uses epistemic actions to probe decision-critical unknowns without modifying the retained solution. For information management, Iris synthesizes observations across experiments into task knowledge composed of revisable claims with explicit scope and status. It updates this knowledge as new evidence arrives and constructs each decision context from raw evidence, structured summaries, or task knowledge at the required level of detail. On MLE-Bench, Iris attains a 64.9% any-medal rate under a 12-hour budget, the highest among compared systems. Across four tasks spanning harness engineering and model post-training, Iris also demonstrates cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond the Mean: Multi-Moment Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02149)
*Yijun Zhang,Yule Xie,Jiaxin Ding,Xin Ding,Fan Xu,Haoxiang Zhang,Luoyi Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于矩（moment）视角的策略优化框架MMPO，将LLM推理中随机采样问题的失败概率视为随机变量，通过联合最小化其多个矩来改善推理能力，并在五个数学推理基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法通常只优化失败概率分布的单阶矩（如期望），未充分刻画其整体分布结构，限制了推理能力的进一步提升。

Method: 提出MMPO（Multi-Moment Policy Optimization）框架，将失败概率视为随机变量并联合最小化其多个矩；同时开发通用矩变换框架，通过系统性诱导不同矩分布来统一刻画更广泛的策略优化目标。该方法具有直观的操作解释：最小化获得首次成功响应所需的期望截断时间。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准和不同规模模型上，MMPO一致性地优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 矩视角为LLM推理策略优化目标的设计提供了新的理论洞察，MMPO验证了多矩联合优化相较于单矩优化的优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Existing methods generally aim to reduce the failure probabilities induced across problems. In this paper, we introduce a moment-based perspective on policy optimization for LLM reasoning by treating the failure probability of a randomly sampled problem as a random variable and characterizing optimization objectives through its moments. Under this perspective, many existing methods optimize only a single moment of the failure-probability distribution, leaving its broader distributional structure largely uncharacterized. We propose \textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{M}oment \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (MMPO), a novel policy optimization framework that jointly minimizes multiple moments of the failure-probability distribution. MMPO admits a direct operational interpretation as minimizing the expected truncated time required to obtain the first successful response. Beyond MMPO, we further develop a general moment-transformation framework that systematically induces different moment profiles and provides a unified view of a broader family of policy optimization objectives. Experiments across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and models of different scales demonstrate that MMPO consistently outperforms strong baselines. We hope this moment-based perspective offers new insights into the design of policy optimization objectives for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [165] [Auditing Data Provenance in LLM Fine-tuning via Intrinsic Distributional Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02154)
*Zirui Huang,Yunlong Mao,Wei Tong,Tingting Wu,Xin Ge,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DPA框架，一种后验的分布来源审计方法，用于检测大型语言模型在微调过程中是否使用了受保护数据进行数据知识产权侵权，在黑盒和恶意混淆（如改写和知识蒸馏）场景下依然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定制化LLM的泛滥使得通过未授权的微调侵犯数据知识产权成为严重风险。现有审计技术需要在数据准备或训练阶段介入，且容易被数据改写和知识蒸馏等恶意混淆手段攻破，亟需一种更鲁棒的后验审计方法。

Method: DPA基于一个关键洞察：无论微调策略如何规避来源追踪，维持模型效用的实际需求迫使模型保留语义内容和词汇形式的基本交集。DPA捕捉这种持久的词汇-语义交集作为内在分布指纹，将审计形式化为统计假设检验，通过无偏输出采样量化这些指纹，以可靠地拒绝"未使用受保护数据"的原假设。

Result: 在医学和法律微调任务上的大量实验表明，DPA持续优于现有基线方法，并且对采用改写和知识蒸馏的对抗性训练者保持鲁棒性。同时揭示了双用途张力：高保真的分布指纹既能实现可靠审计，也可能被利用进行隐私攻击。

Conclusion: DPA提供了一种在黑盒和恶意设置下有效的后验数据IP侵权审计框架，但其高保真指纹带来的双用途矛盾值得进一步关注和权衡。

Abstract: The proliferation of customized Large Language Models (LLMs) poses critical risks of Data Intellectual Property (Data IP) infringement via unauthorized fine-tuning on proprietary data. Existing audit techniques are limited, as they require intervention during data preparation or training and remain fragile under malicious obfuscations such as data paraphrasing and knowledge distillation.
  We propose \textit{Distribution Provenance Audit (DPA)}, a post-hoc framework for auditing data IP infringement in LLM fine-tuning under black-box and malicious settings. DPA is grounded in a critical insight: regardless of fine-tuning tactics to evade provenance, the practical necessity of maintaining utility constrains the model to preserve the fundamental intersection of semantic substance and lexical form. Accordingly, DPA captures this persistent lexical-semantic intersection as intrinsic distributional fingerprints. The framework formulates the audit as a statistical hypothesis test, effectively quantifying these fingerprints via unbiased output sampling to reliably reject the null hypothesis of non-usage.
  Extensive experiments on medical and legal fine-tuning tasks show that DPA consistently outperforms existing baselines, remaining robust against adversarial trainers employing paraphrasing and knowledge distillation. We further highlight a fundamental dual-use tension: the same high-fidelity distributional fingerprints enabling reliable auditing may also facilitate privacy attacks.

</details>


### [166] [From Simple QA to Deep Research: A Verifiable Benchmark Constructed through Iterative Task Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02163)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Haowen Gao,Hao Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含500个深度研究任务的验证性基准，覆盖31个主题和10大类别，通过Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger流水线自动构建，每个任务以有向无环图（DAG）表示原子步骤和检查点，支持细粒度、人类对齐且稳定的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究基准过度依赖专家编写或已有的人类作者材料，而全自动构建难以保证一致性和可追溯的验证。需要一种既能自动扩展又能在任务特定知识基础上进行可靠评估的验证性基准。

Method: 采用迭代式Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger流水线，逐步将简单问题转化为深度研究任务；每个任务表示为原子步骤和检查点的有向无环图（DAG），使查询、DAG和评分标准协同演化；提供三种查询形式以探测深度研究所需的不同互补能力。

Result: 实验表明该基准能清晰区分不同模型和查询类型，基于事实的逐点评分标准支持细粒度、与人类对齐且稳定的评估。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了自动构建验证性深度研究基准的空白，其数据、实现和结果均已公开，为深度研究评估提供了可追溯、一致且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Deep research benchmarks require expert-level tasks and reliable evaluation grounded in task-specific knowledge. Existing benchmarks rely heavily on expert authoring or pre-existing human-authored materials, while fully automatic construction struggles to ensure consistent and traceable verification. To address this gap, we introduce a verifiable benchmark of 500 deep research tasks spanning 31 topics and 10 major categories, with three query forms designed to probe complementary capabilities required for deep research. The benchmark is constructed automatically using an iterative Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger pipeline that progressively transforms simple questions into deep research tasks. Each task is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of atomic steps and associated checkpoints, enabling the query, DAG, and rubrics to evolve together in a controlled manner. Experiments demonstrate that the benchmark clearly discriminates among models and query types, while its fact-grounded pointwise rubrics enable fine-grained, human-aligned, and stable evaluation. Our data, implementation, and results are publicly available.

</details>


### [167] [From Profiling to Synthesis: Benchmarking Implicit Behavioral Alignment in Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02171)
*Jiajia Song,Bobo Li,Haiwen Yi,Zibo Ji,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Min Zhang,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 IBA-Bench，一个基于纵向交互历史评估智能体隐式行为对齐的基准测试，并引入 IBA-Agent 框架来弥补从用户知识到行动执行的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化智能体基准大多依赖静态偏好快照、固定交互日志或预定义用户档案上的问答，无法捕捉用户偏好的动态演变，也忽视了偏好条件化任务执行的需求，即所谓"知识到行动的差距"（knowledge-to-action gap）。

Method: 构建 IBA-Bench 基准，使用包含噪声、隐式线索和时间不一致性的纵向交互历史来评估智能体在满足从历史交互中推断出的隐式用户约束的同时执行任务的能力。同时提出 IBA-Agent 框架，通过广泛检索和轨迹级对齐来调和冲突的优先级。

Result: 实验表明，对最先进的 LLM 智能体而言，有效的个性化仍是重大挑战；所提出的 IBA-Agent 在九个应用领域的复杂场景中显著提升了行为对齐性能。

Conclusion: 个性化智能体的隐式行为对齐是一个尚未被充分解决的研究问题，基于纵向交互历史的评估范式比静态偏好快照更能反映真实场景，IBA-Agent 验证了轨迹级对齐方法的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models have enabled increasingly capable autonomous agents, yet personalization remains critical for making such agents practically useful. Recent benchmarks have begun evaluating personalization in agents, but they largely rely on static preference snapshots, fixed interaction logs, or question answering over predefined user profiles. Such designs fail to capture the complexity of evolving user preferences and neglect preference-conditioned task execution-a discrepancy we term as the knowledge-to-action gap. To address this challenge, we introduce IBA-Bench, a benchmark for implicit behavioral alignment constructed from longitudinal interaction histories that contain noise, implicit cues, and temporal inconsistencies. Unlike prior work, IBA-Bench evaluates whether an agent can execute tasks while satisfying implicit user constraints inferred from historical interactions. We further propose IBA-Agent, an agent framework that reconciles conflicting priorities through broad retrieval and trajectory-level alignment. Experiment results on IBA-Bench show that effective personalization remains a significant challenge for state-of-the-art LLM agents, and the proposed IBA-Agent substantially improves behavioral alignment in complex scenarios across nine application domains.

</details>


### [168] [PAC Approximation and DIRECT Optimization for Parametric Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02184)
*Zhiming Chi,Ying Liu,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,David N. Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化马尔可夫决策过程（pMDPs）的参数综合与优化问题，采用场景方法（scenario approach）采样参数配置并求解线性规划，获得满足PAC保证的多项式近似函数；进一步将该框架与统计模型检测（SMC）结合以支持黑盒参数模型分析，并集成DIRECT全局优化算法，在Lipschitz和PAC好集假设下建立了条件最优性差距保证。在2997个基准上的实验评估表明，DIRECT变体虽解决实例数少于场景优化器，但在共同成功的实例上往往能获得略优目标值且运行更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算pMDP中参数估值到PRCTL属性满足值的映射函数f是一个计算开销巨大的任务，尤其是当最优策略随参数空间变化时。

Method: 采用场景方法：通过采样参数配置并求解线性规划，获得带误差界保证的多项式近似；将该PAC框架与统计模型检测（SMC）结合以处理黑盒参数模型；基于PAC近似集成DIRECT（DIviding RECTangles）算法进行无导数全局优化。

Result: 在2997个基准上，DIRECT变体相比场景优化器解决的实例更少，但在共同成功的实例上通常返回略好的目标值且运行更快，数值接近场景值且在PAC误差范围内。

Conclusion: 在显式Lipschitz和PAC好集假设下，DIRECT找到的值与真实最优值之间的差距受到分区直径项（以及PAC情况下额外的近似误差项）的约束，证明了该优化方法具有条件最优性保证。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the parameter synthesis and optimization problem for parametric Markov decision processes (pMDPs), the extension of classical MDPs where exact probability values are replaced by parametric expressions. Computing the rational function $f_{\lsf}$ that maps parameter valuations to the satisfaction value of a PRCTL property $\lsf$ is a computationally expensive task, particularly for pMDPs where the optimal policy may vary across the parameter space. We adopt the \emph{scenario approach} to efficiently synthesize a probably approximately correct (PAC) approximation $\ApproxFunOfProperty{f}$ of $f_{\lsf}$: by sampling parameter configurations and solving a linear program, we obtain a polynomial approximation whose error margin $\margin$ is guaranteed, with prescribed confidence, for all but an $\errorRate$-fraction of the parameter domain under the sampling distribution. We further show how this PAC framework can be combined with statistical model checking (SMC), enabling the analysis of black-box parametric models. Building on the PAC approximation, we integrate the DIRECT (DIviding RECTangles) algorithm for derivative-free global optimization over the parameter space. We establish conditional optimality-gap guarantees: under explicit Lipschitz and PAC-good-set assumptions, the difference between the true optimum $f_{\lsf}(\parameters^{*})$ and the value found by DIRECT is bounded by a partition-diameter term and, in the PAC case, an additional approximation-error term. An empirical evaluation on 2997 benchmarks focuses on the new DIRECT-based optimization component. The results show that DIRECT variants solve fewer instances than the scenario optimizer, but on their common successful instances they often return slightly better objective values and usually run faster, while remaining close to the scenario values within the PAC margin.

</details>


### [169] [MEGRAG: Multi-Granular Evidence Graphs for Answer-Aware Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02195)
*Weidong Bao,Yingying Sun,Jun Yang,Yilin Wang,Zili Wei,Yubin Bao,Fangling Leng,Minghe Yu,Tiancheng Zhang,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MEGRAG是一个面向多跳问答的答案感知框架，将多跳推理表示为路径结构的多粒度证据图，通过离线跨粒度索引和在线迭代检索，从紧凑三元组到句子/段落逐步补充上下文，并在推理过程中动态判断查询是否已解决，从而提升RAG性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代RAG方法存在两个局限：一是每个推理步骤仅支持单粒度证据，难以平衡信息密度与上下文噪声；二是通常在聚合所有中间步骤证据后才回答原始问题，导致冗余证据和中间检索错误累积，降低最终答案质量。

Method: MEGRAG包含离线与在线两个阶段。离线阶段通过跨粒度索引将段落与其句子和抽取的三元组关联起来。在线阶段，对当前查询检索段落并选择对齐证据，先使用紧凑的三元组，再按需补充句子或段落上下文；利用中间答案和先前推理判断初始查询是否已解决，若未解决则识别缺失信息并构造聚焦的下一个查询，否则停止检索并返回答案。

Result: 在一系列多样化的RAG基线对比中，MEGRAG显示出一致性的性能提升。

Conclusion: MEGRAG通过路径结构的多粒度证据图和动态的查询解决判断机制，有效缓解了单粒度证据和中间检索错误累积的问题，显著提升了多跳RAG的问答效果。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering is a fundamental challenge in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), because deriving an answer requires integrating dispersed evidence. Iterative RAG (iRAG) is widely used for this challenge, but existing methods have two limitations. First, most methods still support each reasoning step with single-granularity evidence, making it difficult to balance information density and contextual noise. Second, existing methods often answer the original question only after aggregating evidence retrieved across intermediate steps, so redundant evidence and intermediate retrieval errors may accumulate and degrade the final answer. To address these limitations, we propose MEGRAG, an answer-aware framework that represents multi-hop reasoning as a path-structured multi-granular evidence graph. Offline, MEGRAG links passages to their sentences and extracted triples through a cross-granularity index. Online, it retrieves passages for the current query and selects aligned evidence, starting with compact triples and adding sentence or passage context as needed. MEGRAG uses the resulting intermediate answer and prior reasoning to decide whether the Initial Query has been resolved. If not, it identifies the missing information and formulates a focused next query; otherwise, it stops retrieval and returns the answer. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains over a diverse set of RAG baselines.

</details>


### [170] [PosterMELD: Multi-Agent Paper-to-Poster Generation for Controllable Design Diversity with Editable Print-Ready Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02218)
*Haojie Hu,Chenhao Dang,Yaojia Liu,Hengrui Kang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PosterMELD是一个基于模板条件的多智能体论文海报生成管道，通过容量感知槽位引导写作、确定性门控与VLM审查实现失败修复，以极低成本（每次请求0.38美元）达到81.3%的打印就绪率（PRR），并提供可编辑的PPTX与PNG输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学术海报生成系统存在两类核心缺陷：1）仅对完整输出评分，隐藏了请求级别的失败，无法真实反映系统可靠性；2）直接图像生成无法进行元素级编辑，而编码智能体（coding-agent）工作流成本高昂。因此需要一种既能保证输出质量（可打印）、又保持原生可编辑性且成本可控的海报生成方案。

Method: PosterMELD采用模板条件化多智能体管道：首先通过容量感知槽位（capacity-aware slots）在渲染前引导文本写作，确定可放置的内容布局；然后使用确定性门控（deterministic gates）结合视觉语言模型（VLM）审查机制，将失败的请求路由到有界修复（bounded repair）流程。每个被接受的请求导出可编辑的PPTX和PNG文件，并支持显式设计控制以生成同一篇论文的多种变体。评估方法包括：PRR（打印就绪率）从几何、可读性、资产完整性和明显事实错误四个维度计数通过请求，并单独报告原生可编辑性；使用冻结VLM对打印就绪输出分配条件性CHE（工艺-和谐-表现力）评分。

Result: 在621篇论文上，PosterMELD达到81.3%的PRR，是P2P的3.4倍、PosterGen的5.2倍；在具备多个打印就绪输出的生成方法中获得最高的条件CHE分数。同时保留原生可编辑性和显式设计控制，平均每次请求成本仅0.38美元，相当于Codex+Skill方法的3.5%。

Conclusion: PosterMELD证明了一条可行的技术路线：通过模板条件约束生成过程、以请求级评估暴露真实失败、用印刷级质量门控保障输出合格率，能够在保持低成本和原生可编辑性的同时显著超越现有方法，为学术海报的自动化生成提供了可靠且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific poster construction compresses a long multimodal paper into a readable, editable canvas. Existing systems hide request-level failures by scoring only completed outputs; direct image generation is not element-editable, while coding-agent workflows are costly. PosterMELD is a template-conditioned multi-agent pipeline: capacity-aware slots guide writing before rendering, and deterministic gates plus vision-language model (VLM) review route failures to bounded repair. Each accepted request exports editable PowerPoint (PPTX) and Portable Network Graphics (PNG) artifacts; explicit design controls yield same-paper variants. Across 621 papers, Print-Ready Rate (PRR) counts requests passing geometric, readability, asset-integrity, and obvious-factual-error checks, with native editability reported separately. A frozen VLM assigns conditional Craftsmanship-Harmony-Expressiveness (CHE) scores to print-ready outputs. PosterMELD attains 81.3% PRR, 3.4 times P2P's rate and 5.2 times PosterGen's, and the highest conditional CHE among generated methods with multiple print-ready outputs. Native editability and explicit design controls are retained at a mean cost of USD 0.38 per request, 3.5% of Codex+Skill's. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/Shannon4Science/PosterMELD.

</details>


### [171] [Trustworthy AI in Digital Health: A Comprehensive Review of Robustness and Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02238)
*Abdullah Mamun,Shovito Barua Soumma,Hassan Ghasemzadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇系统性综述，聚焦数字健康领域中可信AI的鲁棒性与可解释性两大维度，构建了涵盖可信AI支柱、应用场景信任考量及最新技术进展的框架化综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量研究涉及可信AI的不同方面，但针对医疗健康场景下鲁棒性与可解释性的系统性综述仍然缺乏。高风险的数字健康领域迫切需要对这两个关键维度进行聚焦整合，以支持研究人员和从业者开发可靠且可解释的AI解决方案。

Method: 综述文章分为三大部分：首先介绍可信AI的支柱（鲁棒性、可解释性、公平性、问责制、隐私）及其在数字健康中的技术与伦理挑战；其次探讨重症监护、新生儿健康、代谢健康等特定应用领域的信任考量；最后回顾提升数据稀缺和分布偏移下鲁棒性的最新技术，以及从特征归因到梯度解释和反事实解释的可解释AI方法。

Result: 该综述提供了关于数字健康中鲁棒性与可解释性贡献的详细讨论，涵盖LLM时代可信AI系统的发展，以及衡量信任及其相关参数（如有效性、保真度、多样性）的多种评估指标。

Conclusion: 本文通过构建可访问的框架化综述，系统整合了数字健康领域鲁棒性与可解释性的最新进展，为研究人员和从业者提供了技术方案与实践考量，填补了该领域聚焦性综述的空白。

Abstract: Ensuring trust in AI systems is essential for the safe and ethical integration of machine learning systems into high-stakes domains such as digital health. Key dimensions, including robustness, explainability, fairness, accountability, and privacy, need to be addressed throughout the AI lifecycle, from problem formulation and data collection to model deployment and human interaction. While various contributions address different aspects of trustworthy AI, a focused synthesis on robustness and explainability, especially tailored to the healthcare context, remains limited. This review addresses that need by organizing recent advancements into an accessible framework, highlighting both technical and practical considerations. We present a structured overview of methods, challenges, and solutions, aiming to support researchers and practitioners in developing reliable and explainable AI solutions for digital health. This review article is organized into three main parts. First, we introduce the pillars of trustworthy AI and discuss the technical and ethical challenges, particularly in the context of digital health. Second, we explore application-specific trust considerations across domains such as intensive care, neonatal health, and metabolic health, highlighting how robustness and explainability support trust. Lastly, we present recent advancements in techniques aimed at improving robustness under data scarcity and distributional shifts, as well as explainable AI methods ranging from feature attribution to gradient-based interpretations and counterfactual explanations. This paper is further enriched with detailed discussions of the contributions toward robustness and explainability in digital health, the development of trustworthy AI systems in the era of LLMs, and various evaluation metrics for measuring trust and related parameters such as validity, fidelity, and diversity.

</details>


### [172] [Homebot: A Personal AI Agent for Conversational Home Assistance and Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02254)
*Shengyuan Ye,Yixin Zhang,Han Liang,Liekang Zeng,Jiangsu Du,Mu Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Homebot是一个可本地部署的AI代理，用于对话式家务协助与自动化，通过共享运行时统一处理语音和即时消息请求，结合语言模型响应、注册工具与任务特定技能，并区分会话所有权（消息按频道/聊天范围，语音按唤醒词激活边界）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为家庭环境提供可本地部署的对话式AI助手，支持语音和即时消息两种交互方式，同时确保隐私性与可定制性。

Method: 采用共享运行时架构，将通用请求处理与会话所有权分离；语音交互集成本地唤醒词检测、流式语音识别与合成、以及显式对话状态协议（结束、追问或继续）。通过清晰的频道、工具和技能契约支持家务定制。

Result: 提出了一种兼具语音与消息双通道交互、本地可部署、支持工具与技能扩展的家庭自动化AI代理系统设计。

Conclusion: Homebot通过架构分层与明确的接口契约，实现了灵活、可定制且保护隐私的家庭对话式自动化方案。

Abstract: \texttt{Homebot} is a locally deployable AI agent for conversational household assistance and automation. It accepts voice and instant-messaging requests through a shared runtime that combines language-model responses with registered tools and task-specific skills. The design separates common request processing from session ownership: messaging history remains scoped to a channel and chat, whereas voice interaction is bounded by wake-word activation. For hands-free use, \texttt{Homebot} combines local wake-word detection, streaming speech recognition and synthesis, and an explicit dialogue-state protocol for ending, following up, or continuing a conversation. Clear channel, tool, and skill contracts support practical customization for household use.

</details>


### [173] [Self-Certification of Representation Adequacy: Sequential Certification at Minimum Task Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02267)
*Zijie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四层理论框架，用于解决智能体在压缩历史表征下的"表征充分性自我认证"问题。该问题源于表征混淆（aliasing）会导致不可约的每回合损失且智能体可能无法自检。框架包括静态层（贝叶斯风险分组恒等式与一次性外部验证定价）、序列层（将认证建模为以任务损失为货币的最优停止问题，提出 Certification Track-and-Stop 策略）、边界层（给出核切换实例并指出开放性定理）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在压缩表征下行动时存在结构性风险：不同最优动作的历史可能被映射到同一表征（aliasing），导致任何基于该表征的规则都无法避免不可约的每回合损失，且智能体无法从自身转录中检测这一缺陷。因此需要系统化的理论来回答"表征何时足够、如何自我认证、代价几何"。

Method: 四层理论：(1) 静态层：通过贝叶斯风险分组恒等式定义决策理论充分性，用精确全变差阈值定价一次性外部验证；(2) 序列层：将认证形式化为以任务损失为货币的最优停止问题，定义环境级认证复杂度常数（基于覆盖线性规划），对所有 delta 正确策略给出信息-任务损失下界，并提出 Certification Track-and-Stop 策略使代价渐近匹配该下界；(3) 边界层：给出显式核切换反例，识别覆盖策略切换或表征修复所需的关键定理，不声称固定核保证可推广到表征修订。

Result: 推导了贝叶斯风险分组恒等式作为静态充分性的判据；建立了序列认证的最优停止框架并证明信息-任务损失下界；提出的 Certification Track-and-Stop 策略渐近达到该下界；提供核切换反例并明确界定开放问题。两个主要定理在附录中给出完整证明。

Conclusion: 本文为表征充分性的自我认证提供了严谨的决策理论框架，揭示了认证的固有代价（下界）并给出渐近最优策略，同时诚实地划定了方法的适用边界——固定核保证不自动适用于表征修订场景，后者仍需开放定理来解决。

Abstract: Agents that act on a compressed representation of their history face a structural risk: if the representation aliases histories with different optimal actions, no rule measurable with respect to the representation can avoid an irreducible per-round loss, and the agent may be unable to detect this from its own transcript. This paper develops a four-layer theory of self-certification of representation adequacy. The static layer defines decision-theoretic adequacy through a Bayes-risk grouping identity and prices a one-shot external verification by an exact total-variation threshold. The sequential layer poses certification as an optimal-stopping problem in the currency of task loss: we define an environment-wise certification complexity constant through a covering linear program, prove an information-task-loss lower bound for every delta-correct strategy, and give a Certification Track-and-Stop policy whose cost matches the bound asymptotically. A final boundary layer gives an explicit kernel-switching example and identifies the open theorem needed to cover policy switching or representation repair; it does not claim that the fixed-kernel guarantees extend to representation revision. The proofs of the two main theorems are given in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [174] [Harness-R1: Learning to Edit Executable Runtime Harnesses from Agent Failure Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02276)
*Shuai Shao,Kangning Zhang,Qingyao Li,Shijian Wang,Hao Wang,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Yi Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Harness-R1，这是首个通过在线强化学习使失败条件下的、贯穿生命周期的可执行运行时编辑成为可学习能力的方法。它训练一个专用的"harness 工程师"模型，将目标智能体的失败批处理转化为可执行补丁，并通过同批次重跑提供结果奖励来优化编辑策略，仅更新工程师而不改变冻结的目标智能体。在 WebShop、ALFWorld 和 DBBench 上，Harness-R1 将 vanilla Qwen3.5-9B 的成功率从 44.3% 提升至 53.6%（+9.3 个百分点）；对目标智能体直接微调后，目标专属工程师进一步将平均成功率从 59.2% 提升至 64.2%（+5.0 个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体在部署过程中持续积累交互轨迹，但其行为通常是固定的。除了更新模型权重之外，这些轨迹还可以用于改进构建上下文、调解工具、验证动作和恢复执行的智能体 harness（运行时框架）。现有的方法通常使用固定编辑器提出修改，缺乏对实际任务成功率的优化机制，因此需要一种能够从失败中学习并优化 harness 编辑能力的可学习方法。

Method: Harness-R1 采用两阶段训练策略：（1）冷启动监督微调初始化编辑策略；（2）使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行在线强化学习训练。具体而言，一个独立的 9B 工程师模型将目标智能体的失败批处理转换为可验证的可执行补丁，然后对冻结的目标智能体进行同批次重新运行，以实际任务的成功结果作为奖励信号，从而仅更新工程师模型的参数。

Result: 在 WebShop、ALFWorld 和 DBBench 三个基准上，Harness-R1 将 vanilla Qwen3.5-9B 的成功率从 44.3% 提升至 53.6%（相对提升 9.3 个百分点）。在对目标智能体进行直接微调后，目标专属的工程师模型可将平均成功率从 59.2% 进一步提升至 64.2%（相对提升 5.0 个百分点），且这些增益在目标微调前后均保持有效。

Conclusion: Harness-R1 验证了将 harness 编辑作为可学习能力的可行性，其研究成果表明智能体引擎和目标智能体可以共同进化（co-evolving）：由于编辑带来的增益在目标智能体微调前后都成立，这为未来同时优化智能体运行时和智能体模型提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Agents built around large language models continually accumulate interaction trajectories during deployment, yet their behavior typically remains fixed. Beyond updating model weights, these trajectories can improve the agent harness that constructs context, mediates tools, validates actions, and recovers execution. We introduce Harness-R1, the first method, to our knowledge, that makes failure-conditioned, lifecycle-wide editing of an existing executable runtime a learned capability. It post-trains a dedicated harness engineer with online reinforcement learning so that its edits are optimized for the realized task success they produce, rather than proposed by a fixed editor. A separate 9B engineer converts batches of target-agent failures into validated executable patches; fresh same-batch reruns of the frozen target provide outcome rewards, so training updates only the engineer. Cold-start supervised fine-tuning initializes this editing policy, which is then trained online with group-relative policy optimization. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and DBBench, Harness-R1 raises vanilla Qwen3.5-9B success from 44.3% to 53.6% (+9.3 percentage points). After direct target-agent fine-tuning, a target-specific engineer raises the average further from 59.2% to 64.2% (+5.0 points); because these gains hold both before and after fine-tuning the target, Harness-R1 points toward co-evolving the harness engineer and the target agent.

</details>


### [175] [SKT: Skill-Use Training at Scale via Verified Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02287)
*Zelin Tan,Yiqun Zhang,Hao Li,Zhiyao Cui,Hejia Geng,Shao Zhang,Hangfan Zhang,Yang Chen,Xiaosong Wang,Lilong Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Shuyue Hu,Chen Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SKT，一个可验证的数据合成流水线，从大规模智能体技能库中构建技能导向的任务与可执行轨迹，用于提升语言模型智能体的技能使用能力。同时构建了SkillEval基准用于评估，实验表明基于SKT数据进行监督微调能持续提升技能使用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能（skills）已成为赋予语言模型智能体可复用程序性知识的重要机制，然而仅提供技能并不保证当前模型能够有效地识别、应用和协调它们。需要一种方法来提升模型的技能使用能力。

Method: SKT是一个可验证的数据合成流水线：(1) 从大规模技能库中选取合适的单技能和多技能配置；(2) 通过基于规则和基于智能体的验证以及反馈驱动的修复来合成任务；(3) 仅保留成功且充分使用每个所需技能的轨迹。另外使用相同流水线和不相交的测试池构建了SkillEval基准。

Result: 使用2,000个公开技能，SKT生成了4,000个任务包和27,164条可验证轨迹。跨多种模型、基准和智能体框架的实验表明，在SKT生成的轨迹上进行监督微调可持续提升技能使用性能。验证消融、跨框架评估和规模实验进一步证明这些提升依赖于高质量的监督、可扩展到多个智能体接口，并随技能覆盖范围的扩大而增强。

Conclusion: 可验证的数据合成为技能使用训练提供了一种有效且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Agent skills have become an important mechanism for equipping language-model agents with reusable procedural knowledge. However, providing skills alone does not guarantee that current models can effectively identify, apply, and coordinate them. To improve skill-use capabilities, we introduce SKT, a verified data synthesis pipeline that constructs skill-grounded tasks and executable trajectories from large collections of agent skills. SKT selects suitable single-skill and multi-skill configurations, synthesizes tasks through rule-based and agent-based verification with feedback-guided repair, and retains only successful trajectories that substantially use every required skill. Using 2,000 public skills, SKT produces 4,000 task packages and 27,164 verified trajectories. Based on the same pipeline and a disjoint test pool, we further construct SkillEval, a held-out executable benchmark for evaluating skill use. Experiments across diverse models, benchmarks, and agent harnesses show that supervised fine-tuning on SKT-generated trajectories consistently improves skill-use performance. Verification ablations, cross-harness evaluation, and scaling experiments further demonstrate that these gains depend on high-quality supervision, extend beyond a single agent interface, and increase with broader skill coverage. Together, these results establish verified data synthesis as an effective and scalable approach for skill-use training.

</details>


### [176] [Shared Prefixes, Better Credit: Adaptive Routing for Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02291)
*Yiqing Liu,Zihao Wang,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TreeCredit，一种共享前缀信用分配框架，通过状态匹配的下游比较来估计多智能体推理中每个操作符的效用，而非将轨迹级结果直接归因到先前决策。该方法构建共享前缀协作树，基于终端正确性和累积额外成本为状态-操作符对分配后缀信用，训练轻量级成对状态路由器，在推理时动态选择下一个可执行操作符，在六个推理基准上以更低的推理成本获得更好的准确率-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应多智能体推理方法通常使用查询级标签或轨迹级回报来学习路由决策，但这种粗粒度的监督信号无法准确估计多步协作中单个操作符在特定状态条件下的效用，导致信用分配不精确、路由决策次优。

Method: TreeCredit构建共享前缀协作树：从同一中间状态扩展候选操作符，基于完整延续的终端正确性和累积额外成本为每个状态-操作符对分配正确性优先的后缀信用；将这些结构化信用转换为状态局部操作符偏好，训练轻量级成对状态路由器，在推理时动态选择下一个可执行操作符。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，TreeCredit适度提升了准确率，同时大幅降低了推理成本，实现了比代表性多智能体推理方法更好的准确率-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 通过共享前缀信用分配的细粒度监督，TreeCredit能够更精准地估计操作符在特定状态下的效用，从而实现更高效的自适应多智能体推理路由，在准确性和推理成本之间取得更优的平衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning (MAR) improves reasoning reliability through iterative solution exchange and refinement. Existing adaptive MAR methods typically learn routing decisions from query-level labels or trajectory-level returns, but such coarse supervision cannot accurately estimate the state-conditioned utility of individual operators in multi-step collaboration. We propose TreeCredit, a shared-prefix credit assignment framework for efficient adaptive MAR. Its core insight is to estimate operator utility through state-matched downstream comparisons, rather than directly attributing trajectory-level outcomes to preceding decisions. TreeCredit constructs shared-prefix collaboration trees by expanding candidate operators from the same intermediate state and assigns each state--operator pair a correctness-prioritized suffix credit based on the terminal correctness and cumulative additional cost of its complete continuation. These structured credits are converted into state-local operator preferences to train a lightweight pairwise state router, which dynamically selects the next admissible operator during inference. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that TreeCredit modestly improves accuracy while substantially reducing inference cost, achieving a better accuracy--cost trade-off than representative MAR methods.

</details>


### [177] [MechGeo: Autoformalizing and Proving Euclidean Geometry in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02295)
*Hao Shen,Junyu Guo,Tian Cui,Yuxuan Xiao,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MechGeo是一个面向欧几里得几何的Mathlib原生智能体框架，联合解决可靠的自动形式化和认证证明构造。通过GeoFormalizer将非正式问题表示为GeoIR并确定性翻译为Lean 4，GeoProver构造几何证明计划并选择性代数化子目标，所有证明和反例均经Lean内核验证。在43个IMO几何问题上证明了29个，其余14个生成反例并在专家修正后全部证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动形式化和证明构造通常被视为独立任务，导致形式化语句不可靠或证明构造缺乏可信的输入。作者旨在构建一个联合框架，同时解决几何问题的忠实自动形式化与内核认证的证明构造，以弥合非正式数学问题与形式化可验证证明之间的鸿沟。

Method: 框架包含两大核心组件：(1) GeoFormalizer——将非正式问题表示为GeoIR中间表示，确定性翻译为Lean 4，通过结构诊断和语义评估迭代修复候选语句；(2) GeoProver——构造几何证明计划，推导中间引理，通过Lean验证的库选择性地代数化子目标，可使用Singular或SymPy生成代数证书，但所有结果均由Lean内核检查。

Result: 在七个LLM骨干上显示自动形式化显著改进，尤其对直接翻译能力较弱的模型。在43个历史IMO几何问题中，GeoFormalizer生成的形式化语句中29个被GeoProver证明；其余14个构造Lean验证的反例，专家修正后全部得证。加上IMO 2026 Problem 2，构成最大的自动内核检查IMO几何证明集合。在LEAP的Lean-IMO-Bench中14个几何语句中首次证明12个，形式化反驳2个，并证明两个修正后的语句。

Conclusion: 反例引导的诊断、几何推理和认证符号计算构成了可信形式化几何的实用基础，证明了该框架在自动形式化和证明构造联合任务上的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: We present MechGeo, a Mathlib native agentic framework that jointly addresses faithful autoformalization and certified proof construction for Euclidean geometry. In this framework, GeoFormalizer represents informal problems in GeoIR, deterministically translates them into Lean 4, and iteratively repairs candidate statements using structural diagnostics and semantic evaluation. GeoProver constructs geometric proof plans, derives intermediate lemmas, and selectively algebraizes suitable subgoals through a library verified in Lean.
