Conclusion: 联邦TSFM微调中，隐私保护与预测精度可以互补而非相互竞争。DP噪声的隐式正则化效应与本框架的轻量级特性，使得该方案兼具隐私保障、精度提升和边缘部署可行性，为机构隔离场景下的时序预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) such as Chronos have demonstrated strong forecasting capabilities across domains, yet adapting them to institutionally fragmented settings, where data cannot be centralized due to regulatory, competitive, or sovereignty constraints, remains unexplored. We introduce FedChronos, a framework for federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning of an already pre-trained TSFM, a setting that existing federated time-series work has not addressed, since prior methods either pre-train from scratch or align prototypes rather than adapt a fixed backbone. Our approach applies Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to the Chronos-T5 backbone and trains across distributed clients using FedAvg and FedProx, transmitting only lightweight adapter weights (384~KB per round, an 86$\times$ reduction over full-model exchange). We evaluate FedChronos on daily commodity prices from 15 Indian agricultural markets across 9 states, a naturally non-IID federated setting, and find that naïve LoRA fine-tuning overfits substantially on small per-client datasets, dropping below zero-shot performance. We further observe that differential privacy (DP) noise can act as implicit regularization and counteract this overfitting: in our experiments the strongest configuration ($\varepsilon = 5$) reduces mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) by 31% over zero-shot and 26% over the best traditional baseline, while bounding each round's information leakage via per-round $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy. Because the model is compact and the updates are small, the approach also suits edge AI deployments where both the network link and the client device are constrained. Overall, our findings suggest that privacy and accuracy can be complementary rather than competing objectives in federated TSFM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [276] [AlphaG-OPD: Reliability-Gated Sibling Counterfactuals for On-Policy Distillation in Symbolic Alpha Factor Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01303)
*Yaoyu Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaG-OPD，一个结构化的在策略蒸馏框架，将符号Alpha因子挖掘中的终端因子评估转化为局部动作指导，通过三个组件分别决定在何处教学、教学什么内容可靠、以及如何强化教学，在不改变终端奖励、轨迹平衡、反向策略等核心组件的前提下提升跨市场因子挖掘性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成流网络（GFlowNets）虽然能保持对完整表达式多样且与奖励成比例的分布，但其轨迹级目标函数无法在中间状态比较未被选中的兄弟动作。符号Alpha因子发现只能对完整表达式打分，无法为产生该表达式的结构决策提供直接标签，因此缺乏中间状态层面的指导信号。

Method: 提出AlphaG-OPD框架，包含三个设计组件：(I) 决定在何处教学——在当前前向策略访问的部分抽象语法树（AST）状态下暴露语法合法的兄弟节点；(II) 决定什么内容可靠可教——在四个共享后缀下评估三个受支持的兄弟节点，仅当匹配比较展现出足够的胜者一致性和正的实证下置信界（LCB）时，才接受KL有界的目标；(III) 决定教学强度与时长——通过有界回放、分数索引过期和前向梯度平衡来巩固被接受的目标，无需额外的因子评估。终端奖励、轨迹平衡、反向策略、语法和因子池规则均保持不变。

Result: 通过等物理分数的四臂消融实验测试配对教学、可靠性门控和巩固三个组件。在A股CSI300、CSI500、CSI1000以及美股S&P 500上进行验证，完整方法在多个随机种子下展现出强劲的跨市场表现。

Conclusion: AlphaG-OPD通过将终端因子评估转化为局部动作指导，弥补了GFlowNet轨迹级目标无法比较中间状态兄弟动作的不足，在多个市场的数据集上验证了框架的有效性和稳健性。

Abstract: Symbolic alpha factor discovery can score a completed expression, but it provides no direct label for the structural decisions that produced it. Generative flow networks (GFlowNets) preserve a diverse, reward-proportional distribution over complete expressions, yet their trajectory-level objective does not compare unchosen sibling actions at an intermediate state. We introduce AlphaG-OPD, a structural on-policy distillation framework that turns terminal factor evaluations into local action guidance. Its design separates three decisions. Component I determines where to teach by exposing grammar-valid siblings at partial abstract-syntax-tree (AST) states visited by the current forward policy. Component II determines what is reliable enough to teach: it evaluates three supported siblings under four shared suffixes and admits a KL-bounded target only when their matched comparisons exhibit sufficient winner agreement and a positive empirical lower confidence bound (LCB). Component III determines how strongly and for how long to teach by consolidating accepted targets through bounded replay, score-indexed expiry, and forward-gradient balancing, without additional factor evaluations. Terminal reward, Trajectory Balance, the backward policy, grammar, and factor-pool rules remain unchanged. An equal-physical-score four-arm ablation tests paired teaching, reliability gating, and consolidation. Across China's CSI300, CSI500, and CSI1000 and the U.S. S&P 500, the complete method delivers strong cross-market performance over multiple random seeds.

</details>


### [277] [Spatiotemporal Proximal Causal Inference under Hidden Confounding and Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01352)
*Omar Faruque,Pavan Raj Ravi,Jianwu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空近端因果推断框架，通过引入处理诱导近端变量和结果诱导近端变量，在存在隐藏混淆变量和时空干扰的情况下识别因果效应，并利用Transformer编码器和条件互信息判别器实现近端桥函数的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的时空数据常存在隐藏混淆变量和干扰，标准因果识别方法假设给定观测协变量的条件可交换性，这在隐藏混淆同时影响处理和结果时失效。现有方法无法在时空干扰背景下处理隐藏混淆，需要一种能联合刻画局部和邻域水平混淆信息的因果推断框架。

Method: 该方法将近端识别理论扩展到时空设置，引入处理诱导近端变量和结果诱导近端变量以捕获局部与邻域层面的混淆信息，推导时空结果混淆桥函数。理论上在近端排除限制和时空完备性条件下建立桥函数的可识别性，并通过近端g-计算公式泛化恢复潜在结果。实现上采用基于Transformer的时空编码器学习近端变量，配合条件互信息判别器施加排除限制，以及矩匹配网络保证桥函数满足识别方程，并引入稳定加权方案处理处理支持不平衡问题。

Result: 在合成数据集上的实验表明，该方法与基线因果推断方法达到可比性能，并且据作者所知，首次在时空干扰存在且存在隐藏混淆的情况下，通过近端因果推断框架提供了理论上扎实的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为时空数据中的隐藏混淆与干扰问题提供了首个理论有据的近端因果推断解决方案，拓展了近端因果推断理论的适用边界，在气候、环境政策、流行病学和区域经济学等具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from real-world spatiotemporal data is challenging due to hidden confounders and interference. Standard causal identification methods assume conditional exchangeability given observed covariates, which fails whenever hidden confounders affect both treatment and outcomes - a common setting in domains such as climate, environmental policy, epidemiology, and regional economics. In this paper, we propose a novel spatiotemporal proximal causal inference framework that extends proximal identification theory to spatiotemporal settings. The proposed method jointly captures local and neighborhood-level confounding information by introducing treatment- and outcome-inducing proxies, and we derive a spatiotemporal outcome confounding bridge function that identifies the potential outcome without requiring direct recovery of the hidden confounder. We establish the identifiability of this bridge function under proxy exclusion restrictions and a spatiotemporal completeness condition, and show that the resulting estimator recovers the outcome through a proximal generalization of the g-computation formula. To operationalize this identification result, we propose a neural architecture that learns proxies via transformer-based spatiotemporal encoders - coupled with a conditional mutual information critic to enforce exclusion restrictions and a moment-matching network to guarantee that the learned bridge function satisfies the underlying identifying equation. We further introduce a stabilized weighting scheme to address treatment support imbalance. Experiments on synthetic datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves comparable performance to baseline causal inference methods, while providing, to our knowledge, the first theoretically grounded outcomes for the hidden confounding in the presence of spatiotemporal interference through a proximal causal inference framework.

</details>


### [278] [Do Neural Networks Really Beat the Curse of Dimensionality? A Bit-Complexity View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01357)
*Tong Mao,Jinchao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于二进制编码和度量熵的统一逼近框架，以计算比特复杂度而非参数数量来衡量逼近效率，比较了经典方法（多项式逼近、稀疏网格、有限元）与神经网络方法的逼近速率，发现大部分经典方法相对于度量熵决定的固有极限是次优的，且许多神经网络的表面优势源于函数类复杂度的差异而非架构的固有优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逼近理论以参数数量或自由度衡量收敛速率，但实际计算受有限精度约束，参数必须用有限比特位编码。因此逼近效率应以计算比特复杂度来评估，这与底层函数类的度量熵密切相关。作者希望建立统一框架来公平比较不同方法在比特复杂度维度上的逼近表现。

Method: 作者开发了基于二进制编码和度量熵的统一逼近框架，在相同比特复杂度约束下分析经典方法（多项式逼近、稀疏网格、有限元）以及浅层和深层神经网络，并通过具有可比度量熵的函数类来比较各方法的逼近速率。

Result: 以比特衡量复杂度时，大多数经典方法相对度量熵决定的固有极限通常是次优的；没有任何方法在比特复杂度维度上从根本上超越经典方法所达到的逼近阶。许多神经网络的表面优势（如维度无关速率和超收敛现象）源于函数类复杂度差异而非架构优越性，传统维度灾难具有误导性，根本限制是受度量熵支配的比特复杂度灾难。

Conclusion: 当以比特复杂度而非参数数量来评估逼近效率时，应当以度量熵作为底层函数类逼近极限的基准。该方法为公平比较不同逼近技术提供了新视角，并指出应重新审视神经网络优势的本质来源。

Abstract: Traditional approximation theory measures convergence rates in terms of the number of parameters or degrees of freedom. However, practical computation operates under finite precision: parameters must be encoded using a finite number of bits. Therefore, approximation efficiency should be evaluated in terms of computational bit complexity, which is intrinsically connected to the metric entropy of the underlying function class.
  In this work, we develop a unified approximation framework based on binary encoding and metric entropy. We analyze classical methods (including polynomial approximation, sparse grids, and finite elements) as well as shallow and deep neural networks, and compare their approximation rates for function classes with comparable metric entropy. We observe that, when evaluated in terms of bits, most classical methods are in general suboptimal relative to the intrinsic limits dictated by metric entropy, while neural network methods may exhibit different behaviors. We show that when complexity is measured in bits rather than parameters, no method fundamentally exceeds the approximation order achieved by classical approaches.
  Our results also indicate that many seeming advantages of neural networks, including dimension-independent rates and superconvergence phenomena, stem from differences in function class complexity rather than intrinsic architectural superiority. In this sense, the traditional curse of dimensionality can be misleading; the fundamental limitation is instead a curse of bit complexity, governed by metric entropy.

</details>


### [279] [When May a Model Replace the Experiment? Audits, Licenses, and the Price of Trust in Surrogate-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01378)
*Shuangxiu,Ma,Wenhe,Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过数学分析和三个穷尽真值标注的设计任务验证，系统研究了机器学习代理模型在候选筛选和认证中的安全性问题。核心发现：预测精度（R²）不能作为信任锚点，近完美R²也可能导致最差选择；安全的保障来自一个架构规则——预测可用于提议和训练，但所有认证结论必须基于真实评估。作者推导出代理可充当oracle的最小判据（排名保持而非精度），提出选择感知审计方法，并证明审计后的代理可将认证评估成本降低25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学、材料科学和机器学习中的设计活动共享一个瓶颈：评估候选的真实质量需要昂贵的实验、第一性原理模拟或完整训练。代理模型越来越多地被用于评分候选甚至将预测反馈到搜索中，但缺乏对"何时可以安全用预测替代真实评估"的严格理论分析。现有实践可能因预测误差在选择过程中被系统性放大而导致严重后果。

Method: 1) 数学建模：推导代理预测在N个候选中筛选时过度预测的"选择税"上下界；2) 架构规则分析：证明"预测可提议、认证须真实"规则在任何代理假设下都充分，且必要（否则存在确定性自确认失效模式）；3) 提出选择感知审计方法，最优查询复杂度；4) 实证验证：432个代理拟合覆盖六个任务-制度条件，三个穷尽真值标注的设计任务。

Result: 1) 近完美R²与最差选择兼容，N个候选中筛选会使选中候选的过度预测按可量化的"选择税"膨胀，并有匹配的上下界；2) 排名保持（而非精度）是代理可作为oracle的最小判据；3) 审计统计量与部署搜索性能的Spearman秩相关达0.80-0.99，而R²与部署遗憾的秩相关低至0.33；4) 审计筛选将认证oracle成本降低实测25倍。

Conclusion: 预测精度不能作为代理模型可靠性的信任基础；安全的架构规则是将预测限制在提议和训练阶段，所有认证结论必须基于真实评估。信任需要通过选择感知审计来购买，审计后的代理可以大幅降低认证评估成本，同时保持可证明的安全性。

Abstract: Design campaigns in chemistry, materials science, and machine learning share a bottleneck: determining how good a candidate truly is requires an expensive evaluation - an experiment, a first-principles simulation, or a full training run. Machine-learning surrogates that predict these outcomes are increasingly used not only to propose candidates but to grade them, and even to feed their own predictions back into the search as though they were measurements. Through mathematical analysis validated on three exhaustively ground-truthed design tasks, we establish when this practice is safe, what any certificate of safety must cost, and when the substitution provably pays. Predictive accuracy cannot anchor trust: near-perfect R^2 is compatible with worst-possible selections, and screening N candidates inflates the over-prediction at the selected candidate by a quantifiable "selection tax" with matching upper and lower bounds. Safety follows instead from an architectural rule - predictions may propose and train without restriction, but every certified conclusion must rest on true evaluations - which is sufficient with no assumptions on the surrogate, and necessary, since admitting predictions into certification with the standing of measurements opens a deterministic self-confirmation failure mode. We derive the minimal criterion under which a model may act as an oracle (rank preservation, not accuracy), show that trust must be purchased through selection-aware audits that are optimal in query complexity, and prove a dichotomy fixing when audited surrogates cut certified evaluation cost. Across 432 surrogate fits over six task-regime conditions, the audit statistic tracks deployed search performance at Spearman rank correlation 0.80-0.99, while the rank correlation of R^2 with deployed regret falls as low as 0.33; audited screening reduces certified oracle cost by a measured factor of 25.

</details>


### [280] [On the Identifiability of Masked Prediction: Mode Blindness and Mask Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01383)
*Yichao Cai,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了掩码预测（masked prediction）中，条件分布族在何种条件下能确定联合分布的问题。对于具有两个分离全局模态的数据，作者发现答案仅由掩码调度（mask schedule）决定：当掩码调度由大上下文主导时，模型对全局模态权重"失明"，联合分布在总变差距离上可偏移一个常数，而掩码目标函数仅受指数级微扰。作者引入ε-可辨识模量来量化这一现象，并通过信息分解证明低可见性掩码可恢复模态权重敏感性，全掩码项能锚定联合分布。实验从可计算枚举、梯度训练和真实语料三个层面验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码预测是许多自监督学习模型（如BERT类模型）的核心目标，但当前理论尚不清楚：当条件预测的过剩风险接近最优时，是否总能唯一确定数据背后的联合分布？此前的快速混合（rapid-mixing）恢复保证不适用于具有分离全局模态的数据，因此需要新的理论框架来界定掩码预测的可辨识性边界。

Method: 1) 理论上：证明在大上下文掩码主导的调度下，模态权重重加权可在总变差上移动联合分布一个常数，而对掩码目标仅产生e^{-O(可见上下文大小)}的指数级微扰，从而证明此类调度对全局模态权重不可辨识。2) 引入ε-可辨识模量（ε-identifiability modulus）量化"给定过剩风险下最大的分布误差"，证明在指数级过剩风险下该模量仍是宏观量。3) 通过精确信息分解，将模态权重敏感性归结为"给定可见上下文后残余模态不确定性"，进而证明低可见性掩码可恢复敏感性，且正的全掩码质量可在无数据假设下锚定联合分布。4) 实验在三个层面验证：可计算分布上的枚举（验证预测的速率）、梯度训练（复现失明与恢复现象）、真实文本语料（位于两个认证区间之间）。

Result: 大上下文主导的掩码调度对全局模态权重不可辨识：模态加权可在总变差上产生O(1)的分布偏移，而目标函数仅受指数级微扰。ε-可辨识模量在指数级过剩风险下仍为宏观量。低可见性掩码（保留模态不确定性）可恢复模式权重敏感性；正的全掩码质量可在无任何数据假设下完成联合分布的锚定。实验结果表明理论研究与实际训练行为一致，真实自然语言处于两种认证状态之间。

Conclusion: 掩码预测能否确定联合分布由其掩码调度单独决定：大上下文掩码对全局模态权重不可辨识（可辨识性盲区），而低可见性掩码和全掩码项能恢复可辨识性。该工作为自监督掩码预训练提供了精确的可辨识性边界，对设计预训练任务（如掩码比例的选取）具有指导意义。

Abstract: Masked prediction learns representations by fitting a schedule-weighted family of conditional laws, but it remains unclear when near-optimal conditional prediction pins down the underlying joint law. We study this question for data with two well-separated global modes, outside the reach of rapid-mixing recovery guarantees, and show that the answer is decided by the mask schedule alone. Under large-context mode pinning, reweighting the two modes can move the joint law by a constant in total variation while perturbing the masked objective exponentially little in the visible-context size: mask schedules dominated by large contexts are provably blind to the global mode weights. To quantify this, we introduce an $\varepsilon$-identifiability modulus, the largest distributional error consistent with a given excess risk, and prove that it remains macroscopic at an excess risk that is exponentially small. An exact information decomposition pinpoints what restores identifiability: mode-weight sensitivity is governed by the residual mode uncertainty given the visible context. Consequently, low-visibility masks recover this sensitivity, and positive full-mask mass anchors the joint law over all admissible models with no assumption on the data law. Empirically, we test our theory at three levels: enumeration on computable laws verifies the predicted rates, gradient training reproduces both the mode blindness and the recovery, and measurements on real corpora place natural text between the two certified regimes.

</details>


### [281] [TabDPT-Turbo: Efficient In-Context Learning for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01400)
*Rasa Hosseinzadeh,Alex Labach,Zexin Xue,Shuyi Han,Valentin Thomas,Anthony L. Caterini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabDPT-Turbo（即TabDPT v1.2），一种基于行式注意力与长上下文预训练的表格基础模型，在不使用检索机制的情况下实现了与TabDPT v1.1相当的性能，但推理速度提升了数个数量级，成为目前最快的领先表格基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（如基于单元格架构或检索的方法）为追求原始性能而牺牲了推理效率，这在计算资源有限或对推理速度要求高的场景中严重限制了其实际应用。作者希望在不依赖检索的情况下，保持行式注意力的高效性，同时通过长上下文预训练弥补表达能力。

Method: 坚持使用行式注意力架构，引入长上下文预训练以消除对检索机制的需求；结合架构改进和自监督学习（SSL）预训练，并在新采集的、更大规模的真实数据语料上进行训练，最终构建出TabDPT-Turbo模型。

Result: TabDPT-Turbo在TabArena-Lite、CC18和CTR23基准上取得了与TabDPT v1.1相当的默认性能，同时推理速度比其快数个数量级。在实验中，TabDPT-Turbo是领先基础模型中总体最快的，已作为TabDPT v1.2开源发布。

Conclusion: 在不牺牲性能的前提下，通过长上下文预训练+行式注意力的组合可以有效替代检索机制，实现表格基础模型的速度优化。TabDPT-Turbo证明了高效推理与高质量预测可以兼得，为计算受限场景下的表格基础模型应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, driven by in-context learning, have rapidly grown in quality and popularity. However, recent approaches with either cell-based architectures or retrieval have sacrificed efficiency for raw performance, restricting their utility in situations where compute is limited or inference speed is crucial. We adopt an alternate approach, sticking with row-based attention while incorporating long context pre-training to eliminate the need for retrieval. By combining this with architectural improvements and SSL pre-training on a newly-sourced, larger corpus of real data results, we present TabDPT-Turbo, a model that provides comparable default performance to TabDPT v1.1 on TabArena-Lite, CC18, and CTR23, at orders of magnitude faster. In our experiments, TabDPT-Turbo is the fastest model overall among leading foundation models. We have released the new model as TabDPT v1.2 at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/TabDPT-inference.

</details>


### [282] [Cluster-Aware Over-the-Air Federated Learning with Energy-Harvesting Devices: From Global Training to Model Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01426)
*Furkan Bagci,Busra Tegin,Mohammad Kazemi,Tolga M. Duman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的联邦学习框架，用于在带有能量采集设备的无线网络中同时支持全局训练和个性化训练两种模式，利用用户聚类信息进行能量与多样性感知调度，并通过过空口（OTA）同时传输实现多模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在无线边缘网络中面临三大挑战：数据异构分布、通信资源受限和能量供应不稳定。现有研究通常忽略移动设备的聚类结构（即用户自然形成的可联合训练子组），以及能量采集带来的随机性对设备参与调度的影响。需要一个统一框架，既能通过利用聚类信息减少数据偏差来训练更代表性的全局模型，又能利用该偏差训练个性化的簇专属模型。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，包含两种学习模式：(1) 全局训练模式：利用聚类信息指导能量与多样性感知的调度策略，确保被调度的活跃用户提供更具代表性的聚合更新；(2) 个性化模式：利用同一聚类结构定义簇级学习目标和OTA恢复目标，参数服务器通过无线多址信道同时传输训练多个簇专属模型。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的统一框架根据运行模式不同，能够分别提高公平性或个性化性能，同时降低通信开销。

Conclusion: 该统一框架证明了利用用户聚类结构可以在同一个OTA联邦学习系统中灵活实现全局模型代表性与个性化模型定制之间的权衡，兼顾能量采集约束下的调度效率与通信开销优化。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables distributed optimization and learning across decentralized edge devices while preserving data privacy, but its performance is fundamentally constrained by heterogeneous data distributions, limited communication resources, and energy availability. In practical wireless networks, mobile devices (MDs) often exhibit diverse data and learning objectives, naturally forming clusters of users with jointly trainable models. When devices rely on energy harvesting (EH), stochastic energy arrivals further complicate participation and scheduling under communication constraints. In this work, we study over-the-air (OTA) FL with EH MDs under heterogeneous data distributions, and investigate two closely related learning objectives within a unified framework: one aiming for a more representative global model by reducing data bias, and the other learning more personalized cluster-specific models by exploiting this bias. In the global training mode, cluster information guides energy- and diversity-aware scheduling, ensuring that the scheduled active users provide a more representative aggregate update. In the personalization mode, the same cluster structure defines cluster-level learning objectives and OTA recovery targets, enabling the parameter server to train multiple cluster-specific models through simultaneous transmissions over the wireless multiple-access channel. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed unified framework improves fairness or personalization, depending on the operating mode, while reducing communication overhead.

</details>


### [283] [Statistical Mechanics of Learning on Product Wasserstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01434)
*Srinivasa Rao P Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将分布约束（而非视为容量缩减）重新解释为学习内在的几何结构。作者将深度神经网络和变分量子电路形式化为经典/量子Wasserstein流形上的梯度流，并设计了Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo两种算法，在teacher-student问题、图像分类和量子分类器等任务上验证了该几何方法可提升泛化、稳定训练并缓解梯度消失（barren plateaus）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学将权重分布约束视为缩小解空间的限制，从而降低模型容量。作者希望采取相反视角：将预设的权重分布视为学习自然展开的内在几何，而非单纯限制。这既是对分布约束感知机（distribution-constrained perceptrons）工作的延伸，也期望将结构约束重新定义为几何先验，为经典和量子学习系统引入生物学、频谱或硬件相关的分布信息开辟路径。

Method: 1) 将深度神经网络和变分量子电路形式化为Wasserstein流形上的梯度流——每层对应一个经典Wasserstein空间，电路参数对应量子Wasserstein空间（使用阶数为1的量子Wasserstein距离）。2) 开发深度网络的分层平均场（hierarchical mean-field）描述。3) 将框架扩展到量子设置。4) 提出两种实用算法：Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo，沿流形上近似测地线更新。

Result: 在teacher-student问题、标准图像分类任务和小型变分量子分类器上进行实验，结果表明：尊重分布几何相比无约束和纯范数约束的基线方法，能改善泛化性能、稳定训练过程，并降低barren plateaus的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将结构约束重构为几何先验，并展示了将生物、频谱或硬件来源的分布信息融入经典与量子学习系统的方法路径。分布约束带来的"容量缩减"在此几何框架下实为约束流形本身的度量结构属性。

Abstract: Normally the statistical mechanics of learning treats constraints on weight distributions as restrictions that shrink the space of possible solutions. Therefore, it reduces model capacity. In this paper we would like to take a contrary approach, which, however, is based on the earlier work on distribution-constrained perceptrons. Rather than treating a prescribed weight distribution as a mere restriction, we propose that it defines the intrinsic geometry upon which learning naturally unfolds. We formulate both deep neural networks and variational quantum circuits as gradient flows on a product of Wasserstein manifolds -- one classical Wasserstein space for each layer and one quantum Wasserstein space for the circuit parameters. Within this geometry, the capacity reduction, which was previously associated with distributional constraints, appears as the metric structure of the constraint manifold itself. We develop a hierarchical mean-field description for deep networks, extend the framework to the quantum setting using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1, and introduce two such practical algorithms, Hierarchical DisCo-SGD and Quantum DisCo, that follow approximate geodesics on the manifold of the product itself. Experiments on teacher-student problems, standard image classification tasks, and small variational quantum classifiers show that respecting these distributional geometries improves generalization, stabilizes training, and reduces the severity of barren plateaus compared with unconstrained and purely norm-based baselines. This approach firstly reframes structural constraints as geometric priors and suggests a route for incorporating biological, spectral, or hardware-derived distributional information into both learning systems, viz., classical and quantum learning.

</details>


### [284] [Conformalized Large Language Models under Configuration Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01460)
*Yuqicheng Zhu,Jialin Yu,Lin Li,Gengyuan Zhang,Zhen Yang,Steffen Staab,Puneet Dokania,Philip Torr,Jie Tang,Evgeny Kharlamov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在共形预测（CP）中因配置变化（提示模板、解码温度、权重量化）导致的"配置偏移"问题，发现该偏移持续侵蚀CP的覆盖率保证，并提出了基于覆盖率下界的经验诊断方法及两种实用缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测为LLM提供了具有有限样本覆盖率保证的不确定性量化框架，但LLM的非一致性分数由推理管线（而非固定模型）诱导，依赖于提示模板、解码参数、部署设置等可配置因素。这些配置在实践中经常被修改，却很少被视为偏移来源，其对CP有效性的影响尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 沿三个轴（提示模板、解码温度、权重量化）系统研究配置偏移；在9个LLM、4个数据集和4种非一致性分数上进行大规模实证研究；推导覆盖率下界以将覆盖率损失归因于校准分布与测试分布之间的差异，并使用有限样本插件版本作为偏移严重程度的经验诊断；提出两种缓解策略：基于下界的重校准（有限测试样本下有效）和脆弱性感知的校准集成（无需测试数据）。

Result: 配置偏移持续侵蚀CP有效性，常使经验覆盖率低于目标值；效率基本保持，有效预测集规模与i.i.d.基线接近；覆盖率下界可准确归因覆盖率损失；两种缓解策略均能有效恢复覆盖率。

Conclusion: 配置偏移是LLM共形预测中一个此前被忽视但系统性存在的重要问题，作者提供了理论分析、经验诊断工具和实用缓解方案，为在真实部署场景中更可靠地应用CP提供了指导。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) is a distribution-free framework for uncertainty quantification that has recently been adapted to large language models (LLMs), providing prediction sets with finite-sample coverage guarantees under exchangeability. Yet for LLMs, nonconformity scores are often induced by an inference pipeline, not just a fixed model, making them depend not only on the data distribution but also on configurable factors such as the prompt template, decoding parameters, and deployment setting. Since such configurations are routinely modified in practice but rarely treated as a source of shift, their impact on CP validity remains poorly understood. We call this \emph{configuration shift} and study it systematically along three axes: prompt template, decoding temperature, and weight quantization. In a broad empirical study spanning $9$ LLMs, $4$ datasets, and $4$ nonconformity scores, we find that configuration shift consistently erodes CP validity, often driving empirical coverage below the target. By contrast, efficiency is largely preserved: valid prediction sets remain close in size to the i.i.d. baseline. We derive coverage lower bounds that attribute this loss to a discrepancy between calibration and test score distributions, and use their finite-sample plug-in versions as empirical diagnostics of shift severity. We further show that these findings lead to practical mitigations: bound-inspired recalibration is effective with limited test examples, while fragility-aware calibration ensembling recovers much of the lost coverage without test data.

</details>


### [285] [Plasticity of Growing and Elastic Neural Networks in Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01475)
*Jeong Min Kong,Richard S. Sutton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了增长型神经网络和弹性神经网络在在线持续学习中的可塑性保持能力，发现这两种自适应结构调整的网络不仅能维持高预测精度，还能避免可塑性丧失，弹性网络还能在保持紧凑规模的同时实现优秀性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三个关键观察驱动本研究：1）在线持续学习更接近动物的学习方式；2）可塑性丧失（即学习网络学习能力的渐进衰退）是持续学习面临的另一关键挑战；3）增量引入随机初始化的隐藏单元已被证明有助于保持可塑性。此前这些增长/弹性网络主要被用于离线持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，其在在线持续学习中对可塑性的影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在监督学习设置下进行实验，系统性地研究了多种基础的增长型网络（仅生长）和弹性网络（同时生长和剪枝）在在线持续学习中的可塑性表现。具体包括：1）自适应增长网络：增量式添加新的随机初始化单元，同时保持所有现有连接可自适应，衡量其精度保持与可塑性表现；2）自适应弹性网络：在逐步添加新隐藏单元的基础上，在每个新任务开始时剪除估计的死隐藏单元，评估其在精度、可塑性和模型紧凑性三方面的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）自适应增长网络能够在不损失可塑性的同时维持高预测精度，尽管死隐藏单元比例持续上升；2）自适应弹性网络在保持可塑性并实现优秀精度的同时，能够将模型维持在一个近乎恒定且紧凑的尺寸；3）两种网络均验证了结构自适应能力对在线持续学习中可塑性保持的有效性。

Conclusion: 增长型网络和弹性网络能够根据相关学习目标自适应调整其结构，是保持在线持续学习中高可塑性的有前景的算法类别，为持续学习中的可塑性丧失问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Neural networks that can grow or both grow and shrink during learning, referred to as growing neural networks and elastic neural networks, respectively, have recently been explored in offline continual learning with a particular focus on catastrophic forgetting. Driven by the observations that 1) online continual learning closely resembles how animals learn; 2) loss of plasticity---the progressive decline in a learning network's ability to learn---is another crucial challenge facing continual learning; and 3) incremental introduction of randomly initialized hidden units was recently shown to help preserve plasticity, in this paper, we study the plasticity of several foundational growing and elastic networks in online continual learning. Our experiments in supervised learning settings show that adaptive growing networks, which incrementally incorporate new, randomly initialized units to the network while keeping all existing connections adaptive, can maintain high prediction accuracy without losing plasticity despite the continuous increase in the dead hidden unit proportion. Furthermore, we demonstrate that adaptive elastic networks, which in addition to progressively adding new hidden units also prune estimated dead hidden units at the beginning of each new task, can achieve excellent accuracy without loss of plasticity while simultaneously maintaining a near-constant, compact size. Our results suggest that growing and elastic networks, which exhibit the ability to adapt its structure to the relevant learning objectives, can be a promising class of algorithms also for preserving high plasticity in online continual learning.

</details>


### [286] [Interpretable MEG Decoding of Perceived Speech: Cortical Sources and the Stimulus Features That Drive Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01481)
*Ilia Semenkov,Daria Kleeva,Ivan Dakhtin,Zarina Maksudova,Alex Ossadtchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重新设计了一个高性能的MEG到音频检索架构：用球谐函数替代平面传感器布局的空间注意力，将270个分支缩减至25个并添加时间滤波器以匹配神经源，同时使解码器更浅。模型在MEG-MASC上达到39.75%的Top-1准确率（1005个候选中），参数减少约20倍，且权重可映射到源空间，恢复语音感知网络中的发生器。通过MEG遮挡实验发现15/19个刺激特征均有贡献（沉默、声音强度、元音和声学起始影响最大），并显示无叙事结构的活动携带更少可恢复信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP风格训练的MEG-to-audio检索深度网络虽然性能良好，但其权重无法映射到电生理学量，且不清楚哪些语音属性驱动了检索行为。作者旨在通过重新设计架构实现权重可解释的源空间映射，并系统探究驱动检索的语音特征。

Method: 重新设计MEG-to-audio检索架构的前端和解码器：用三维MEG头盔几何上的球谐函数替代扁平传感器布局的空间注意力；将270个分支缩减至25个，每个分支添加时间滤波器以在时空上匹配神经源；使用更浅的卷积解码器。训练前去除眼动和心电成分以避免刺激锁定的捷径。通过MEG遮挡实验和随机词表对照实验分析语音特征贡献，并通过PCA压缩wav2vec目标维度进行消融。

Result: 在MEG-MASC上达到39.75±0.34%的Top-1准确率（1005候选），解码器参数约减少20倍。权重成功映射到源空间，恢复与语音感知网络一致的生成器，左侧化分支携带更高的频率节奏成分。MEG遮挡显示15/19个特征有贡献，沉默、声音强度、元音和声学起始影响最大。随机词表实验中，用叙事MEG替换可提升检索性能。wav2vec目标可压缩至约12个特征维度而不损失精度，但强时间压缩会造成明显损失。

Conclusion: 通过源空间映射和输入干预，论文揭示了MEG检索性能的驱动因素：架构的可解释性（球谐前端+时间滤波分支）既能保持较高检索性能又能映射到神经源，语音刺激的多种声学特征（尤其是静音、强度、元音和声学起始）共同驱动检索，叙事结构对可恢复信息至关重要，且wav2vec特征存在显著的维度冗余但时间信息不可压缩。

Abstract: Short segments of perceived speech can be retrieved from non-invasive magnetoencephalographic (MEG) recordings by deep networks trained with a CLIP-style objective against wav2vec 2.0 audio embeddings. Yet their weights do not map onto electrophysiological quantities, and it remains unclear which speech properties drive retrieval.
  We build on a high-performing MEG-to-audio retrieval architecture but redesign both its front end and decoder. Its spatial attention operates on a flattened sensor layout; we replace it with spherical harmonics defined on the three-dimensional MEG helmet geometry. We reduce the subject-specific representation from 270 to 25 branches, add a temporal filter to each branch to match it to a neuronal source in space and time, and make the convolutional decoder shallower. Ocular and cardiac components are removed before training to reduce the risk of stimulus-locked shortcuts.
  On MEG-MASC, the model reaches 39.75 +/- 0.34% Top-1 accuracy among 1005 candidates across six trained solutions, with about 20 times fewer decoder parameters. Its weights map to source space, recovering generators consistent with the speech-perception network, while left-lateralized branches carry higher-frequency rhythmic components not evident on the right. Paired MEG occlusion shows that 15 of 19 stimulus features contribute, with the largest effects for silence, sound intensity, vowels, and acoustic onsets. Random word lists behave oppositely: substituting narrative MEG into them improves retrieval, indicating that activity without narrative structure carries less recoverable information than activity during coherent speech. The wav2vec target can be reduced to about twelve learned feature dimensions without loss of accuracy, whereas strong temporal compression causes a clear loss.
  Together, source mapping and input interventions reveal what drives retrieval.

</details>


### [287] [BiKAN: Restoring Collapsed Basis of Binary Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01490)
*Kazi Ahmed Asif Fuad,Lizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiKAN通过向二值化KAN层添加Walsh特征来解决"空间正交性坍缩"问题——即当激活值被限制为±1时，多项式的偶次幂坍缩为常数、奇次幂坍缩为线性，导致层级函数空间退化的结构性问题。该方法在CIFAR-10上提升3.09个点，并在W1A1量化下达到MNIST 99.48%、CIFAR-10 84.38%、CIFAR-100 55.81%的准确率，同时保持了硬件高效性（FPGA上DSP消耗从164降至72）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值化KAN改变了参数精度，更关键的是改变了每层可用的函数空间。当激活被限制为±1时，多项式基函数发生坍缩：所有偶次幂变为常数1，所有奇次幂变为x，使得逐元素多项式基退化为常数和一阶响应，丧失表达能力。作者将此结构性问题定义为"空间正交性坍缩"，并需要提出针对性修复方案。

Method: BiKAN在每个二值KAN层中增补选定的2阶Walsh特征（即成对奇偶校验字符）。通过固定的循环通道翻转生成成对奇偶性，利用学习到的二值投影通过XNOR-popcount操作进行混合，与其余W1A1路径保持一致。该方法恢复了显式的成对坐标而不依赖学习路由或乘法器特征生成，并在FPGA硬件上实现了卷积设计和幂2感知的稠密设计两种实现。

Result: 实验表明：去除奇偶校验会使CIFAR-10准确率降低1.23个点（五组配对种子，p=0.003）；增益随宽度减小而增大；准确率随奇偶平面增多单调提升；在相同约11.9M参数预算下，奇偶校验优于传统宽度扩展3.09个点（p<0.0001）。BiKAN在W1A1下达到MNIST 99.48%、CIFAR-10 84.38%、CIFAR-100 55.81%的准确率。FPGA实现中，卷积设计将DSP使用从164降至72，计算核心延迟从401ms降至54.8ms；幂2感知稠密设计实现零DSP推理，仅损失0.03点准确率。

Conclusion: BiKAN通过引入2阶Walsh特征有效修复了二值KAN的空间正交性坍缩问题，在不增加计算复杂度的前提下显著提升了二值化KAN的表达能力和准确率，并在FPGA上验证了其硬件高效性，为二值化KAN的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Binarizing a polynomial Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (KAN) not only changes parameter precision, but also alters the function space available to each layer. When activations are restricted to ${-1,+1}$, all even powers reduce to $1$ and all odd powers reduce to $x$, causing the elementwise polynomial basis to collapse to constant and first-order responses. We refer to this structural failure as Spatial Orthogonality Collapse. Our proposed BiKAN addresses this critical issue by augmenting each binary KAN layer with selected degree-2 Walsh characters. Fixed circular channel rolls generate pairwise parities, and learned binary projections mix them using the same XNOR--popcount operations as the remaining W1A1 paths. This restores explicit pairwise coordinates without learned routing or multiplier-based feature generation. Experiments on CIFAR-10 confirms that removing parity reduces accuracy by $1.23$ points over five paired seeds ($p=0.003$), the gain increases as width decreases, and accuracy improves monotonically as more parity planes are added. At an equal $\sim$11.9M-parameter budget, parity outperforms conventional widening by $3.09$ points ($p<10^{-4}$). At W1A1, BiKAN reaches $99.48\%$, $84.38\%$, and $55.81\%$ on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100, respectively. Post-route Zynq-7020 FPGA results show that the repair remains hardware-efficient; the convolutional design cuts DSP usage from 164 to 72 and estimated compute-core latency from 401 to 54.8 ms, while the power-of-two-aware dense design achieves zero-DSP inference with a 0.03-point accuracy loss. The BiKAN implementation is available at https://github.com/OSU-STARLAB/BiKAN.

</details>


### [288] [Question Begets Question: Self-Evolving Curriculum for Reinforcement Fine-Tuning on Competition Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01522)
*Longtian Bao,Jianyou Wang,Yang Zhang,Youze Zheng,Ramamohan Paturi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对语言模型技能学习中的三大障碍（数据稀缺、缺少真值推理轨迹、数据量增加不再带来改进的瓶颈），提出"Question-begets-Question"(QbQ)自演化课程学习方法，通过教师模型将问题变换为多样变体，仅用强化学习基于问题和最终答案训练（不依赖教师推理轨迹），并在自演化课程中每次从模型能答对的问题中生成QbQ变体，突破了数据扩充性能封顶的问题，将AIME竞赛数学pass@1从5.6%提升至16.5%且20轮后未见饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型学习新技能时面临三大反复出现的困难：训练数据稀缺、真值推理轨迹通常不可用、模型存在明显的数据瓶颈（继续增加数据不再带来改进）。作者希望在受控环境下研究并克服这些困难。

Method: 1）提出QbQ（Question-begets-Question）可扩展流程：教师模型将现有问题转换为探测相同底层技能的不同变体以解决数据稀缺；2）仅通过强化学习在问题陈述和最终答案上训练，完全不使用教师推理轨迹；3）提出自演化课程（self-evolving curriculum）：每轮评估当前检查点，从模型能大部分答对的问题中生成QbQ种子，再在生成变体上训练，在相同数据预算下迭代20轮。

Result: 静态训练（真实+合成增广数据）封顶pass@1为12.5%，非课程式QbQ合成数据训练封顶14.5%；而自演化课程方法在相同数据预算下将pass@1提升至16.5%，且20轮后无饱和迹象。反直觉的发现是：在模型能大部分答对问题的变体上训练能提升性能，且模型能解决训练中未见过的更难问题。

Conclusion: 模型的数据瓶颈并非模型内在固有的，通过自演化课程结合QbQ可以在相同数据预算下突破静态训练的封顶效应并持续提升性能，为缺乏推理轨迹的稀缺数据任务提供了一种可扩展的训练范式。

Abstract: Teaching a language model a skill it has not mastered is obstructed by three recurring difficulties: training data is scarce, ground-truth reasoning traces are usually unavailable, and models often exhibit an apparent ceiling beyond which additional data yields no further improvement. We study these difficulties in a controlled setting, fine-tuning Qwen2.5-Math-7B on competition mathematics (AIME), a task on which it initially solves only 5.6\% of problems (pass@1). To address data scarcity, we introduce Question-begets-Question (QbQ), a scalable procedure in which a teacher transforms existing problems into diverse variants that probe the same underlying skills; to model the absence of oracle reasoning, we train exclusively via reinforcement learning on problem statements and final answers, never on teacher reasoning traces. Static training on such data, however, plateaus well short of the task: real-plus-synthetic augmentation and non-curriculum QbQ generated synthetic data training cap pass@1 at 12.5\% and 14.5\% respectively, despite large increases in data. Our central finding is that this ceiling is not intrinsic to the model. We propose a self-evolving curriculum that, each round, evaluates the current checkpoint, seeds QbQ from the problems it can mostly get right, and trains on the resulting variants; under an identical data budget, this breaks the ceiling and lifts pass@1 to 16.5\% with no sign of saturation after 20 rounds. Counterintuitively, we find that models improve when trained on variants of problems they can mostly get right, and that models trained this way go on to solve harder problems never seen during training.

</details>


### [289] [Gram-Space: Structure-Preserving Codebook Compression for Memory-Efficient Neuro-Symbolic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01528)
*Weilun Wang,Wantong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gram-Space压缩框架，通过Gram-Schmidt正交化将VSA码本向量表示为紧凑的正交坐标系，在保持点积结构不变的同时显著降低GPU内存占用（最多15.75x）并提升推理速度（最多3.62x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VSA广泛应用于神经符号AI推理，但其高维码本会造成严重的内存瓶颈，限制了模型的可扩展性和部署。现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 应用Gram-Schmidt正交化将码本向量表示为紧凑的正交归一坐标系，通过保持内积结构来保证基于矩阵的VSA算子（矩阵相似度、概率向量化、注意力分数计算）在数值上等价执行，并提供内积在正交基表示下不变性的正确性分析。

Result: 在标准神经符号推理数据集上的基准测试显示，Gram-Space在多个先进VSA模型上降低了最多15.75x的模型级GPU内存占用，提升了最多3.62x的推理延迟；性能剖析表明其在码本相关阶段减少了分配密集的开销并提升了NeSy工作负载的硬件利用率。

Conclusion: Gram-Space通过正交化压缩有效解决了VSA码本导致的内存瓶颈，在不损失数值精度的情况下大幅提升资源效率与推理性能，为神经符号AI的可扩展部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vector symbolic architectures (VSA) are widely used for reasoning in neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI, yet high-dimensional codebooks often create severe memory bottlenecks that limit scalability and deployment. In this paper, we propose Gram-Space, a compression framework that applies Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization to represent codebook vectors in a compact orthonormal coordinate system. Gram-Space preserves the dot-product structure required by matrix-based VSA operators, which supports numerically equivalent execution of matrix similarity, probability vectorization, and attention score computations. We provide a correctness analysis showing that inner products are preserved under the orthonormal basis representation. Using modern GPU hardware, we benchmark the Gram-Space framework on standard neuro-symbolic reasoning datasets. Experimental evaluations across state-of-the-art VSA models show that Gram-Space reduces model-level GPU memory usage by up to 15.75x and improves inference latency by up to 3.62x. Profiling results further indicate that Gram-Space reduces allocation-heavy overhead in codebook-associated stages and improves hardware utilization for NeSy workloads.

</details>


### [290] [Rethinking Personalized Reward Modeling for LLMs under Preference Heterogeneity via Group-Debiased Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01556)
*Seongyoon Kim,Boryeong Cho,Jihwan Oh,Seokhyun Chung,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGD方法，证明在偏好数据异质的联邦奖励模型学习中，单个共享模型通过FedAvg即可超越按真实分组训练的多个模型，关键在于共享初始化的"平坦性"；FedGD通过组去偏的客户端采样消除组不平衡的影响，无需预知分组即可获高度可适配的初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户偏好数据敏感无法集中，联邦学习可保持数据本地化，但用户偏好存在异质性（同一响应对不同用户可能得到相反的标签）。现有方法假设偏好分组需要各自独立的初始化，本文旨在验证这一假设是否必要，并解决组不平衡带来的挑战。

Method: 1) 理论/实验分析：在偏好组平衡的条件下，单个FedAvg模型尽管初始准确率近乎随机，但在少量本地优化步骤后即可超越为每个真实组单独训练的奖励模型；将这一现象归因于共享初始化的平坦性——跨所有客户端的平均学习获得了更丰富的共享表征，同时抵消了冲突的偏好方向，使模型保持在可快速适配的决策边界附近。2) 提出FedGD：在联邦训练过程中自动发现潜在偏好组，通过组去偏的客户端采样学习单一奖励模型，抵消组不平衡的影响。

Result: 平衡偏好组下FedAvg优于分组独立训练；组不平衡会破坏取消效应导致少数群体远离决策边界、难以恢复；FedGD通过学习组去偏采样克服组不平衡，获得高度可适配的初始化，实现无需预知分组的有效个性化。

Conclusion: 偏好分组的独立初始化并非必要；共享初始化的平坦性是关键，组去偏的联邦训练可在不平衡偏好群体下依然保持初始化适配性，实现有效的联邦个性化奖励模型学习。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly aligned to human preferences via reward modeling, but user preference data are sensitive and often cannot be centralized. Federated learning keeps such data local while learning a shared initial reward model, which is later personalized for each client through local fine-tuning. Because users often assign opposite labels to the same pair of responses, existing federated methods address preference heterogeneity by clustering similar clients and training one reward model per group, assuming that each group requires its own initialization. We show that this assumption is unnecessary. Under balanced preference groups, a single FedAvg model, despite starting at nearly random accuracy, surpasses reward models trained separately for each ground-truth group after only a few local optimization steps. We attribute this phenomenon to the flatness of the shared initialization: averaging across all clients learns richer shared representations that distinguish responses while canceling conflicting preference directions, leaving the model near a decision boundary that can be rapidly adapted. Group imbalance breaks this effect as the cancellation becomes asymmetric and leaves minority clients too far from the boundary to recover. Motivated by this observation, we propose FedGD (Federated Learning with Group Debiasing), which discovers latent preference groups during federated training and learns a single reward model using group-debiased client sampling. By counteracting the effect of group imbalance, FedGD learns an initialization that remains highly adaptable, enabling effective personalization without prior knowledge of the underlying groups.

</details>


### [291] [Meganeura: Portable GPU Training and Inference through Vulkan and Metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01563)
*Dzmitry Malyshau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Meganeura是一个紧凑的原生编译器，通过Vulkan和Metal将训练与部署统一在消费级GPU上。在50个设备-工作负载-模式组合中48个通过验证，严格f32下12/20的GPU延迟单元胜出，推理差距中位数1.8x，编译时间0.1-2.4秒（远优于torch.compile的6-96秒），二进制仅13MiB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练与部署推理通常跨越导出、转换和平台专属运行时等多个边界，导致工具链碎片化和效率损失。作者希望探究是否可以用一个紧凑的原生编译器同时覆盖训练和部署两个阶段，在消费级GPU上运行。

Method: Meganeura包含类型化静态图、自动微分、优化器、检查点、内存规划和运行时，通过Vulkan和Metal两个通用图形API执行。使用五个匹配的工作负载与PyTorch对比，覆盖NVIDIA/AMD独立GPU、AMD APU、Apple silicon和Intel iGPU，协议将严格f32与验证过的快速路径分离，并独立门控前向与反向传播。

Result: 50个单元中48个通过双门控，另2个仅在新支持的APU上存在一个未解决的反向引用分歧。严格f32下12/20个GPU最小延迟单元获胜，训练差距中位数1.8x；离散AMD GPU上4/5推理工作在1.10x以内，3个训练工作更快。加速契约下最差训练差距4.6x。编译耗时0.1-2.4秒（vs torch.compile的6-96秒），剥离后二进制13MiB。瓶颈主要定位在卷积导数与注意力反向传播。

Conclusion: 通用消费级图形API可以支撑一个紧凑的训练到部署共享栈，达到有用且部分与厂商竞争的性能水准。观测到的差距源于内核覆盖、调度和算术策略，而非API本身的根本性限制。Android XR案例研究进一步证明了将Meganeura训练的解码器迁移到Adreno/OpenXR应用的物理可行性。

Abstract: Training and deployed inference often cross export, conversion, and platform-specific runtime boundaries. Meganeura asks whether one compact native compiler can span both phases on consumer GPUs. Its typed static graph, automatic differentiation, optimizer, checkpoint, memory planner, and runtime lower specialized programs through Vulkan and Metal.
  We compare five matched workloads with PyTorch on NVIDIA and AMD discrete GPUs, an AMD APU, Apple silicon, and an Intel iGPU. The protocol separates strict f32 from validated fast paths and gates forward and backward independently. Forty-eight of 50 device-workload-mode cells pass both gates; the other two share one unresolved backward-reference disagreement on a newly supported APU. In strict f32, Meganeura wins 12 of 20 GPU-referenced minimal-latency cells and has a median valid training gap of 1.8x. On the discrete AMD GPU, four of five inference workloads are within 1.10x of compiled ROCm PyTorch and three training workloads are faster. Under accelerated contracts, the worst training gap is 4.6x.
  Compilation takes 0.1-2.4 seconds versus 6-96 seconds for torch.compile on supported GPU paths; the stripped binary is 13 MiB. Dispatch profiles localize the largest gaps to convolution derivatives and attention backward. A physical Android XR case study transfers a Meganeura-trained decoder into an Adreno/OpenXR application sharing the graphics queue. The results show that general consumer graphics APIs can support a compact shared train-to-deploy stack at useful, sometimes vendor-competitive performance. The measured gaps point to kernel coverage, scheduling, and arithmetic policy rather than an identified API limitation.

</details>


### [292] [Measuring in-context algorithmic reasoning in language models against an exact Bayes-optimal standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01575)
*Hector Zenil,Luan Ozelim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F-ICL基准，基于图灵完备机器sF穷举15亿个程序并计算贝叶斯最优后验，用于检测大语言模型的真实算法推理能力。在37个开放模型和前沿系统的105种配置中，模型虽然能答对高达92%的查询，但其分布与最优解的差距甚至大于无循环的"打印机"参考基线，且呈现非单调更新行为（6545次已解转未解），说明模型更多在做模式补全而非真正的算法推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏正确归纳推理的真值（ground truth），难以区分大语言模型是进行真正的算法推理还是仅仅完成模式补全。作者希望通过提供一个具有精确理论最优解的基准来检验模型的真实推理能力。

Method: 搭建F-ICL基准：使用图灵完备机器F并加以互补对称化为sF以消除输出极性偏差；穷举所有长度L≤13的15亿个程序，在Levin-Solomonoff有界先验下闭式计算贝叶斯最优后验；每个任务配对其按位补码版本以隔离模型的归纳偏差；通过分布距离、准确率和更新单调性等指标对模型进行评估。

Result: 45/46个模型的输出分布距离最优解的差距比一个按键参考（keystroke reference）还大；模型行为被仅基于可见证据的低阶前缀统计所框定；更新非单调（6545次已解转未解对13702次增益）；差距与准确率无关（ρ=-0.19, p=0.21），不随规模扩大而缩小，也不随前沿模型代际更替而改善，且指令和推理后训练会加剧这一差距。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的上下文学习行为更接近无循环的模式匹配（loop-free mixture），而非真正的贝叶斯最优算法推理。F-ICL作为开放的可复现基准和工具包发布，为评估和改善LLM的算法推理能力提供了精确的参照基准。

Abstract: Whether large language models perform genuine algorithmic reasoning or mere pattern completion is hard to test, because most benchmarks lack a ground truth for correct inductive inference. We introduce F-ICL, an in-context-learning benchmark that supplies one exactly. Using the Turing-complete machine F, complement-symmetrised into sF to remove output-polarity bias, we exhaustively enumerate all 1.5 billion programs of length $L\le13$ and compute the Bayes-optimal posterior in closed form under a bounded universal (Levin--Solomonoff) prior; models are scored by how closely their served distributions approach it at matched evidence. Each task is paired with its bitwise complement, on which the optimum scores identically, so an original-twin gap isolates the model's inductive bias. Across 105 serving configurations spanning 37 open models (0.8B--675B) and frontier systems from four laboratories, models answer up to 92\% of queries correctly, yet 45 of 46 models yield distributions farther from the optimum than a keystroke reference, and their behaviour is bracketed by low-order prefix statistics fitted only on visible evidence. That reference is itself an algorithmic mixture, induced by a print-only machine with no loops, so the panel's implied measure sits closer to a loop-free mixture than to the loop-bearing optimum, independently of the reference machine. Updating is also non-monotone, which no prior explains: a Bayes-rational solved set can only grow in this realisable, noiseless setting, yet added examples produce $6{,}545$ solved-to-unsolved transitions against $13{,}702$ gains. The gap is not predicted by accuracy (Spearman $ρ=-0.19$, $p=0.21$), does not close with scale or across frontier generations in the serving modes that expose distributions, and is widened by instruction and reasoning post-training. F-ICL is released as an open, reproducible benchmark and toolkit.

</details>


### [293] [HindSearch: Trajectory-Level Hindsight Critique for Search-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01597)
*Haowei Liu,Jiamian Wang,Hsin-Tai Wu,Zhiqiang Tao,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HindSearch是一种针对GRPO的 hindsight self-distillation 方法：每次rollout后，冻结的judge基于gold answer为每个失败轨迹撰写简短critique，并将critique作为辅助的on-policy蒸馏信号作用于学生的搜索动作。在七个基准测试套件上，以Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct为底座达到39.4%的平均EM，超过此前搜索RL基线。消融实验表明去掉judge对gold answer的访问会抹去大部分收益，验证了hindsight是改进来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强的语言模型智能体通常使用二值精确匹配（exact-match）奖励进行训练，这种粗粒度奖励浪费了失败轨迹中关于"为何失败"的丰富信息。作者旨在利用失败轨迹中的hindsight信息来提供更密集、更有指导性的训练信号。

Method: 提出HindSearch方法：结合GRPO框架，在每次rollout后由一个冻结的judge模型基于gold answer为每条失败轨迹生成简短critique，这些critique作为auxiliary on-policy distillation信号，用于监督学生模型的搜索动作，从而在训练中提供比二值奖励更细粒度的反馈。

Result: 在标准七项基准套件上，以Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct为backbone，HindSearch达到39.4%的平均EM，优于先前搜索RL基线。消融实验证明，移除judge对gold answer的访问后大部分性能增益消失，证实hindsight信息是提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: 通过将失败轨迹转化为hindsight critique并作为额外蒸馏信号，能够显著提升搜索增强LM智能体的训练效果。失败并非无用的信号，利用后见之明可以充分挖掘其教学价值，且这种提升确实源于对成功标准的后验理解而非其他因素。

Abstract: Search-augmented LM agents are typically trained with a binary exact-match reward, which throws away most of what a failed trajectory tells us about why it failed. We introduce HindSearch, a hindsight self-distillation procedure for GRPO: after each rollout, a frozen judge writes a short critique of every failed trajectory using the gold answer, and the critique supplies an auxiliary on-policy distillation signal on the student's search actions. On the standard seven-benchmark suite with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, HindSearch reaches 39.4% average EM, outperforming prior search-RL baselines. Removing the judge's access to the gold answer erases most of the gain, isolating hindsight as the source of the improvement.

</details>


### [294] [Latent-Regime Bias Auditing for Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01599)
*Arthur Chagas,Pedro Bento,Yan Aquino,Arthur Buzelin,Wagner Meira,Cristiano Arbex Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的审计框架，用于评估波动率预测在潜在市场状态（regime）下的条件可靠性。通过时间序列表示学习对市场状态窗口进行聚类，并在样本外分配状态，对比总体聚合误差与条件偏差、尾部低估和损失指标。实验发现，具有竞争性总体精度的模型仍可能表现出显著的状态特定偏差和严重尾部低估，主张预测评估应关注"在哪里"和"如何"失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的波动率预测评估依赖RMSE、MAE等总体精度指标，这些指标会掩盖对风险管理至关重要的条件失效（conditional failures）。现有评估方法缺乏对潜在市场状态下预测可靠性的诊断能力，无法回答"模型在哪些市场环境下不可靠"这一关键问题。

Method: 1) 学习市场状态窗口的时间序列表示；2) 仅使用训练信息将表示聚类为潜在状态；3) 在样本外为观测分配所属状态；4) 对比总体聚合预测行为与状态条件偏差（conditional bias）、尾部低估（tail-underprediction）以及对低估敏感的逐状态经济损失（underprediction-sensitive economic losses）。

Result: 在加密货币和ETF资产的日度波动率预测中，具有竞争性总体精度的模型仍展现出显著的状态特定偏差和严重的尾部低估问题。总体指标无法捕捉到的条件失效在状态层面清晰显现。

Conclusion: 波动率预测的评估不应仅关注平均误差，更应关注预测在何处以及如何变得不可靠。该框架将评估范式从"哪个模型总体最准确"转向"识别那些表面准确但在特定市场状态下条件失效的模型"。

Abstract: Volatility forecasts are commonly evaluated with aggregate accuracy metrics such as RMSE and MAE, but these metrics can hide conditional failures that matter for risk management. This paper proposes a model-agnostic audit framework for evaluating whether volatility forecasts remain reliable across latent market regimes. We learn time-series representations of market-state windows, cluster them into regimes using only training information, assign regimes out of sample, and compare aggregate forecast behavior with regime-conditional bias, tail-underprediction, and underprediction-sensitive economic losses. Applied to daily volatility forecasting across cryptocurrency and ETF assets, the audit shows that models with competitive aggregate accuracy can still exhibit substantial regime-specific bias and severe tail underprediction. The results suggest that volatility forecasting should be evaluated not only by average error, but also by where and how forecasts become unreliable. Our framework shifts forecast evaluation from asking which model is most accurate on average to identifying the market regimes in which apparently accurate forecasts fail conditionally. Reproducibility: https://github.com/arthurchagas1/Latent-Regime-Bias-Auditing-for-Volatility-Forecasting

</details>


### [295] [Online Algorithms via Minimax and Posterior Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01616)
*Thomas Kesselheim,Marco Molinaro,Kalen Patton,Sahil Singla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于极小极大视角和后验匹配（posterior matching）原理的统一方法，将在线算法的竞争比分析转化为贝叶斯在线设计问题，并为多个经典在线分数问题（如集合覆盖、负载均衡、匹配等）提供最优或近似最优的竞争比保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线算法的竞争比分析中，上界通常高度依赖具体问题而定，缺乏统一的通用方法论。作者希望通过极小极大视角（结合Yao原理）建立一种可复用的分析框架，减少对具体问题结构的依赖。

Method: 利用Yao原理将最坏情况竞争比分析约化为任意相关先验下的贝叶斯在线设计问题。定义X*为离线最优分数解，X^(t)为其后验过程（鞅）。核心原则为后验匹配：在每个时刻选择在在线约束下最接近当前后验X^(t)的可行在线动作。分析上，将竞争保证转化为由离线最优解后验所生成向量鞅的概率不等式。

Result: 该方法为集合覆盖、负载均衡、匹配及更一般的资源分配问题恢复或改进了现有最优竞争比界（含范数/凹目标）；通过取整约化，还得到了加权分页、星度量上的MTS以及滑雪租赁问题的随机整数算法保证。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一条从任意相关先验下的贝叶斯在线设计到信息论意义下最坏情况竞争保证的可复用路径，为在线算法分析提供了统一且普适的工具。

Abstract: Competitive analysis is central to the study of online algorithms, but upper bounds are often highly problem-specific. We develop a more unifying methodology via the minimax viewpoint. Guided by Yao's principle, we reduce worst-case competitive analysis to Bayesian online design under an arbitrary correlated prior over arrival sequences. For such a prior, let $X^*$ be the hindsight-optimal fractional solution for the realized instance, and let $X^{(t)}=\mathbb E[X^*\mid \mathcal F_t]$ be its posterior process. Our guiding rule is posterior matching: at each time $t$, choose the feasible online action that tracks the current posterior $X^{(t)}$ as closely as the online constraints permit.
  We show that this single principle yields optimal or near-optimal guarantees for several classical online fractional problems, including set cover, load balancing, matching and more general resource-allocation problems, recovering or improving state-of-the-art bounds in these settings with norm/concave objectives. Via known rounding reductions, it also yields randomized integral guarantees for weighted paging, MTS on star metrics, and ski-rental. At a technical level, our analysis reduces competitive guarantees to key probabilistic inequalities for the vector martingales generated by the posterior of the offline optimum. The resulting framework gives a reusable route from Bayesian online design under arbitrary correlated priors to information-theoretic worst-case competitive guarantees.

</details>


### [296] [QWRF-Net: A Quantum-Wavelet Framework with Rectified Flow for Short-Term Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01626)
*Zhuo Wang,Chaorong Li,Wenjie Luo,Chuanhu Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QWRF-Net提出了一种结合量子-小波框架与整流流（rectified flow）的短临降水临近预报方法，通过将潜特征显式分解为小波子带并进行差异化量子调制，再由基于整流流的非自回归解码器生成未来序列，在KNMI雷达和SEVIR基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能，尤其在极端事件子集和中高降水阈值上表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向预警的短临降水临近预报面临两个核心挑战：一是雷达降水场包含强耦合的多尺度结构，难以保持；二是预报质量在后期的预报时效（lead time）显著退化，难以在整个预警相关时间内保持强降水核心及其空间组织。现有方法未能同时解决多尺度表征和稳定多步生成这两个问题。

Method: QWRF-Net由三个关键组件构成：(1) 将潜特征显式分解为小波子带（wavelet sub-bands），实现多尺度解纠缠；(2) 在分解后的潜空间中对不同子带执行差异化的量子启发式调制（differentiated quantum-inspired modulation），增强条件表征；(3) 采用基于整流流（rectified flow）的非自回归解码器生成未来序列，避免误差累积并提升多步生成稳定性。

Result: 在KNMI雷达和SEVIR基准上采用统一评估协议，QWRF-Net在整体性能上表现良好，在中高降水阈值、极端事件子集以及强降水核心和精细尺度结构的保持方面均获得一致的性能提升。消融实验表明小波尺度解纠缠、差异化子带调制和流式生成三者提供互补收益。

Conclusion: 联合增强多尺度降水表征与稳定多步生成是面向预警的短临降水临近预报的一个有前景的方向。QWRF-Net所取得的改进有望为下游水文和预警应用提供更有用的降水基础。

Abstract: Short-term precipitation nowcasting is important for hydrometeorological early warning, especially when intense convective rainfall may trigger urban flooding, flash floods, and other high-impact hazards. A key challenge in warning-oriented nowcasting is that radar precipitation fields contain strongly coupled multi-scale structures, while forecast quality often degrades at later lead times, making it difficult to preserve intense precipitation cores and their spatial organization over the full warning-relevant horizon. To address this problem, we propose QWRF-Net, a quantum-wavelet framework with rectified flow for short-term precipitation nowcasting. The core idea is to improve the conditional representation of precipitation by explicitly decomposing latent features into wavelet sub-bands and then performing differentiated quantum-inspired modulation in the decomposed latent space, before generating future sequences through a rectified-flow-based non-autoregressive decoder. Experiments on the KNMI radar and SEVIR benchmarks under a unified evaluation protocol show that QWRF-Net achieves favorable overall performance, with relatively consistent gains at medium-to-high precipitation thresholds, on an extreme-event subset, and in preserving intense precipitation cores and fine-scale structures. Ablation results further indicate that wavelet-based scale disentanglement, differentiated sub-band modulation, and flow-based generation provide complementary benefits within the proposed framework. Overall, these results suggest that jointly enhancing multi-scale precipitation representation and stable multi-step generation is a promising direction for warning-oriented short-term precipitation nowcasting. The observed improvements may also provide a more useful precipitation basis for downstream hydrological and warning-related applications.

</details>


### [297] [GraphIR: Architecture-Level Search States for LLM-Guided Neural Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01633)
*Zhen Liu,Wanqi Zhou,Shuanghao Bai,Yuhan Liu,Jinjun Wang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphIR，一种面向架构感知的中间表示，为LLM引导的神经架构搜索提供突变对齐的候选状态，通过计算骨架、突变表面和有效性包络三个互补视图解决可执行程序与架构状态之间的表示不匹配问题，并在120题基准和六个下游基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以直接在可执行的神经网络程序上进行神经架构搜索（NAS），但代码级的灵活性并未提供有效突变所需的架构状态信息——LLM必须从实现细节中推断张量依赖、可编辑组件和兼容性约束。这种表示不匹配导致架构搜索效率低下。

Method: GraphIR是一种架构感知的中间表示，通过三个互补视图组织每个候选架构：(1) 计算骨架——描述张量流；(2) 突变表面——暴露可编辑模块和操作；(3) 有效性包络——捕获接口契约、传播形状和下游依赖。并构建了NAS-Dependency，一个包含120个问题、覆盖六个依赖推理维度的基准进行诊断评估。

Result: GraphIR在识别精确生产者出现、追踪依赖传播、诊断接口和失败风险方面尤为有效。在包括CLRS在内的六个下游基准上，GraphIR取得了最佳的整体搜索性能，同时保持相当的模型大小和良好的端到端NAS效率（集成到OpenEvolve中）。

Conclusion: 突变导向的架构状态（GraphIR）为可执行神经程序与LLM引导的架构演化之间提供了有效接口，验证了面向突变的架构表示在LLM-NAS中的价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable neural architecture search (NAS) directly over executable neural network programs. However, code-level flexibility does not provide the architecture state needed for effective mutation: LLMs must infer tensor dependencies, editable components, and compatibility constraints from implementation details. To address this representation mismatch, we propose GraphIR, an architecture-aware intermediate representation that supplements executable programs with a mutation-aligned candidate state. GraphIR organizes each candidate through three complementary views: a computation skeleton describing tensor flow, a mutation surface exposing editable modules and operations, and a validity envelope capturing interface contracts, propagated shapes, and downstream dependencies. To evaluate our method, we construct NAS-Dependency, a 120-question benchmark covering six complementary dependency-reasoning dimensions. The diagnostic shows that GraphIR is particularly effective at identifying exact producer occurrences, tracing dependency propagation, and diagnosing interface and failure risks. Across six downstream benchmarks including CLRS, GraphIR achieves the best overall search performance while maintaining comparable model size and favorable end-to-end NAS efficiency when integrated into OpenEvolve. These results show that a mutation-oriented architecture state provides an effective interface between executable neural programs and LLM-guided architecture evolution.

</details>


### [298] [Evaluating Forecasting Techniques for Hardware Errors on a Large-scale HPC System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01648)
*Kaiyuan Liao,Xiwei Xuan,Tanwi Mallick,Kevin Brown,Christopher D. Carothers,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用Theta超级计算机七年的生产日志，评估了经典统计模型和深度学习模型在HPC硬件错误时间序列预测上的有效性。结果表明，预测效果高度依赖于错误序列的时间结构：规律且结构稳定的错误可被LSTM和Transformer等模型准确预测，而稀疏和突发性错误则难以预测。文章不提出部署就绪的故障预测框架，而是为何时预测有效提供经验指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC系统中的硬件错误日志能提供异常行为的早期信号，但使用现代预测方法有效预测这些错误仍存在挑战。现有方法对不同类型硬件错误的时间动态适应性不明确，缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 使用Theta超级计算机七年生产日志，对比经典统计模型与深度学习模型（包括LSTM和Transformer架构）在HPC硬件错误时间序列上的预测性能，并引入时间特征进行分析。

Result: 预测有效性强烈依赖错误序列的时间结构：规律性和结构稳定的错误可被精确建模，LSTM和Transformer结合时间特征表现最佳；稀疏和突发主导的错误难以预测。

Conclusion: 该研究不提供部署就绪的故障预测框架，而是提供经验性指导，阐明何时预测有效，并指出改进HPC硬件错误分析预测精度的潜在方向。

Abstract: Hardware error logs in high-performance computing (HPC) systems provide early signals of abnormal behavior, yet there remain challenges in effectively forecasting these errors using modern predictive methods. This work investigates the boundaries of applying time series forecasting to HPC hardware error dynamics. We use seven years of production logs from the Theta supercomputer to evaluate the predictive efficacy of classical statistical and deep learning models. Our results show that forecasting effectiveness depends strongly on the temporal structure of the error series: regularly occurring and structurally stable errors can be modeled accurately, particularly by LSTM and Transformer architectures with temporal features, while sparse and burst-dominated errors remain difficult to predict. Rather than proposing a deployment-ready failure prediction framework, this study provides empirical guidance on when forecasting is effective and highlights potential directions for improving forecasting accuracy in HPC hardware error analysis.

</details>


### [299] [Sharp Root Anti-Concentration via Projective Incidence and Ordered Root Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01670)
*Zijun Wang,Yuchen Miao,Yifan Hu,Huanmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了一维局部根抗集中问题（由Balcan、Pegden和Sharma提出），给出了在线优化分段Lipschitz函数的最坏情形区间命中常数的精确刻画。齐次特征曲线与有界密度系数下，该常数等于A乘以截面平均投影关联速度；对立方体支撑系数，该速度与投影Lipschitz常数仅差通用常数，移除了之前的√N损失。对d次首一多项式，区间命中常数有限当且仅当有序实根分布具有有界密度，且因子d的对比是紧的。条件与联合系数空间面积公式及双图证书使该准则对相依与奇异系数定律可验证。两个图学习应用完成了向遗憾界转变的链条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线优化分段Lipschitz函数时，需要精确理解局部根抗集中性质（即区间命中常数），因为该常数直接决定在线算法的遗憾界。此前的结果存在√N的次优损失，且对相关特征设置（如多项式核）缺少普适的刻画。

Method: 引入"截面平均投影关联速度"（section-averaged projective incidence speed）概念，将区间命中常数归结为该速度与密度界A的乘积。使用投影Lipschitz常数建立与立方体支撑系数情形的等价性。对多项式情形，构造有序实根分布密度作为有限性判据，利用条件/联合系数空间面积公式和双图证书处理奇异与相依系数定律，并通过刚体平移、RBF调和分类器等构建图学习应用。

Result: (1) 对齐次特征曲线，区间命中常数 = A × 截面平均投影关联速度，并证明该速度与投影Lipschitz常数在通用常数意义上等价，获得锐利、无维数依赖的刻画并移除√N损失。(2) 对d次首一多项式，区间命中常数有限 ⟺ 有序实根分布具有有界密度，因子d的对比是紧的。(3) 图学习应用：高斯RBF调和分类器达到期望遗憾Õ((An²De^{BD}/ℓ+1)√T)；公共偏移多项式核模型即使系数定律奇异也达到Õ((qn²κ+1)√T)遗憾。

Conclusion: 本文给出了分段Lipschitz在线优化中根抗集中性质的完整刻画，消除了维数损失，建立了多项式情形下区间命中常数有限性的充要条件，并将理论延伸到奇异系数定律下的图学习场景，完成了从根抗集中分析到遗憾界的完整链条。

Abstract: This paper answers the one-dimensional local root anti-concentration questions posed by Balcan, Pegden, and Sharma in the context of online optimization of piecewise-Lipschitz functions. For a homogeneous feature curve and coefficients whose density relative to the uniform law on a symmetric convex body $K$ is bounded by $A$, we show that the worst-case interval-hitting constant equals $A$ times a section-averaged projective incidence speed. For cube-supported coefficients, this speed is equivalent, up to universal constants, to the projective Lipschitz constant. This yields a sharp, dimension-free characterization and removes the previous $\sqrt N$ loss. For monic degree-$d$ polynomials under arbitrary coefficient laws, we prove that the interval-hitting constant is finite if and only if the ordered real-root laws have bounded densities, with a factor-$d$ comparison that is sharp. Conditional and joint coefficient-space area formulas, together with a two-chart certificate, make this criterion verifiable for dependent and singular coefficient laws. We also give two graph-learning applications that complete the transition-to-regret chain. A cost-sensitive Gaussian-RBF harmonic classifier uses the projective incidence theorem and achieves expected regret $\widetilde O((An^2D e^{BD}/\ell+1)\sqrt T)$. A common-offset polynomial-kernel model uses rigid translation of the ordered roots and achieves $\widetilde O((qn^2κ+1)\sqrt T)$ regret, even when the induced coefficient law is singular in the ambient coefficient space.

</details>


### [300] [Progressive Agent Skill Generation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01678)
*Junhao Shen,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Skill-α，一种基于强化学习的技能生成方法，将技能构建分解为可独立评估的序列化编辑过程，并引入回滚奖励机制，通过在锚定查询上比较原始与编辑后技能的下游执行效果来评估每次编辑。在CL-Bench和tau2-bench上分别比最强基线提升3.3和6.7个点的下游成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能生成方法主要依赖启发式规则或流水线式整合，需要针对不同证据来源专门设计。虽然学习方法能更统一地建模异构来源的技能生成，但技能缺乏基于相关性或正确性的自然监督信号，其价值只能通过是否改善智能体在下游任务上的行为来判断，这使得基于学习的技能生成充满挑战。

Method: 1) 将技能生成建模为序列化编辑过程，把技能构建分解为可独立评估的编辑步骤；2) 提出新颖的回滚奖励（rollback reward），通过在锚定查询下比较原始技能与编辑后技能的下游执行效果来评估每次编辑的贡献；3) 采用强化学习框架渐进式地生成高质量技能。

Result: 在文档到技能（document-to-skill）和经验到技能（experience-to-skill）两种设置下，Skill-α生成的技能均优于基于启发式或流水线的方法。在主GPT-4o worker下，相比最强技能生成基线，Skill-α在CL-Bench上提升平均下游成功率3.3个点，在tau2-bench上提升6.7个点。消融实验验证了回滚奖励和渐进式生成的重要性。

Conclusion: Skill-α通过强化学习与回滚奖励机制，有效解决了技能生成缺乏自然监督信号的问题，在多种设置下显著优于现有方法，并验证了所提各组件的关键作用。

Abstract: Existing skill generation methods largely rely on heuristics or pipeline-style consolidation, which must be specially designed for different evidence sources. In contrast, learning-based approaches offer a more unified way to model skill generation across heterogeneous sources. However, learning-based skill generation remains challenging because skills lack a natural supervision signal based on relevance or correctness; their value can largely be determined only by whether they improve the behavior of the agent on downstream tasks. To address this challenge, we propose Skill-$α$, a reinforcement learning method for progressively generating high-quality agent skills. Specifically, we formulate skill generation as a sequential editing process that decomposes skill construction into individually evaluable edits, and introduce a novel rollback reward that evaluates each edit by comparing downstream execution under the original and edited skills on an anchored query. Extensive experiments show that Skill-$α$ generates more effective skills than methods based on heuristics or pipelines in both document-to-skill and experience-to-skill settings. Under the main GPT-4o worker, Skill-$α$ improves average downstream success rates over the strongest skill-generation baseline by 3.3 points on CL-Bench and 6.7 points on tau2-bench. Further ablations validate the importance of rollback reward and progressive generation.

</details>


### [301] [Beckmann Transport Models: From Autonomous Flows to One-Step Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01692)
*Lee Cheuk-Kit,Florentin Coeurdoux,Peter Potaptchik,Yilun Du,Michael Samuel Albergo,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自治流（时间无关速度场）的流匹配实例，可精确地在两个分布之间建立映射（当目标分布是奇异/低维流形支撑时），并证明一步生成映射是某个守恒方程的唯一解，可直接从样本学习。该框架统一了Poisson流生成模型和平衡匹配等方法，并在ImageNet 256x256上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配方法通常依赖时间相关的速度场（非自治流），在目标分布位于低维数据流形（奇异分布）时存在不一致性或效率问题。作者希望构建一个时间无关的速度场（自治流），从而在理论层面更简洁地刻画两个分布之间的映射，并纠正现有方法中的不一致。

Method: (1) 提出自治流（autonomous flow），即时间无关的速度场，仅在目标分布为奇异（低维流形支撑）时能精确映射两个分布；(2) 证明一步生成映射是某个简单守恒方程的唯一解，该方程可端到端地从样本学习；(3) 将自治流和映射与Beckmann最优输运问题中的通量约束建立动力学联系；(4) 统一了Poisson流生成模型和带有二次流匹配回归损失的平衡匹配方法，并修正了其不一致之处。

Result: 该方法提供了一个统一的理论框架，能够恢复已有的若干方法（如Poisson流生成模型、平衡匹配），纠正了现有方法中的理论不一致性，并在ImageNet 256x256上展示了自治流和一步映射的有效性。

Conclusion: 自治流为奇异目标分布下的分布映射提供了一种简洁而统一的动力学解释，为生成建模中的一步生成提供了一个理论与实践上均有优势的新范式。

Abstract: We propose an instantiation of flow matching that relies on a time-independent velocity field (an \emph{autonomous flow}) to exactly map between two distributions, so long as the target is singular, i.e.\ supported on a lower-dimensional data manifold. We also show that the one-step generative map associated with this flow is the unique solution of a simple conservation equation, which can be used to learn the map directly from samples. These autonomous flows and maps give a dynamical meaning to the flux constraint of Beckmann's transportation problem. Their construction provides a unifying framework that recovers, for instance, the closed-form Poisson-flow generative model and equilibrium matching with a quadratic flow-matching regression loss. We illustrate how this theory corrects inconsistencies in existing methods and demonstrate the effectiveness of the autonomous flow and the one-step map on ImageNet 256x256.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond On-Policy Exploration: Integrating External Policy Rollouts for Reinforcement Learning in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01717)
*Wonseok Lee,Jimyeong Kim,Jungmin Ko,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERILS框架，通过引入外部更强策略生成的高奖励轨迹，并采用长度控制与源特定奖励处理两项技术，解决了扩散大语言模型（dLLM）在强化学习中因on-policy正奖励稀缺而进步缓慢的问题。实验表明，ERILS在Sudoku、Countdown和MATH500三个任务上均显著提升多采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对dLLM的强化学习方法主要依赖目标模型自身的on-policy rollout，但当成功轨迹稀缺时，正奖励信号稀少，训练进展有限。因此需要引入外部更强策略生成的高奖励轨迹来补充训练信号，但直接整合外部轨迹会面临轨迹长度不一致和on-policy与外部奖励联合处理的稳定性问题。

Method: 提出ERILS（外部轨迹整合的长度控制与源特定处理）框架，包含两个核心技术：(1) 长度控制——对外部生成的rollout长度进行控制，使其与on-policy rollout长度匹配；(2) 源特定奖励处理——将on-policy和外部rollout的奖励分开处理，避免联合处理带来的训练不稳定。

Result: 在Sudoku、Countdown和MATH500的零样本评估下，ERILS在所有三个任务上均提升多采样性能。在Sudoku任务上，best-of-4完成准确率达98.4%，远超最强基线的40.3%；同时在Sudoku上，不同生成长度（128/256/512 token）下保持约90%的确定性单采样完成准确率。消融实验表明长度控制的外部rollout优于无控制版本，源特定奖励处理避免了联合奖励处理导致的训练崩溃。

Conclusion: 轨迹构建与奖励处理是将外部rollout整合进dLLM强化学习时的重要设计维度，ERILS证明了通过合理的长度控制和源特定奖励处理，可以有效利用外部更强策略的轨迹来提升dLLM的强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning methods for diffusion large language models (dLLMs) commonly rely on on-policy rollouts generated by the target dLLM itself. When successful on-policy rollouts are scarce, however, on-policy training may receive little positive reward and make only limited progress. To mitigate this problem, we explore incorporating higher-reward rollouts generated by a stronger external policy alongside on-policy rollouts from the target dLLM. However, directly incorporating these external rollouts introduces two practical challenges: differences in rollout length and instability when jointly processing rewards from on-policy and external rollouts. To address these challenges, we propose External Rollout Integration with Length Control and Source-Specific Processing (ERILS), which controls external-rollout length and processes the rewards of on-policy and external rollouts separately. Experiments on Sudoku, Countdown, and MATH500 under zero-shot evaluation show that ERILS improves multi-sample performance across all three tasks, with the largest gains on Sudoku. On Sudoku, ERILS achieves 98.4% best-of-4 completion accuracy, compared with 40.3% for the strongest baseline. ERILS also maintains approximately 90% deterministic single-completion accuracy on Sudoku across generation lengths of 128, 256, and 512 tokens. Our component analysis further shows that length-controlled external rollouts are more effective than uncontrolled external rollouts, and that source-specific reward processing avoids the training collapse observed with joint reward processing. These results show that rollout construction and reward processing are important design dimensions when integrating external rollouts into dLLM reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [303] [LLM-Guided Retrieval for Prediction of Molecular Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01734)
*Betty Xiong,Jan-Christian Huetter,Gabriele Scalia,Tommaso Biancalani,Sepideh Maleki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM引导检索（LGR）方法，利用大语言模型对候选邻域药物进行排序，并通过固定均值聚合器组合观察到的表达变化来预测未见药物的转录组响应，在Tahoe-100M单细胞扰动图谱上一致优于多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-细胞组合的全面实验筛选不可行，需要有效的零样本预测方法来近似未测量药物在细胞系中的转录组响应。现有方法主要依赖预测器复杂性，而忽略了检索质量本身对预测性能的关键作用。

Method: 将分子扰动预测建模为检索-聚合任务：首先利用大语言模型（LLM）对候选邻域药物进行排序（限定在目标细胞系已测药物范围内），然后使用固定的均值聚合器将所选邻域药物的已知表达变化进行平均，形成对未测量药物响应的预测。

Result: 在Tahoe-100M单细胞扰动图谱的未见药物、未见细胞系和开放世界三种场景下，LGR一致优于药物均值、ChemCPA和基于化学结构的kNN基线。在未见细胞系泛化场景中增益最大，同时在基因调控的方向（符号）准确性方面也表现更好。

Conclusion: 检索质量而非预测器复杂度是零样本分子扰动预测的关键驱动因素；LLM在受约束的检索模块中能够提供有效的生物学先验知识。

Abstract: Predicting transcriptomic responses to small-molecule perturbations across cell lines is central to drug discovery, but exhaustive profiling of drug-cell combinations is infeasible. We frame molecular perturbation prediction as retrieve-and-aggregate: approximate an unmeasured drug's response in a cell line by aggregating measured responses of a small set of biologically related compounds. We propose LLM-Guided Retrieval (LGR), where a large language model (LLM) ranks candidate neighbor drugs (restricted to those profiled in the target cell line); after which a fixed mean aggregator combines their observed expression deltas to form the prediction. We evaluate on the Tahoe-100M single-cell perturbation atlas under unseen-drug, unseen-cell-line, and open-world regimes. LGR consistently improves over drug mean, ChemCPA, and chemistry-based kNN baselines, with the strongest gains for unseen cell-line generalization, where it achieves higher correlation and lower error than mean baselines. Across settings, LGR improves directional (sign) accuracy of gene regulation, indicating better recovery of biologically meaningful perturbation effects even when magnitude-based metrics are similar. These results suggest that retrieval quality, rather than predictor complexity, is a key driver of zero-shot molecular perturbation prediction, and that LLMs can provide a useful biological prior when used as constrained retrieval modules.

</details>


### [304] [Disagree to Accelerate: Closing the Loop on Diffusion Feature Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01740)
*Yanchao Li,Jiaqing Xie,Ben Gao,Wanhao Liu,Yanbo Wang,T. Y. Tsui,Jinfei Liu,Yuqiang Li,Tianfan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RACER，一种训练无关的闭环控制器，利用缓存中两个预测之间的分歧作为可靠性信号，在跳过去噪步骤时动态收缩不确定的预测，并在最危险步骤主动刷新特征，从而以相同的去噪评估次数显著提升扩散模型加速采样的图像/视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的特征预测加速方法仅专注于设计更强的预测器，但在跳过步骤中对预测结果无条件信任（开环缓存）。当加速趋于激进时，预测误差在步骤间剧烈变化，固定信任机制会失效。核心缺失问题不仅是"如何预测更好"，还包括"何时以及多大程度信任一个预测"。

Method: RACER利用缓存内部两个预测之间的一致性作为可靠性信号（两者一致处特征轨迹平滑，分歧处预测变难），该信号无需额外去噪评估、运行开销低。具体响应机制为：1）持续将不确定的预测向最后计算的特征收缩；2）在最危险的步骤刷新特征，并通过跳过后续预定步骤来补偿增加的评估成本。同时推导了收缩操作的确定性误差界。

Result: 在相同去噪评估次数下，RACER在SD3.5-Large、FLUX.1-dev、Wan2.1-14B和HunyuanVideo上，相较最强开环基线在DrawBench、VBench和COCO上均取得改进。在SD3.5上以相同质量实现更快采样。RACER对不同预测设计具有泛化能力，例如在Taylor基线上恢复了大部分质量损失。

Conclusion: 扩散加速的可靠性不仅取决于预测器的设计，还取决于预测结果的使用方式。通过从缓存自身提取信任信号并动态调控预测，可以在无额外训练成本下实现更稳健、更高质量的加速采样。

Abstract: Training-free feature forecasting accelerates diffusion sampling by predicting features at skipped denoising steps. Recent work has mainly focused on designing stronger forecasters. Yet forecast error varies sharply across steps, and open-loop caches trust the forecast in full at every skipped step. This fixed trust is what breaks as acceleration turns aggressive. The missing question is not only how to forecast better, but when and how much to trust a forecast. We show that reliability can be observed from the cache itself. Two forecasts agree where the feature trajectory is smooth, and they diverge where prediction turns hard. Their disagreement is a cheap runtime signal, and it costs no extra denoiser evaluation. Based on this signal, we introduce RACER, a training-free closed-loop controller with two responses. It continuously shrinks uncertain forecasts toward the last computed feature. At the riskiest steps, RACER refreshes the feature and repays the added evaluation by skipping a later scheduled one. We derive a deterministic error bound for the shrinkage and empirically evaluate its validity and tightness across acceleration regimes. At the same number of denoiser evaluations, RACER improves the strongest open-loop baseline across SD3.5-Large, FLUX.1-dev, Wan2.1-14B, and HunyuanVideo on DrawBench, VBench, and COCO. On SD3.5, we further show that RACER samples faster at equal quality. RACER generalizes across forecasting designs as well. For example, it recovers much of the quality lost on a Taylor base. These results show that reliable diffusion acceleration also depends on how forecasts are used. Code is available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/RACER

</details>


### [305] [Toward Plasticity-Preserving KL Regularization for Capability Retention in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01743)
*Li Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Xiaohan Wang,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Tianhao Peng,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoKL（Correctness-Conditioned KL Regularization），一种基于正确性条件的KL正则化框架，用于LLM强化学习后训练。与传统全策略KL正则化约束整个响应分布不同，CoKL仅约束正确性条件化的响应分布，从而在保留已有能力的同时减少对新任务学习的干扰，并在多个实验中取得了更优的目标任务提升与先验能力保持之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练在优化新目标时可能导致基础模型已有能力退化。标准全策略KL正则化约束整个响应分布，可能过度限制探索和新任务学习。作者希望设计一种更精确的约束方式，既能保留已有能力，又能最小化对学习新任务的干扰。

Method: CoKL将保留约束从完整输出分布收窄为正确性条件化的响应分布。采用前向KL散度实现CoKL，并推导出适用于RL的有限分组训练目标。在总体层面，CoKL将正确响应的总概率与其条件分布解耦，仅正则化参考模型支持的正确答案之间的相对概率分配，而不直接锚定错误输出或正确性总质量。

Result: 在受控多解环境以及多个模型规模的持续后训练实验中，CoKL相比现有正则化方法（如全策略前向/反向KL正则化）在目标任务提升与先验能力保留之间取得了更优的平衡。理论分析还表明，当参考策略不完美时，全策略前向和反向KL正则化会产生严格的最优正确性差距，而CoKL避免了这一局限。

Conclusion: CoKL通过正确性条件化的正则化设计，实现了对能力保持与新任务学习的更精确权衡，为LLM RL后训练中的遗忘缓解提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central paradigm for large language model (LLM) post-training, but optimization toward new objectives can degrade capabilities already present in the base model. KL regularization is widely used to mitigate such forgetting by constraining policy drift toward a reference model. However, standard full-policy KL regularization constrains the entire response distribution and may unnecessarily restrict exploration and target-task learning. This raises a natural question: can a more precise constraint preserve existing capabilities while minimizing interference with learning new tasks? To this end, we propose \underline{Co}rrectness-Conditioned \underline{KL} Regularization (CoKL), a conditional regularization framework that narrows the preservation constraint from the full output distribution to correctness-conditioned response distributions. We instantiate CoKL with forward KL divergence and derive a practical finite-group training objective for RL-based LLM post-training. At the population level, CoKL decouples the total probability assigned to correct responses from their correctness-conditioned distribution, thereby regularizing the relative probability allocation among reference-supported correct responses without directly anchoring incorrect outputs or total correctness mass. We further show that full-policy forward and reverse KL regularization induce a strict optimal correctness gap when the reference policy is imperfect, whereas CoKL avoids this limitation. Experiments in controlled multi-solution environments and continual post-training settings across multiple model scales demonstrate that CoKL achieves a more favorable balance between target-task improvement and prior-capability retention than existing regularization methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/CoKL.

</details>


### [306] [Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Radio Resource Management under Coupled Finite-Horizon Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01745)
*Yeonseo Jeong,Wonhyeok Ko,Sungweon Hong,Songnam Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HeLyMARL，一种Lyapunov嵌入的异构多智能体强化学习框架，通过虚拟队列的漂移加惩罚分解将有限时域能量和切换预算约束转化为每时隙奖励，解决密集无线网络中用户关联、调度、基站激活与切换控制的联合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集无线网络中，最大化比例公平下的吞吐量需要联合管理用户关联、调度、基站激活和切换控制，同时需满足有限时域的能量与切换预算约束。这一约束带来了基站侧能量管理与用户侧切换调节之间的根本性矛盾。虽然MARL是分布式序列控制的自然框架，但面临两大难点：有限时域预算约束无法在每个时隙直接评估，以及非线性比例公平效用缺乏原则性的每时隙分解方法。

Method: 提出HeLyMARL框架，采用漂移加惩罚分解与虚拟队列机制。通过构造虚拟队列将能量和切换约束压力内化到统一的每时隙奖励中，将受约束的有限时域问题转化为无约束的MARL问题。该方法实现了异构多智能体的分布式控制。

Result: 与两种基于拉格朗日的方法对比揭示了时间尺度分离现象：拉格朗日松弛仅在训练回合间调节约束，而HeLyMARL的虚拟队列能在回合内的每个部分时域上约束累计预算消耗。仿真表明HeLyMARL是唯一能够在整个时域内维持吞吐量-公平性平衡并保证服务不间断的方法，优于传统MARL、Lyapunov方法及约束MARL基准。

Conclusion: HeLyMARL通过虚拟队列提供了超越贪婪Lyapunov控制的"节奏保证"（pacing guarantee），即在部分时域上都能约束预算消耗，从而避免了预算过早耗尽的缺陷，实现了吞吐量、公平性与服务连续性的三者兼顾。

Abstract: Maximizing throughput under proportional fairness in dense wireless networks requires jointly managing user association, scheduling, base station (BS) activation, and handover control under hard finite-horizon energy and handover budgets, which induces a fundamental tension between BS-side energy management and user-side handover regulation.
